File:Icon tab unity Unity
Unity is the 3rd major prestige layer in the game. It is reached when you have 1.08e2466 EP. When you Unite, you will be able to pick one of 4 Zodiacs from each element type: Fire, Wind, Water, or Earth

Astrology

The Astrology tab
The Astrology system is the first of four core progression mechanics in Unity, alongside Trials, Attacks and Relics. It revolves around acquiring and assigning Zodiacs to Planets, granting a wide variety of bonuses.

Zodiacs
Main page: Zodiacs

Zodiacs are collectible entities in the Astrology system that provide bonuses when assigned to Planets. Each Zodiac belongs to one of four Elements — Fire, Wind, Water, or Earth — and corresponds to one of the four seasons.

There are 12 Zodiac signs in total. Each can have up to four possible bonuses unique to its sign and element group. Some bonuses are shared across signs of the same element.

The strength of each Zodiac's bonuses scales with its Quality, Level, and Rarity.

Planet Shop
Main page: Planet Shop
The planet shop is unlocked after the completion of Easy Trial 6. The Planet shop is a new place in the unity main page. The planet shop uses Astrodust to buy donuts, or use Zodiac modifiers. the 4 donuts you get to buy are Game speed, Luck, Quality, and Zodiac Exp. Factor. The 4 zodiac modifiers you get access to are Merging, Enhancing , Redistribution and Sacrificing.

Planets
Main page: Planets
Zodiacs must be assigned to Planets to activate their effects. This means that having a zodiac in the inventory does not automatically activate it’s effects.

A Zodiac placed on a Planet contributes:

All its unlocked bonuses
A Planet bonus, determined by the Zodiac’s Season
The combined effects of all Zodiacs placed on Planets are displayed under Zodiac bonuses in the Astrology tab.

Each Planet can only hold one Zodiac at a time. The cumulative effects of all Planet placements are listed in the Extra bonuses panel in the Astrology tab.

Trials
Main page: Trials
Trials are the Unity version of eternity challenges. Each completed trial will give you a unique permanent reward and +2 default EC completions. when you complete all 6 trials of a certain difficulty, you will get another reward. There are 25 trials (as of V1.068) with 6 Easy Trials, 6 Medium trials, 6 Hard Trials, 6 Insane Trials and 1 Bonus Trial. When you want to attempt a trial, you should hit the lock and then reset your unity, forgetting to lock may lead to you accidentally breaking a trials condition. Remember to unlock the trial after you complete it!


The Trial tab


Relics
Main page: Relics
Relics are powerful artifacts that provide various bonuses across different game stages. Relics are purchased using Gold earned from Attack runs. As of update V1.068, there are a total of 70 unique relics, each unlocking at specific Attack levels.


The Relics tab
Attacks

The Attack tab
Main page: Attacks
Attacks are unlocked after completing 15 trials. A new tab is added with a new revolution type, Attack Circles. For every Attack Level, there is an enemy, depicted as a HP bar in the middle of the screen. Your goal is to deal enough damage to deplete the enemy's HP bar and advance to the next Attack Level.

There are a total 5 Attack Circles and they work similarly to the base game's circles. Every revolution/lap increases the Attack Circle's Attack Damage Multiplier on the top of the screen. In addition, each revolution/lap counts as a hit to the enemy, dealing damage to its HP bar.

When you do a proper Unity by selecting a Zodiac, the Attack Level gets reset to 0 and you will be rewarded with the amount of Gold that is shown at the bottom of the screen. Your Circle Attack Multipliers will NOT be reset upon Uniting and performing a Soft Unity Reset (by e.g. swapping Zodiacs) will not reset Attack Level.

Minerals

The Mineral tab
Main page: Minerals
Minerals are unlocked after completing Achievement 240 (reach attacks level 1337), which adds a new subtab to the Unity tab. There are total of 100 minerals in the game, and each mineral generates a resource called Value Points (VP). These can be used to buy VP upgrades.

The base formula for VP gain is: 
0
.
0
1
⋅
3
(
M
i

l
−
1
)

Upon reaching Mineral level 30, you can perform Polish Prestige. Doing this, you will gain Polish Points (PP) based on what level your highest mineral was (gains a boost every 5 levels, scales faster after lvl 50 Mineral). You can spend PP on 5 Weapons: Sword, Axe, Spear, Bow and Knuckles. Sword multiplies VP gain, Axe powers Mineral cost fall, Spear multiplies gold gain, Bow multiplies Attacks Ascension Power, and Knuckles boosts Minerals Local Speed exponent.

Upon reaching Mineral level 70, you can perform Refinement Prestige. Doing this, you will gain Refinement Points (RfP) based on what level your highest Mineral was (gains a boost every 10 levels). RfP can be used to buy nodes in the Refine Tree. There are 136 nodes, and some are upgradeable to certain levels, while most are single-time buy.

Magnets

Magnets are unlocked through Refine Tree Node 18, giving a base 1% chance to obtain a magnet upon merging common minerals. Magnets also unlock Magnet Upgrades, giving buffs to minerals.

Runes
Runes are unlocked with Refine Tree Node 35 There are 2 types of runes: Sun and Moon runes. Both of these are generated over time, albeit very slowly. Their generation speed can be boosted with RfP, and each rune can be spent on 3 decent upgrades.

Special Minerals

Special Minerals are unlocked through Refine Tree Node 41, unlocking a new grid of minerals. 1 special mineral can be spawned at a time at the cost of Sun and Moon Runes. There are 10 special minerals, Red boosts VP gain, Blue decreases cost increase, Green increases Cost Fall, Pink boost Luck & Quality, Yellow boost Gold Gain, Orange boosts Magnet Chance, Dark Purple boosts PP Gain, Light Blue boosts RfP Gain, White boosts Game Speed, and Black grants tarot draws when destroyed. Special Mineral Progression can also be unlocked through Refine Tree Node 46 where you can spend RfP to boost the effect of the other Gems.

Special Minerals Progression (SMP)
SMP is unlocked with Refine Tree Node 46. Here, you can gain SMP EXP through merging Special Minerals, with each Special Mineral having its own SMP EXP counter. Once you have enough, your spawn level for that special mineral increases. Additionally, the efficiency of these minerals’ effects can be upgraded with RfP.

Special Mineral Sacrifice
Special Mineral Sacrifice is unlocked via Refine Tree Node 85. After unlocking, you can sacrifice a special mineral by destroying 100 level 20 minerals of that type. Once sacrificed, you can no longer spawn that mineral, and you will instead be granted a level 27 version within the Sacrifice tab (equal to 128 level 20 minerals, more than a max grid). Once all special minerals have been sacrificed, spawning a special mineral will grant sacrifice dust instead, which can be used to level up sacrificed minerals.

Elements

Element Trees
Main page: Elements
Elements are unlocked after completing Achievement 321, which adds a new sub-tab to the Unity tab. Here, you can generate 4 different Elements: Fire, Earth, Wind, and Water. You can only generate one element at a time. Each element’s generation is boosted by specific Factors, and your Global Element Multiplier. Each of these factors usually comes from the effects of Zodiacs of that element, like how Earth generation is boosted by your Infinity count. Additionally, each element can be spent on their respective Element Tree for powerful upgrades.

Tarot
Main page: Tarot
Tarot is unlocked after purchasing Refine Tree Node 70, which adds a new subtab to the Unity tab. Unlocked along with Tarot are Black Gems, the 10th Special Mineral type. Destroying these will grant Tarot Draws, which can be used to draw Tarot Cards. These cards come in 5 suits: Swords, Wands, Pentacles, Cups, and a special Arcana suit. For the 4 regular suits, each card boosts a specific stat, and this boost gets better the more copies, or levels, of that card you have. The arcana cards can be used as a sort-of power up, each one providing a boost when activated, and requiring a long cooldown before it can be used again. In total, there are 78 unique cards; 14 for the regular suits, and 22 Arcan cards.

Upgrades
Each of these 4 regular suits has their respective resource, generated by the Ace card and boosted by the other cards in that suit. With 1,000,000 of each, you unlock Tarot Upgrades where you can spend these resources on many powerful upgrades.

Challenges
Unlocked with Refine Tree Node 89, these are very similar to the other challenge systems from before. Each Tarot card has its own respective challenge, and you start with the first card of every suit. More challenges are unlocked with each completion of a new challenge, and each one gives a boost to the respective card’s effect.

Artifacts
Artifacts are unlocked through Refine Tree Node 99. Here, you can convert regular Tarot Resources into their golden version with the flush button. Doing so will instantly grant the golden resources, though a ^0.001 resource generation penalty will be incurred that gradually weakens over time. Each golden resource has its own Artifact, which comes in multiple parts. These parts must be unlocked by having enough of a specific Golden Resource. Each part provides powerful boosts, and can be further leveled up with Golden Resources. (Note: you do not spend any of the resources, you just need to have enough to get the upgrade.)

Plague
Main page Plague
Plague is unlocked after purchasing Refine Tree Node 103, which adds a new sub-tab to the Unity tab. Here, you can infect many populations inside certain locations, or Plague Stage. Successfully completing a Plague Stage will reward Plague Generators, or PlG. These generate Plague Points, or PlP. These resources and other statistics like your highest stage cleared, total infected, etc. boost many aspects of the game.

Endoplasmic Reticulum
Unlocked through Refine Tree Node 104, you can craft Endoplasmic Reticulums (ERs) using your PlP. These are basically different plague strains, and have 3 different stats: Spread Power, Infectivity, and Stealth. Spread Power determines overall speed, Infectivity allows for faster spreading during the middle stage of infection, and stealth decreases the slowdown caused by the remaining healthy population.

Unwanted ERs can be dissolved for Virus Essence, which generates Virus Points, or ViP that can be spent on upgrades that boost your ERs.

Singularity
Main page Singularity
Singularity is unlocked after purchasing Refine Tree Node 121, which adds a new subtab to the Unity tab. Singularity is centered around Atoms, which multiply themselves every second based on your Singularity Multiplier. This multiplier can be increased by performing a Singularity, which resets the following:

Minerals (except the Refine Tree)
Elements
Tarot (except Tarot Upgrades and Challenges)
Plague
Your current Atom Count
After your first singularity, you will need 1.8e308 Atoms to perform a Singularity again.

Milestones
Unlocked with your first Singularity, you can collect Milestone rewards by achieving a certain amount of Singularities or Atoms. Additionally, there are Progression milestones, which inflict penalties until a specific requirement is satisfied, in which the penalty disappears and is replaced with a buff.

Effects
Unlocked with your second Singularity, each of your Singularity Count, Atoms amount, and Singularity Multiplier will provide powerful boosts. More effects can be unlocked the more you progress.

Tree
Unlocked at 5 Singularities, the Tree allows you to purchase upgrades out of an upgrade tree consisting of 13 nodes using Atoms. Unlike many other upgrades, a purchase will divide your atom count instead of subtracting. Each upgrade can be purchased up to level 10, in which it can be Ascended. Doing so will reset the level but not the effect, though the effect scales slower after and the cost increases faster.

Houses
Houses are unlocked at 32 Singularities, and it functions like an advanced version of Astrology. However, each Zodiac can only be placed in its matching House, and they can only be obtained by performing a Singularity with more than 1e1000 Atoms. Each of these zodiacs provide unique buffs to many aspects of your progression.

Trivia
On your first Unity, you will be guaranteed the option of a Lv3 Rare Aries with 2 buffs: common exponent and mult gain

Guide:Early Unity
Welcome to Unity!

When you reach 1.08e2466 EP, you can Unite.

Automation settings after Unity:

Autobuy revolutions: 100% all

Autoascend: all on

Auto prestige: x1000 P.Mult, +0 P.Exp, 0.2s

Auto promote: (2, +25lvl - 1s)->(1, +25lvl - 1s)->(3, +25lvl - 1s)->(4, +25lv - 1s)

Auto infinity: off

Auto IP adjuster: off

Autobuy infinity upgrade tree: on

Autobuy generators: on

Autobuy stars: on

Autoslowdown: 100x lap speed (only before getting Animals Milestone 4 (own 10 Animals) and Eternity Challenges Milestone 2 , after that 10x)


Contents
1	The Optimal Strategies After First Unity
2	Zodiac Stat Information
3	How To Use Zodiacs
4	Zodiac Loadouts
4.1	Fast Unities Build
The Optimal Strategies After First Unity
Unity is achieved at 1.08e2466 EP. You are given 4 Zodiac options. Always choose the lvl 3 Rare Aries Zodiac for On your first Unity, it is guaranteed on your first unity and has raw score multipliers and common exponent to help you progress faster on your first Unity. After your first Unity, Zodiac options become randomized. While other zodiacs have bonuses that look appealing, they are weak compared to the raw score multipliers and the common exponent provided by the Aries Zodiac.

Zodiac Stat Information
Zodiacs have several stats. They are Quality, Rarity and Level.

Zodiac Quality: Zodiac quality boosts zodiacs. It becomes a significant booster to zodiacs later in the game, so always prioritize it by using water zodiacs but only after unlocking 8 planet slots.

Zodiac level: Zodiac level also has a boost to zodiacs but weaker.

Zodiac Rarity: Zodiac Rarity has a weaker boost to zodiacs than Zodiac Level early in Unity progression so ignore it until you reach Legendary rarity where zodiacs have 3 buffs instead of 2.

How To Use Zodiacs
Zodiacs are like “disposable items” that are needed to be constantly swapped with higher quality zodiacs later in the game. Right now, the only planet slot you have is the Sun. After placing the zodiac on the Sun, You unlock Mercury. To use a zodiac, place the zodiac into one of the planet slots (located at the bottom of the screen) to gain the buff of the zodiac. You can use one of the zodiac loadouts below. Earth Zodiacs are the worst zodiacs in Early Unity progression so do NOT choose them.

Zodiac Loadouts
Early zodiac builds

Fast Unities Build
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aries	Libra	Sagit	Empty	Empty	Empty	Empty	Empty	Empty	Empty	Empty	Empty


This build is effective for fast Unities especially with Libra’s DP gain and Supernova requirement and the Sagittarius’ slowdown power.

Alternative builds:

Guide:Trials
Trials are the second Unity tab and are unlocked when you equip 3 zodiacs.

There are 4 Trial difficulties with 6 Trials per difficulty, making a total of 24 Trials. There is a bonus trial unlocked a lot later in the game. Each Trial has its own unique handicap, but all Trials under the same difficulty share the same goal, except for Insane Trials and the Bonus Trial, which have unique goals and unique handicaps.

Each completed Trial grants a Trial-specific reward but also Trials reduces the need to complete Eternity Challenge difficulties, the reduction is 2x per completed Trial (e.g., 5 completed Trials = 10 Eternity Challenge difficulties counted as completed/aka the first and the second Eternity challenge will be fully completed.

To do a trial, make sure you lock it and restart the Unity (there is tiny button on the top) because when you lock a trial, it doesn’t restart the Unity (the devs forgot to make it do that) (thats intentional) so you can meet the needed requirements properly.

It is possible to complete 2 or more trials at the same time but doing it is nearly impossible before later game stages. (either way completing more than one trial at the same time is practically useless)

Right below this message is little QoL(Quality of Life) to skip straight to what you need, just click any of the blue texts and it will take you to the category you desire.

Easy Trials
list of all the Easy Trials and their Goals, Handicaps and Rewards.

Trial	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Easy Trial 1	Reach Infinity	You can only buy the first 6 colors	x1.4 to zodiac quality
Easy Trial 2	You can't buy promotion 3 and 4	Unlocks Trials exp factor
Easy Trial 3	Common exponent is multiplied by 0.6	Common exponent multiplied by 1.05
Easy Trial 4	You can't buy promotion 2 and 4 or prestige	Unlocks Mars
Easy Trial 5	Common exponent can't be >0.7	Unlocks Max Score factor
Easy Trial 6	You can't prestige, promote or ascend	Unlocks Planet Shop
How to Complete the Easy Trials
Only Fire Zodiacs (the red ones) are needed, preferably Sagittarius with Slowdown Power and Aries with Mult Gain.

Focus on Easy Trial 2 first because it’s actually easier than Easy Trial 1, after that complete the Trials in numerical order (1 to 6).

After uniting 5+ times the best animal purchases are animals with Lap speed and Common exponent.

Recommended Season–Planet bonus is Venus with Summer Season, Leo is your only viable option, granting 3x Lap Speed, which is needed for slowdown.

Easy Trial 5: This Trial is a huge stat check due to the exponent cap, stack more Sagittarius Slowdown or Aries Mult Gain as necessary to clear it

Easy Trial 6: Lap Speed bonuses might be needed to trigger AutoSlowdown for the ascensions, best animals for this trial are 1.2, 2.4, 2.5, and 3.4.

After completing all the Easy Trials
The rewards for completing all the Easy Trials are you not losing your score when you Prestige and Promote, and unlocking the Medium Trials.

After completing the Easy Trials you should re-setup your Prestige and Promotion automations like this for maximum efficiency (you should use it like that either way, but previously you needed to change it time to time and now it's permanent):

Min P.Exponent Gain (+)	Min P.Multi Gain (x)	Min time (s)
Prestige	0	1.01	0
Min lvl Gain	Min time (s)
Promotion 1	+1 lvl	0 s
Promotion 2
Promotion 3
Promotion 4
Medium Trials
After completing all easy trials, You should already have all of your owned zodiac slots with zodiacs with Rare/Uncommon rarity. If you haven’t, it is recommended to farm for Rare/Uncommon zodiacs for medium trials before doing medium trials.

Here is the list of all the Medium Trials and their Goals, Handicaps and Rewards.

Trial	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Medium Trial 1	Reach Eternity	You can only buy the first two Infinity Generators	x1.337 to luck
Medium Trial 2	You can't buy Stars	Infinity & Infinity Challenges are now always 1ms
Medium Trial 3	You can't Prestige	Supernova resets nothing, unlocks Autobuy for DU and DTP
Medium Trial 4	You can only Infinite twice	Unlocks Auto Unity
Medium Trial 5	You can only Infinite once	Unlocks DTP factor
Medium Trial 6	You can't prestige/promote and can only buy the first 5 colors	Keep fastest Eternity on Unity reset at 5ms
How to Complete the Medium Trials
These trials require mostly wind Zodiacs (the cyan-colored Zodiacs)

It’s best to turn off Autobuy for animals (when you unlock it) when doing the Trials, because it might buy more expensive animals and you need as many as possible for some Trials to get the animal milestones and complete the Trials more easily.

The hardest moment is the first one, for this one, make sure you can get 7 animals through ^SR (any wind Zodiac) and fire Zodiacs to unlock the lab!

For the Medium Trials up to the Third, one Zodiac with Lab Levels (stat from only Aquarius) should do it.

But, for Medium Trial 4, before your last Infinity, disable AutoGen, Star and AP & enable AutoEternity to avoid losing the IP from accidental purchases.

For Medium Trials 5, you need ~50 lab levels, going for 20 animals. This will unlock animal milestone 5, giving you passive IP and removing the need to infinite to be able to eternate. Thus, the way to do it is:

Buy cheapest animals
Infinity
Buy cheapest animals until you have at least 20
Wait for passive IP to reach 1.8e308, so you can eternate and complete the Trial
For Medium Trials 6, you need ~100 lab levels

The Recommended Season-Planet Bonus are:

Sun: Autumn (Libra, Unity Rewards ^1.1 for IP Gain),
Mercury Outside of MT2: Winter (Aquarius, Star Costs ^0.9),
Venus: Autumn (Libra, Supernova Rewards ^1.02),
Gives a small bonus on Star Base and AP

Mars: Outside MT2 Only: Autumn/Libra (Star Costs ^0.85),
Jupiter: Summer (Supernova Rewards ^1.03)
After Completing all the Medium Trials
The rewards for completing all the Medium Trials are no longer the need of Infiniting anymore, as now you will get IP per second based on how much you would get if you Infinitied, you also get Max IP Factor and unlock the Hard Trials.

Don't forget to turn off Auto Infinity because you don't need it anymore.

Hard Trials
Hard Trials are personally the hardest trials and take the longest to complete once unlocked, its normal if it feels like it takes forever.

Trial	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Hard Trial 1	Reach Unity	You can't buy more than 20 DTP	Unlocks DP factor
Hard Trial 2	You can't buy Lab or RP upgrades	Unlocks Lab level factor
Hard Trial 3	You can't Supernova	Unlocks Autosell zodiacs
Hard Trial 4	You can't buy more than 1500 AP/get past Supernova 10	Unlocks AP factor
Hard Trial 5	You can only buy 50 of each Dilation upgrade & 2 DTP	Divide all Relic costs by 1M
Hard Trial 6	You can't dilate or enter EC	Unlocks EP factor
How to Complete Hard Trials
Hard Trials Tips
Hard Trials are done in two splits: Hard Trial 1 to Hard Trial 4, and then Hard Trial 5 & Hard Trial 6. There is a big gap between these 2 splits, because Hard Trial 5 and Hard Trial 6 require Attacks Progression.

Hard Trial 1 - Hard Trial 4: Prioritize stacking DP^ bonuses, almost up to DP^4 for Hard Trial 4.

Hard Trial 5 & Hard Trial 6: Requires Attacks Progression.

Do Hard Trial 2 first (before Hard Trial 1), it's much easier (just like Easy Trials 1 and 2).

The Recommended Season-Planet Bonuses for Hard Trials are:

Sun: Winter (Aquarius, Eternity Rewards ^1.2)
Mercury: Winter (Aquarius, Star Cost ^0.9)
Venus: Winter (Aquarius, DU Power x1.05)
Moon: Autumn (Libra, Eternity Rewards ^1.15)
Mars: Autumn (Libra, Star Cost ^0.85)
Jupiter: Autumn (Libra, DU Power x1.03)
Neptune: Summer (Gemini/Cancer/Leo, DU Power x1.06)
Hard Trial 1
Supernova Req^^ is recommended as the second bonus on your Wind zodiacs, with ^DP Gain being the main bonus you want.

For DTP Trees, use the following:

Supernovae: C1;T1,1,5,4;B1,1,1,5
DP: C5;M4,1,5,5
AP/EP: C1;M1,1,5,5;B2,5,0,0
Hard Trial 2
Get enough +free Lab Levels on Aquarius Zodiacs to get to Dilation (250+), then Dilation/Dilation Tree will be easy enough. Strong Fire Zodiacs can also assist with getting to Dilation (Aries with Mult Gain and Saggitarius with +Slowdown are the strongest for this).

Hard Trial 3
Supernova Req^^ will not be useful for Hard Trial 3, and ^DP Gain is very important to have on your Wind Zodiacs. It's very easy with ^3.00 or more total DP Gain, but can be completed with at least ^1.85 DP Gain.

Hard Trial 4
Requires around ^4.00 total DP Gain from Wind Zodiacs. Unlocking Neptune (by getting a Level 30+ Zodiac; ^DP Gain will help you do this) for the extra Planet slot will make this easier to achieve.

Hard Trial 5 and 6
Attack level 250 is recommended. Requires level 60-80 Godly/Divine Fire Zodiacs (as many as you can use, any will do mostly).

After Completing all the Hard Trials
The reward for completing all the Hard Trials is breaking the Unity limit, your Eternity Points limit is gone.



Full Unity Macro tutorial, only works after Hard Trial 6
But before making the macro you need to setup 3 Dilation Tree Point Loadouts (or more if you bought more Dilation Tree Point Loadouts).

Here are the suggested Dilation Tree Point Loadouts:

First Dilation Tree Point Loadout (21 DTP): C5;T0,0,0,0;M5,1,5,5;B0,0,0,0

Second Dilation Tree Point Loadout(45 DTP): C5;T1,1,5,5;M5,1,5,5;B1,1,5,5

Third Dilation Tree Point Loadout (65 DTP) C5;T5,5,5,5;M5,5,5,5;B5,5,5,5


PS: if it doesn’t work for you, manually setup the DTPLs

Now it’s time for the macro:

RepeatUntil (EP > 0.00)
    Infinite()
    Eternate()
Repeat (20, false)
    EnterEC(0, 0, false)
    WaitForSeconds(0.2)
    ExitEC()
    WaitForSeconds(0.1)
    Eternate()
    if (DP == 0.00)
        Dilate(true)
        WaitUntil (Score >= 1.00e10)
Repeat (4, false)
    if (EP > 0.00)
        LoadDT(0)
        LoadDT(1)
        LoadDT(2)
        Dilate(true)
        WaitForSeconds(0.25)
        Dilate(false)
        WaitForSeconds(0.5)
WaitUntil (EP == 0.00)
You may need to increase wait times and/or number of repeats (default 20 repeats is for Eternity Challenges (HT4+), 4 is for loading Dilation Trees), tested at HT4 with no Attacks Progress, could potentially work earlier with good Zodiacs.

Although, for guaranteed workability I recommend making the macro after you complete all the Hard Trials, since a lot of people can’t get the macro working after HT4 and HT5.

The macro can work as early as Hard Trial 3 if you adjust the last 2 WaitForSeconds intervals higher, try 1 second and adjust higher/lower as needed. LoadDT(0) can also use a template with only C5;M5 (10 DTP) to lower the initial DTP requirements. LoadDT(1) can be dropped to C5;T1,1,5,0;M5,0,0,0;B1,1,5,0 (24 DTP). You may also need to add an addition WaitForSeconds block to the first Infinite/Eternate loop, especially when running pure Water builds, but a single Fire/Wind Zodiac could also prevent infinite looping.

Insane Trials
This guide only covers Early, Medium and Hard Trials. Insane trials are unlocked at an entirely different point in progression, and the guide for these can be found at Guide:Insane Trials.

Guide:Attacks
Attacks are unlocked after completing 15 trials. A new tab is added with a new revolution type, Attack Circles. For every Attack Level, there is an enemy, depicted as an HP bar on the top of the screen. Your goal is to deal enough damage to deplete the enemy's HP bar and advance to the next Attack Level.

This Page will walk you thought attacks.

You will find the Builds at the Bottom of the page

For achievment 200, 230-233 that you wont get by simple progression refer to [Guide:Achievements]


Contents

6.2	Level Build
6.3	Luck Build
Primary Gameplay Loop
Your main catalyst for progressing through attacks is gold. You get gold every time you unite. The current attack level determines how much gold you will gain from uniting.

The primary gameplay loop is therefore to push attack level as far as you can as fast as you can, collect the gold and start over. With the gold you earn, you can purchase relics and upgrade your attack circles.

Getting Rich Quickly
Changing Zodiacs resets unity, but it does not reset attack level. You can make use of that game mechanic by starting your attack run with game speed zodiacs(game speed build) and wait until your attack level isn't increasing rapidly anymore. Only then, you switch to the build that maximizes damage(score build). You then go through Eternity Challenges, grow your Dilation Tree fully and do another short dilation run to maximize your attack damage. Unite when attack level isn't increasing anymore to collect the gold.

Later on, you can skip the swapping process by mixing game speed and damage zodiacs (Gold Farm Build). With this build, you can also fully automate Gold Farming by using a Macro (Full Unity Macro) for afk gold farming.

Spending Gold Wisely
Farming gold is frame limited as you can only get up to 60 attack levels per second. If you already are at the maximum speed, gold farming can't be sped up any further using Time Flux because the maximum speed of the increasing attack levels is 60 per second. This makes gold farming time consuming and the right choice of how to spend that gold an important decision.

You can choose to spend your gold on purchasing Relics or on leveling up your Attack Circles. After getting Achievement 239, the effectiveness of your Relics increases with your current attack level. This also applies to the purchase cost effect of Relic 14, making the purchase of Relics a lot cheaper at max attack level. If you have Achievement 239 unlocked, you should always wait for max attack level before any relic purchase.

Circle Ascensions
Ascending your Attack Circles is the most powerful boost to attack progression and should always be prioritized.

While leveling up your Attack Circles only increases lap speed moderately, it does not increase circle damage. Ascending Circles on the other hand increases damage massively by multiplying attack Mult Gain by its ascension power (but decreases lap speed).

You generally want to avoid spending gold on leveling up Attack Circles unless you can gain an Ascension stage. By setting your purchase amount to x25, you can check the total gold cost for the next stage.

After Ascending an Attack circle, you should always follow up with some Attack Mult Farming to actually increase your Attack Mult Damage.

Relics Priority
1) Relic 14

Decreases the purchase cost for all Relics and therefore should always be prioritized.

2) Relics 20 and 21(swap 21 and 17 below after R21 lv100)

Your primary way to increase your gold gain per second is to increase your maximum attack level. The fastest and easiest way to do that is through Attack Mult Farming, so the Relics that increase Attack Mult Gain have a very high priority. Relic 20 is insanely powerful and should have a similar priority as Relic 14 and Circle Ascensions. Due to the fact that relic 21 can empower relic 17, further strengthening relic 9, it overtakes relic 20 at around level 1400.After level 100, relic 21 is softcapped, making relic 17 stronger.

3) Relics 9, 16, 17 and 18

While not as powerful as Relic 20, Relics 16 and 18 still help with Attack Mult Gain, while Relics 9 & 17 directly increase Gold Gain. These Relics should all be high up on your priority list. Unlike for Relics 14, 20 and Circle Ascensions, you shouldn't go out of your way to save up for these though. Relic 24 boosts game speed significantly and speeds up mult farming significantly.

4) Relics 3, 13 & 19

These Relics moderately increase damage output and should get at least some priority over others.

5) Pretty much everything else

All other Relics should have a low priority for progressing through attack stages.

They will still slightly improve attack or revolution damage or greatly help with other aspects of the game (such as Relics 2, 4, 8, 15, 24, 25, 26 and 27 for general zodiac improvements), so just buy these when they are cheap enough to not make much of a dent into your war chest.

Attack Mult Farming
Your Attack Mults do not reset on Unity. Every time a circle completes a revolution, the corresponding Attack Mult increases by its Mult Gain value. This means that you can permanently and greatly increase your attack damage by using some time flux, time warp or just go afk for a bit while maximizing your Game Speed using Game Speed Build.

This is incredibly powerful for increasing your gold gain by increasing your max attack level, especially after ascending your attack circles and upgrading your Mult Gain related Relics. You may feel that running the game on speed build is not effective since your attack level does not increase, but keep in mind that it will increase your attack mult damage permanently for all future runs.

After Achievement 239, you should do your attack mult farming on close to maximum attack level. Don't unite while farming. Achievement 239 massively increases the Mult Gain Value at high attack levels by boosting relics 20, 16 and 18.

Other Sources of Progress
The Score Damage Multiplier on the top left of the Attacks screen is based on the Current Score from Revolutions Tab. The formula that converts your Current Score to the Score Damage Multiplier is improved by Relics 13 and 19, but you can also work on improving your current score by relic 6.

Current Score is mainly determined by your Zodiac Stats, Planet and Unity Bonuses and your Sacrifice Stats, so apart from choosing the right build, you can improve your Attack damage by improving your Zodiacs, improving your Sacrifice Stats and farming unities.

Leveling up your Donuts in Planet shop will help you a lot with Zodiac Improvement because they are boosted by relics 24, 25, 26 and 27. The currency to do so is Astrodust, so farming Astrodust is another viable path towards progress.

Improving Zodiacs
Zodiac stats are generally improved by increasing rarity, quality and level. Apart from its effect on zodiac stats, zodiac level additionally increases attack damage through Relic 3. You can further improve your Zodiacs by increasing certain stats at the expense of other stats by redistributing their Stat Orders. It is generally advisable to only do this for your Pisces to have Zodiac Quality as top stat until very late in the game.

The generic way to improve your zodiacs is to farm new zodiacs using Quality Build. Better Pisces means better Zodiacs both quality and rarity wise, so improving these should be your priority. You generally want to make sure that the zodiacs you farm this way are at maximum level and quality, so you should preferably use a Full Unity Macro for this purpose.

Following this advice will let you breeze through attacks handily, but your zodiacs will plateau around level 122, 80M-100M quality and +12-14 rarity. To push through early minerals faster, you will need to look into a slightly obscure game mechanic called Zodiac Maxing.

Quality
Can be improved by increasing Zodiac Quality Sacrifice Stat, increasing the quality donut in planet shop and by improving Relics 2, 15 and 26. Merging also improves quality by around 7% per merge (see Merging).

Rarity
Can be improved by increasing Luck Sacrifice Stat, increasing the luck donut in planet shop and by improving Relics 4, 15 and 25. If you have souls to waste, increasing shop luck in Soul Shop is not the worst way to spend them.

Level
Zodiac Level is improved by increasing Zodiac Factors. This is generally achieved by making sure that your farming Macro pushes DP gain close to maximum. The Factors can further be improved by increasing the level donut in planet shop, by improving Relics 8 and 27 along with farming unities. Almost all Sacrifice Stats will have a positive impact on your Factors.

Sacrificing
Sacrifice Stats both directly and indirectly increase your Attack damage. Direct ways include increasing revolution score by improving stats like DP gain and SN req, while indirect ways include stats like Zodiac Quality or Luck by improving your zodiac farming power.

Sacrificing better zodiacs give more stat gains, but sacrificing more zodiacs gives more stat gains than sacrificing better zodiacs. To maximize gains from Sacrificing, you'll therefore generally want a good mix between speed and quality by using a Fast Sacrificing Macro.

The most important sac stats for attack progression are Zodiac Quality and Mult Gain. Further very useful stats are Luck & Center DTU (Water), Common Exp (Fire), DP Gain, SN Req, Free Labs & Lab Mult (Wind)

Keep in mind that stat gains from sacrificing decrease with every sacrifice of this stat type. This means that gains are largest for the stats you have sacrificed the least, so it's worthwhile to do at least some sacrificing even for lower priority stats.

Also remember that your stat gains increase with increasing quality zodiacs, so if you haven't done any sacrificing in a bit, but have improved your pisces a lot, give it another go to see if it feels worthwhile again.

For more information, see Sacrificing

Astrodust Farming
Astrodust is the currency you use to upgrade Planet Shop Donuts. It is also used to enhance and redistribute zodiacs. You can increase your Astrodust gain rate by upgrading Relic 5.

Upgrading both Quality and Luck Donuts greatly increase your Zodiac farming power, even more so with relics 25 and 26, and also increase your Astrodust farming rate. Maximize your Astrodust gain rate by using Fast Unity Macro in conjunction with Astrodust Farm Build.

Game Speed Donut helps you to increase your Attack Mult farming efficiency, while Zodiac Exp Donut helps a bit with increasing your Zodiac Levels for farming purposes. Your main focus should be on Quality and Luck, but it is still advisable to spend a bit of time upgrading the other two.

If it takes seconds or minutes to upgrade, it's probably worth it. If it takes hours or days, it's probably not. Keep in mind that as your overall stats go up, you'll also increase your dust gain rate, so it might be good to check back from time to time.

Unity Farming
Unity Bonuses gives only a very minor boost to Attack damage, so farming Unities is not the preferred option to improve Attacks. It can be noticeable if your unity count is very low or if you increase it by a lot.

Unity count will also indirectly help with progression by improving various Zodiac Factors, making it easier to reach higher Zodiac Levels.

Maximize your Unity gain rate by using Fast Unity Macro in conjunction with Unity Farm Build(all Gemini).

Ach. 239-240
To get them you need to complete all hard trials: [Guide:Trials]

To get ach. 239 you need to wait for some time using Game Speed Build, listed below in mid attacks loadout. But while you can get it now it is not necessary for game progression, so it can be skipped untill you complete it naturally by progressing game further.

Ach. 240 is the last and hardest step before minerals. There is not much you can do to get it, the only things are listed above but preferably you should increase gold as it is the main source of pogression now. For maximum efficiency use score build as it provides a lot more gold but it is very slow and tedious to progress. Alternatively you can use gold farm build wich is a lot more faster and provide good enough gold, but technically is worse than score one.

After getting Ach. 240 you unlock Guide:Minerals

Trials
Trials are unlocked when you have 3 zodiacs equipped. There are currently 25 trials as of v1.068.

These work similarly to Infinity and Eternity Challenges; you must reach a certain goal with a handicap applied. To start some trials, you must first lock it, then click the blue reset button in the top right.

There are 5 difficulties of trials: Easy, Medium, Hard, Insane, and the Bonus Trial.


Contents
1	Easy Trials
2	Medium Trials
3	Hard Trials
4	Insane Trials
5	Bonus Trial
Easy Trials
Trial	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Easy Trial 1	Reach Infinity	You can only buy the first 6 colors	x1.4 to zodiac quality
Easy Trial 2	You can't buy promotion 3 and 4	Unlocks Trials exp factor
Easy Trial 3	Common exponent is multiplied by 0.6	Common exponent multiplied by 1.05
Easy Trial 4	You can't buy promotion 2 and 4 or prestige	Unlocks Mars
Easy Trial 5	Common exponent can't be >0.7	Unlocks Max Score factor
Easy Trial 6	You can't prestige, promote or ascend	Unlocks Planet Shop
Reward for completing all Easy Trials

Requirement	Reward
Beat all Easy Trials	Keep revolutions don't take away score, Prestige & Promotions reset nothing, Unlocks Medium Trials.
Medium Trials
Trial	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Medium Trial 1	Reach Eternity	You can only buy the first two Infinity Generators	x1.337 to luck
Medium Trial 2	You can't buy Stars	Infinity & Infinity Challenges are now always 1ms
Medium Trial 3	You can't Prestige	Supernova resets nothing, unlocks Autobuy Dp Upgrades & DTP
Medium Trial 4	You can only Infinite twice	Unlocks Auto Unity
Medium Trial 5	You can only Infinite once	Unlocks DTP factor
Medium Trial 6	You can't prestige/promote and can only buy the first 5 colors	Keeps fastest Eternity at 5ms
Reward for completing all Medium Trials

Requirement	Reward
Beat all Medium Trials	Keep IP/second, unlocks Max IP factor, unlocks Hard Trials.
Hard Trials
Trial	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Hard Trial 1	Reach Unity	You can't buy more than 20 DTP	Unlocks DP factor
Hard Trial 2	You can't buy Lab or RP upgrades	Unlocks Lab level factor
Hard Trial 3	You can't Supernova	Unlocks Autosell zodiacs
Hard Trial 4	You can't buy more than 1500 AP/get past Supernova 10	Unlocks AP factor
Hard Trial 5	You can only buy 50 of each Dilation upgrade & 2 DTP	Divide all Relic costs by 1M
Hard Trial 6	You can't dilate or enter EC	Unlocks EP factor
Reward for completing all Hard Trials

Requirement	Reward
Beat all Hard Trials	Breaks Unity
Insane Trials
These trials work a bit differently than prior trials, as they must be completed in order. To unlock a trial, you must get the respective level to unlock it on weapons in Polish Prestige (see Minerals).

Trial	Unlock	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Insane Trial 1	Level 25 Knuckles	Buy 65 DTP	First DU is useless and dilation penalties are ^2 stronger.	You get 10 more EC from trials
Insane Trial 2	Level 50 Spear	Unite with e5.8M+ score	Common Exponent is divided by 1M	Now you keep all of your EC on Unity Reset. Trials reward is changed to PP gain mult.
Insane Trial 3	Level 50 Knuckles	Reach Unity	IP and EP gains are much worse now. DP Gain is multiplied by Min(1, Eternities amount). Reset eternites amount to 0 on Unity Reset.	Get additional 100% of IP and EP per second passively
Insane Trial 4	Level 100 Axe	Reach 2.018e705+ EP and 8.14e2025+ IP	All EC rewards are much worse now and actually are penalties.	You can complete 1 more difficulty for each EC. EC past 50 boost your magnet gain chance
Insane Trial 5	Level 100 Bow	Reach 65 DTP	DU1 is disabled. Dilation Penalties and DP gain scales much harsher.	Dilation's best score is automatically set to your current score raised to DU Power Penalty
Insane Trial 6	Level 100 Knuckles	Reach Attack Level 100	Attack Divider and Level Scalings are harsher, R13 is inverted, Gold and Ascend Strength is logarithmed. Attack Stats are wiped on Unity.	Passively gain 1 Unity per second, multiplied by Minerals' Game Speed
Reward for completing all Insane Trials

Requirement	Reward
Beat all Insane Trials	You gain 1 merge exp per second, boosted by local speed in minerals tab

Reward for completing the Bonus Trial

Requirement	Reward
Beat the Bonus Trial	Multiplies Golden Resource Gain by 10x

File:Icon tab att Attacks
Attacks are unlocked after completing 15 trials. A new tab is added with a new revolution type, Attack Circles. For every Attack Level, there is an enemy, depicted as an HP bar on the top of the screen. Your goal is to deal enough damage to deplete the enemy's HP bar and advance to the next Attack Level.

There are a total 5 Attack Circles and they work similarly to the base game's circles. Every revolution/lap increases the Attack Circle's Attack Damage Multiplier on the top of the screen. In addition, each revolution/lap counts as a hit to the enemy, dealing damage to its HP bar.

When you Unite and get a new Zodiac, the Attack Level gets reset to 0 and you will be rewarded with the amount of Gold that is shown at the bottom of the screen. Your Circle Attack Multipliers will NOT be reset upon Uniting and performing a Soft Unity Reset (by e.g. swapping Zodiacs) will not reset Attack Level.


Depiction of where the items discussed in this article can be found in-game.
1 - Buyables
1.1-1.6 - Attack Circle Stats
2.1 - HP Bar
2.2 - Current Gold
3.1 - Current Revolution Score and the Score Damage Mult
3.2 - Attack Circle Mults
3.3 - Cumulative Multiplier
3.4 - Damage Exponent
3.5 - Base Damage

Attack Circles
Every Attack Circle has its own button showing its current Lap Speed and cost to upgrade. Clicking the button will level up the Attack Circle by 1. Each Circle Level will cost

Upgrading will increase its lap speed by 5% of its base speed. For Max Level (20), the Lap Speed is double of what it is at Level 1.

When Max Level is reached, you will be able to Ascend the Attack Circle by pressing the A on the left of the Circle Button. This will reset Circle Level to 1 and increase the Circle's Ascension Stage by 1.

Attack Circles
Circle	Color	Base Lap Speed	Base Cost [Gold]	Base Damage
1	Electric Red	1/100s	10	1
2	Firebrick Red	1/200s	100	3
3	Burnt Umber	1/400s	1000	10
4	Van Dyke Brown	1/1600s	1e6	150
5	Dark Lava	1/4000s	1e9	5000
By mousing over the Circle Buttons, the following stats will be shown for the respective Circle:

Ascension Stage
Current Ascension stage. Ascending increases Ascension Stage by 1 and resets circle Level to 1. Reduces Lap Speed by 50%, but multiplies Mult Gain and Damage Mult by Ascension Power. This is extremely powerful.

For each Ascension stage, the cost scaling of purchasing Circle Levels increases.

Current Circle Level. Lap speed is increased by 5% of its Level 1 speed for each Level. Each Circle Level will cost

Lap Speed
Number of revolutions per second. Affected by the Circle's current Level and multiplied by Relic 7 (Snail Statue).

Mult Gain
Every time the circle completes a revolution, its corresponding Attack Multiplier increases by this value.

For every Ascension Stage, Mult Gain gets multiplied by Ascension Power. This is extremely powerful.

Damage Multiplier
Every time the circle completes a revolution, the enemy gets hit and the HP bar is reduced by the total Attack Base Damage multiplied by this Damage Mult.

For every Ascension Stage, Damage Mult gets multiplied by Ascension Power:

Ascension Power
Every time you ascend the circle, Mult Gain and Damage Mult get multiplied by Ascension Power.

The Enemy

Table showing Enemy HP at select Attack Levels
A list of Enemy HP values at select Attack Levels is provided to the right. For all enemy HP values and Gold calculations, you will need to use the Excel-Sheet provided here: Attack Gold & HP Scaling Calculator v2

Enemy HP Bar
Displays the amount of damage your Attack Circles need to deal to advance to the next Attack Level. Your current Attack level is shown to the left.

Gold Gain
How much Gold you will gain from Uniting at the current Attack Level. Increases with every enemy you defeat and is increased by Relics 9 (Midas Hand), 17 (Magical Magnet) and 21 (Queen of spades). The small number shown with a “+” is your Gold Gain for uniting, the larger number is the total amount of gold you currently own.



Damage Multipliers
Every time a circle completes a revolution, damage is dealt to the enemy. The amount of damage is determined by the Circle's Damage Multiplier as well as the Multipliers listed at the Top of the Attacks tab:

Score Damage Multiplier
The number to the right of the Arrow shown at the top left of the Attacks tab. Results from the formula on the top left of the relics tab. The Score Damage Multiplier is based on your Current Score from Revolutions Tab (shown at the top left before the arrow).

Current Score is mainly determined by your Zodiac Stats, Planet and Unity Bonuses and your Sacrifice Stats. The formula that converts your Current Score to the Score Damage Multiplier can be improved by Relics 13 (Strategic Board Game: Increased Exponent, strengthened by bow level 25) and 19 (Ancient Extractor: Reduced Divider).

Attack Multipliers
The 5 numbers shown on the top middle of the Attacks tab. This is the primary way to increase attack damage. If you understand the concept behind Attack Mults, you understand how Attack Progression works.

Every Attack Circle has its own Attack Mult, so there's a total of 5 of them. Every time a circle completes a revolution, the corresponding Attack Mult increases by its Mult Gain value. Mouse over the Attack Circle buttons to see the value.

Attack Mult increases with every revolution, so game speed greatly increases your gains. After Achievement 239, this can be improved even further by reaching max attack level before switching to game speed build.

This is because max attack level improves Relics 16 (Cool Glasses), 18 (Mythical Rune) and 20 (Majestic Halberd), which all improve Mult Gain as follows:

Mult Gain = 
Base Mult Gain displayed in and improved by Relic 16
×
Ascension Power displayed in and improved by Relic 18
A
s
c
e
n
s
i
o
n
S
t
a
g
e
−
1
 Relic 20 raises both Base Mult Gain AND multiplies Ascension Power. This leads to some sort of positive feedback loop that makes the concept of farming Attack Multipliers the single most important game mechanic to progress through attacks.

Cumulative Multiplier
The number shown on the top right of Attacks tab. Product of the Multipliers gained from Relics 1 (Silver Coin), 3 (Copper Bunny Statuette) and 12 (Sandstone Chest) as well as your Damage Multiplier from Achievement Rewards (Unlocked at Ach 201 for a current Maximum of x4.4 at 320/320 achievements).

The main component for the Cumulative Multiplier is Relic 3 (Copper Bunny Statuette). This Multiplier is greatly improved by increasing your Zodiac Levels.

Attack Exponent
The Exponent shown near the top right of the Attacks tab. It is increased by performing an attack prestige, which unlock after getting Achievement 251, as of the 1.025 update.

Total Attack Base Damage
The number shown as Base damage near the top right of the Attacks tab. Product of Score Damage, Attack Multiplier Damage, Cumulative Multipliers and Attack Exponent. Every time a circle completes a revolution, the enemy gets hit and the HP bar is reduced by this base damage value multiplied by the Circle's Damage Mult.

Attacks Strategy
After your first few unities, the gameplay becomes a lot less linear as zodiac selection and choice is different for each playthrough. There still are a number of general concepts, strategies and ideas to be followed for fast progression through attacks.

For progression up to and including Elemental Challenges, please refer to the Walkthrough. After completing these, the general gameplay loop revolves around gathering Gold to spend on Relics and Attack Circle Levels.

A detailed guide on how to progress through attacks is provided in Attacks Strategy.

Relics
Relics are a part of the core gameplay loop of Unity, they are unlocked along with Attacks after completing 15 trials, they can be found as the third tab in the unity section. Relics can be purchased with gold that is obtained from the attacks tab, each relic has a different purchase price that increases exponentially based of how much of that relic you have purchased. There are 70 relics that apply buffs to Attacks, Score, Zodiacs, Minerals as well as other relics. The main gameplay surrounding relics involves farming gold from attacks which will be reinvested into increasing your attack level, gold can then also be used to increase the stats of your zodiacs and score calculations to help progress the rest of the game.


Relics tab header, details the player's current score-to-damage formula on the left, current gold count and number of unlocked relics in the middle and the player's highest attack level reached, their attack ascension power and attack Mult Gain bonus on the right
Below the header is an array of 70 relics, each showing the current number of each relic purchased, the stats they give, and the cost to buy another one of that Relic. All 70 relics and their effects are listed in the table below.

(?)Sacrificing
After reaching 301 levels of any relic, it can be sacrificed. This will make a purple level counter appear below the regular level counter, and this will be set to the amount of relics you already had before you sacrificed it. After this, the level requirement is set to one above the previous sacrificed amount. The price will NOT be reset, but levels can be regained through spawning Minerals. The sacrifice level will boost the respective relic's bonus a decent amount, so relics should always be sacrificed, especially when late into minerals progression.

Notes
Recommended relic priority is 1 and 3 in the early game, and 14 -> 20 -> 16 -> 18 -> 9 -> 17 -> 19 as you approach Attacks Level 1,337 (Achievement 240)

Zodiacs
Zodiacs are collectible entities in the Astrology system that provide bonuses when assigned to Planets. Each Zodiac belongs to one of four Elements — Fire, Wind, Water or Earth — and corresponds to one of the four seasons.

The strength of each Zodiac's bonuses scales with its Quality, Level, and Rarity. If you want to reroll, it will cost you 200 souls. (+50 per reroll)

Zodiac Signs
There are 12 Zodiac signs in total. Each can have up to four possible bonuses unique to its sign and element group. Some bonuses are shared across signs of the same element.

Zodiac Types and Bonus

Zodiac Level
Each Zodiac has a level, which increases based on Zodiac Exp. Zodiac Exp is influenced by multiple factors and determines how strong the Zodiac’s bonuses become.

Below is a list of the contributing factors to Zodiac Exp.

Factor	Unlock Condition	Thresholds
Trials	Clear ET2	No threshold
Unities	Achv 167	No threshold
EP	Clear HT6	1.08e2466
Score	Clear ET5	e1e9
Supernova	Achv 171	151
Planet Shop	Clear ET6	no threshold
Eternity Count	Achv 182	1e12
DTP Count	Clear MT5	40
Max IP	All MT completion	e1e7
DP	Clear HT1	e3000
Lab Level	Clear HT2	20,000
Relic 8	Attack lv 80	no threshold
AP	Clear HT4	5e6
Luck	Achv 238	no threshold
VP	Sword Level 15	no threshold
Zodiac Quality
Zodiac quality will buff the strength of your zodiac bonuses by a ^1.25

buff(edit in the exact amount). Zodiac quality is very hard to buff early game, but becomes the main way to buff your zodiacs later on in attacks.

Zodiac Rarity
Zodiac Rarity will affect the base strength of the stat roll that you can have, it will also affect how many boosts your zodiac will give.

Garbage - Epic: 2 boosts
Legendary - Godly: 3 boosts
Divine - Immortal+: 4 boosts
The luck range for each rarities from Garbage to Immortal are:

Rarity	Range Start	End
Garbage	1	3.00
Common	1	5.19
Uncommon	1	8
Rare	1	12
Epic	2.32	25.61
Legendary	3.73	99.48
Mythic	5.59	550
Godly	9.30	997
Divine	14.23	1665
Immortal	56.74	1665+
After you get ~550-600+ luck you will get Achievement #220, allowing you to get +x Immortal rarity, these never stop showing up past their unlock threshold.

+Immortal	Min. luck
needed

+4	1327
+5	1659
+6	2099
+7	2567
+8	3159
+9	3828
+10	4572
+11	5432
+12	6425
+13	7602
+14	8688
+15	9962
+16	11454
+17	13108
+18	14780
+19	16959
After about 1e32 luck and Achievement #342 (merge 3 zodiacs of Immortal+1000 rarity), you can obtain Ethereal Zodiacs and +x Ethereal rarities.

All Zodiac rarities here on out will use the +x system, and progressing to the next category (as in completely new color, new everything) will require merging 3 +1000 zodiacs of the previous rarity, granting the respective achievement, which allows for obtaining the next rarity and all +x rarities up to +1000.

Additional Zodiac Rarities
Rarity	Range Start	Range Start for +1000 rarity	Respective Achievement (for merging 3 +1000 rarities)
Immortal	56.74	5.17e32	Achievement 342 ==> Ethereal Zodiacs
Ethereal	1.92e33	3.08e296	Achievement 426 ==> Amazing Zodiacs
Amazing	3.3e296	1.2e1472	Achievement 482 ==> Prime Zodiacs
Prime	1.57e1472	7.9e2671235	Achievement 670 ==> Rainbow Zodiacs
Rainbow	?	?	N/A
Note: the requirement for Prime+x rarities scale extremely fast, hence the insane requirement for Prime+1000 Zodiacs.

Zodiac Stats
Zodiac Stat Formula:

Mults Gain          = 1.35^x
Prom Power          = 1.2^log(x)
Common Exponent     = 1 + log(x) * 0.016
Ascension Power     = 1.5^log(x)
Laps Speed          = 2^log(x)
Slowdown Power      = round(log(x + 9)^1.5)
IP Gain             = 1 + log(x) * 0.025
Infinity Gain       = 3^log(x)
Gen Exponent        = 1 + log(x) * 0.024
Mult Per Bought Gen = 1 + log(x) * 0.007
Star Base           = 1.8^log(x)
Stardust Exponent   = 1 + log(x) * 0.02
Eternity Gain       = 2^log(x)
EP Gain             = 1 + log(x) * 0.011
Lab Mult Power      = 1 + log(x) * 0.012
Supernova Req       = 1 / (1 + log(x) * 0.02)
DP Gain             = 1 + log(x) * 0.05
Free Lab Levels     = round(log(x + 9)^3 * 5)
DTP Cost            = 1 / (1 + log(x) * 0.015)
Center DTU Eff      = 1 + log(x) * 0.02
Ach 29 Reward       = 1 + log(x) * 0.005
Game Speed          = 1 + log(x) * 0.1
Luck Add            = log((x / 20)^0.45 * 20) * 0.07
Zodiac Quality Mult = 1 + log(x) * 0.04
where:

x = Zodiac Score = 1.125^ZodiacRarity * ZodiacQuality * (9 + ZodiacLevel^2) * 2 * ((ZodiacLevel - 90) / 10) ^ 2.5
and:

ZodiacRarity = Immo+ Number + 9

Rarity Weight
Rarity "Weight" (unofficial name) is a value derived from Zodiac Luck that is used to calculate the chance for each rarity.

Rarity Chance
The chance that a given rarity is chosen is the fraction that its weight makes of all weights combined.

Chance(this Rarity) = Weight(this Rarity) / Sum(all rarity weights)

Planet Shop
The Planet Shop is unlocked by completing Easy Trial #6.


Contents

2	Donuts
3	Planet Shop Features

4.4	Zodiac Score
4.5	Sacrifice stats
5	Help
Astrodust
Astrodust is a resource which can be used to buy donuts, unlock Planet Shop features and spent to use some of those features.

Astrodust is obtained by selling zodiacs: to sell a zodiac, place it in the slot next to where "Sell for -" is displayed, then press "Sell for X", where X is the amount of astrodust you will receive.

There are several factors which determine how much astrodust your zodiacs sell for, including: zodiac level, quality, and rarity; Ach.193 reward; Relic #005 Wolf Skull effect.

Donuts
There are four donuts, each of which provides a different bonus:

Game Speed - Multiplies your Game Speed. Each level will increase your Game Speed mult by +0.01.

Luck - Multiplies your zodiac luck. Each level will increase your luck mult by +0.02.

Quality - Multiplies your zodiac quality. Each level will increase your zodiac quality mult by +0.03.

Zodiac Exp. Factor - Adds a new Factor. Each level will increase the Factor mult by +0.04.

Donuts are upgraded by spending astrodust and a donut's astrodust cost will increase each time it is bought. Donut upgrades persist through Unities.

Planet Shop Features
You can spend astrodust to unlock four new Planet Shop features:

Merging
Merging can be unlocked for 50 astrodust and allows you to insert 3 zodiacs which will be merged to create a new zodiac of the next rarity tier. Merged zodiacs must be of the same element and rarity. If you If you merge 3 of the same Zodiac, the resulting merged zodiac will always be its zodiac.

Enhancing
Enhancing can be unlocked for 350 astrodust and has a chance to raise the quality of the inserted zodiac, at the cost of astrodust. The higher the quality, the lower the chance of success, and the more astrodust each attempt will cost.

Redistribution
Redistribution can be unlocked for 500 astrodust and will randomize the inserted zodiac's stats, at the cost of astrodust. Redistributed zodiacs have a 1/3 chance of becoming each zodiac of that element and stat values, which stats are present (if the zodiac has less than 4 stats) and the stat order are all randomized.

Sacrificing
Sacrificing can be unlocked for 10,000,000 astrodust and will Sacrifice the inserted zodiac in order to permanently gain a (small) portion of the zodiac's top/first listed stat.

Statistics for Nerds
Zodiac Sale Price
= ((1.125^ZodiacRarity
    * ZodiacQuality
    * (9 + ZodiacLevel^2)
	 * {8 < ZodiacRarity: 2, 8 >= ZodiacRarity: 1}
	 * {ZodiacLevel >= 100: ((ZodiacLevel - 90) / 10) ^ 2.5, ZodiacLevel < 100: 1}) / 10)^0.75 * 1.1^ZodiacRarity
Zodiac Enhance Price Formula
= ZodiacSellCost * 2 * 1.3^lg(1 + ZodiacQuality)
Zodiac Enhance Chance Formula
= 0.9^(lg(1 + ZodiacQuality)^2) * 100%
Zodiac Score
= 1.125^ZodiacRarity * ZodiacQuality * (9 + ZodiacLevel^2) * {8 < ZodiacRarity: 2, 1} * {ZodiacLevel >= 100: ((ZodiacLevel - 90) / 10) ^ 2.5, 1}
Sacrifice stats
x is the zodiac score

All soft caps are the same as in the zodiac stats soft caps Zodiac Stat Formulae

 Mults Gain          = 1.35^x
 Prom Power          = 1.2^lg(x)
 Common Exponent     = 1 + lg(x) * 0.016
 Ascension Power     = 1.5^lg(x)
 Laps Speed          = 2^lg(x)
 Slowdown Power      = round(lg(x + 9)^1.5)
 IP Gain             = 1 + lg(x) * 0.025
 Infinity Gain       = 3^lg(x)
 Gen Exponent        = 1 + lg(x) * 0.024
 Mult Per Bought Gen = 1 + lg(x) * 0.007
 Star Base           = 1.8^lg(x)
 Stardust Exponent   = 1 + lg(x) * 0.02
 Eternity Gain       = 2^lg(x)
 EP Gain             = 1 + lg(x) * 0.011
 Lab Mult Power      = 1 + lg(x) * 0.012
 Supernova Req       = 1 / (1 + lg(x) * 0.02)
 DP Gain             = 1 + lg(x) * 0.05
 Free Lab Levels     = round(lg(x + 9)^3 * 5)
 DTP Cost            = 1 / (1 + lg(x) * 0.015)
 Center DTU Eff      = 1 + lg(x) * 0.02
 Ach 29 Reward       = 1 + lg(x) * 0.005
 Game Speed          = 1 + lg(x) * 0.1
 Luck Add            = lg((x / 20)^0.45 * 20) * 0.07
 Zodiac Quality Mult = 1 + lg(x) * 0.04
Help
Your priorities for the donuts in the Planet Shop will change based on your progress. In the beginning, it is suggested you focus on buying Quality and Zodiac Exp. Factor. Later, once you reach Hard Trials, focus on buying Luck until you can start to get Legendary zodiacs. Once you are getting Legendary zodiacs, return to buying Factor and Quality, but keep investing in Luck, evenly splitting your spending between all three. After you unlock Attacks, focusing on Game Speed to begin with is recommended, then later you can buy all 4 donuts evenly or at your own preference.

Planets
Planets are "identifiers" for slots where Zodiacs are put in. When slotted, Zodiacs give their respective effects alongside bonuses based on the planet and the Zodiac's season.

When you assign a Zodiac to a planet, a confirmation pops up saying that assigning will trigger an Unity reset. This warning can be removed by going into Settings > Gameplay > Hide confirmation from: Zodiac Unity Reset or by clicking the "Don't show again" button in the window.

Relics
Relics are a part of the core gameplay loop of Unity, they are unlocked along with Attacks after completing 15 trials, they can be found as the third tab in the unity section. Relics can be purchased with gold that is obtained from the attacks tab, each relic has a different purchase price that increases exponentially based of how much of that relic you have purchased. There are 70 relics that apply buffs to Attacks, Score, Zodiacs, Minerals as well as other relics. The main gameplay surrounding relics involves farming gold from attacks which will be reinvested into increasing your attack level, gold can then also be used to increase the stats of your zodiacs and score calculations to help progress the rest of the game.


Relics tab header, details the player's current score-to-damage formula on the left, current gold count and number of unlocked relics in the middle and the player's highest attack level reached, their attack ascension power and attack Mult Gain bonus on the right
Below the header is an array of 70 relics, each showing the current number of each relic purchased, the stats they give, and the cost to buy another one of that Relic. All 70 relics and their effects are listed in the table below.

(?)Sacrificing
After reaching 301 levels of any relic, it can be sacrificed. This will make a purple level counter appear below the regular level counter, and this will be set to the amount of relics you already had before you sacrificed it. After this, the level requirement is set to one above the previous sacrificed amount. The price will NOT be reset, but levels can be regained through spawning Minerals. The sacrifice level will boost the respective relic's bonus a decent amount, so relics should always be sacrificed, especially when late into minerals progression.

Notes
Recommended relic priority is 1 and 3 in the early game, and 14 -> 20 -> 16 -> 18 -> 9 -> 17 -> 19 as you approach Attacks Level 1,337 (Achievement 240)

Minerals
Minerals are a late-game mechanic and part of the gameplay loop of Unity.


Contents

2	Common Minerals

4	Polish
5	Refinement

Spawn
The core mechanic allows you to spawn minerals. Each mineral costs Gold and every time you spawn one, its price increases. Over time, the cost gradually decrease back toward its minimum.

Minerals generate VP and you can merge two identical minerals to create a higher-level one, which produces even more VP. To merge a mineral, simply drag it on top of a matching mineral (like a level 5 mineral on another level 5 mineral). VP can be spent on various upgrades, such as increasing the base mineral level, expanding the grid size, boosting luck and gold generation, and more!

Common Minerals
These are the common minerals which are spawned in the first time of mineral section. Every 100 thresholds, the icons will loop.

For example: Mineral 1 and 101 are paper

Here are the list of Common Minerals (click [expand] to see the list):

Common Mineral List 


Mineral Upgrades
Spend VP (and later on, Magnets) to level upgrades to help boost your Minerals, Zodiacs, and Gold Gain.

Max Level

Magnet Chance Mult

More Magnets
Polish
Once you obtain a mineral of level 30 or higher, you can perform a Polish Prestige. Polish resets your VP, normal Minerals, All Mineral Upgrades, Magnets, and Merge Level. In exchange, you earn PP to upgrade the five weapons that grant vastly different buffs to assist not only your minerals, but also Attacks and Gold Gain.


The Polish tab
Sword	Axe	Spear	Bow	Knuckles

Refinement
After obtaining a level 70 mineral, you can perform a Refine Prestige. Refine resets your VP, normal Minerals, normal & magnet mineral upgrades, magnets, merge level, and all polish upgrades. In exchange you earn RfP which can be spent on the Refine Tree.


Refine Tree
Refine Tree
Refine Points (RfP) can be spent in a large upgrade tree. Circle nodes offer one-time upgrades while Squares like Refine Node 1 can be leveled multiple times. These upgrades grant bonuses, QoL improvements, and unlock new features.

View the full Refine Tree here.

Noteable upgrades:

Merge Level: Unlocked with Node 5; merge common minerals to earn Merge Experience. Earn enough to increase your Merge Level, increases VP gain earned every level.
Magnets: Unlocked with Node 18, which gives a chance to obtain Magnets when merging minerals. These can be spent on powerful upgrades.
Polishing Enhance (Node 28): spend RfP to enhance the bonuses of your Polish weapons
Runes: earn Sun and Moon Runes that can be spent on powerful upgrades or used to spawn special minerals.
Special Minerals: there are 10 different special minerals, each providing bonuses when spawned (only 9 are available at the beginning)
Special Mineral Sacrifice (Node 85): You can now sacrifice your special minerals, and start generating sacrifice dust after all minerals have been sacrificed. This dust can be used to level up sacrificed minerals past level 20, further enhancing their effects.
Many other major progression layers within Unity are unlocked through the Refine Tree, such as Elements being unlocked through Refine Node 55, or Tarot being unlocked though Refine Node 70.

Polish Enhance (Refine Node 28)

Polishing Enhance tab
Polish Enhance is a mostly minor mechanic unlocked through Refine Node 28. There are five upgrades, one for each polish weapon, and each level increases the respective polish weapon's effective level by 1%. (Effective level is the level that the effects are calculated based on, and the cost is based on the original level.)

However, the cost for each upgrade scales exponentially, so it is only viable in the late game.

Runes (Node 35)

The Runes tab
Runes are a game mechanic vital to late-game progression. Runes are divided into 2 separate categories: Sun Runes and Moon Runes, each having 2 unique upgrades and 2 similar upgrades (see table below). Moon generates faster than Sun but this is because Moon GRA upgrade (Generation Rate Amount, not to be confused with Generation Rate) is more expensive. Runes is a huge time wall between the refinement stage and the elements stage, often taking close to 2 weeks (from runes unlock to achievement 320) with minimal gameplay all throughout. They both generate at a rate of 1 every 10 days by default but are affected by local gamespeed and TF. Runes can be obtained faster by some nodes (eg. node 48), relic 34, upgrading their respective GRA (this costs runes) or by reducing the time it takes to generate Runes (by buying their respective first upgrades with RfP).

Runes Upgrades
Sun Runes	Moon Runes
Upgrade	Effects	Cost Scaling	Upgrade	Effects	Cost Scaling
Generation Rate	Sun Runes generation duration x0.95/level	x10/level	Generation Rate	Moon Runes generation duration x0.9/level	x100/level
Refine Node 1 efffect	base x1, +x0.1 to mult/level	x2/level	Refine Node 7 effect	base x1, +0.2 to mult/level	x2/level
Magnet Chance	base x1, +0.2 to mult/level	x1.5/level	Magnet Amount	base x1, +1 to mult/level	x3/level
Generation Amount	+1/level	x3/level (?)	Generation Amount	+1/level	x3/level
Special Minerals (SPM, Refine Node 41)

This page is a stub. You can help Revolution Idle Wiki by expanding it.
Reason: Spm permanent cost increase formula, runes info missing, need images for refine tree

The Special Minerals (also known as SPM) section of the Minerals tab is unlocked by purchasing Refine Node 41, which costs 50,000,000 (5e7) Refine Points (RfP). Special Minerals cost Runes to spawn and are kept on both Polish Prestige and Refine Prestige, so they provide permanent bonuses.

There are currently 10* Special Minerals which can be spawned, each with their own bonus, as per the table below.

*The 10th Special Mineral, the Black Gem, requires Refine Node 70 to be bought before they can be spawned, which also unlocks Tarot

Special mineral red.png

Description: A cool shining red gem. It will boost your VP gains!
Base Mineral Boost: 3.00x VP Gain

Blue Gem

Description: Deep blue special gem is great for making mineral cost increase slower
Base Mineral Boost: Cost Increase is powered to ^0.99

Description: Green like an emerald, also makes your mineral cost drop faster
Base Mineral Boost: Cost fall is powered to ^1.01

Description: Pink gem takes us back to astrology! Get zodiacs with better luck and quality!
Base Mineral Boost: Luck and Quality Bonus +0.05

Special mineral yellow.png

Yellow Gem

Description: Yellow is like the color of the money! Boost your gold gain!
Base Mineral Boost: Gold gain is multiplied by 10

Special mineral orange.png

Description: Orange gem is attractive for magnetism! Gives you more magnet chance!
Base Mineral Boost: 1.01x Magnet Chance

Base Mineral Boost: 1.50x PP gain

Base Mineral Boost: +x0.05 RfP Gain

Base Mineral Boost: Make the game speed +0.05x faster

Special mineral black.png

Description: Dark black gem, like hidden and mythical predictions... Gives more tarot draws
Base Mineral Boost: Tarot Draws +0.10 (draws are granted when deleted)

Spawning Special Minerals
Refine Node 41 adds a new Special Minerals grid within the Minerals tab and allows the player to spawn Special Minerals. The grid is 3x3 by default and can be increased in size (up to 9x9) by purchasing levels of Refine Node 44; up to 10x10 with Refine Node 72; and up to 11x11 with Refine Node 80.

When the Spawn button is used with the Special Minerals grid active, a random Special Mineral will be spawned, selected from the 9 currently available Special Minerals (10 after Refine Node 70 has been bought). Spawning Special Minerals costs Runes, starting at 10 Sun Runes and 10 Moon Runes per spawn, with the Rune cost increasing after spawning a certain number of Special Minerals.

With each spawn, you also increase your SMS factor which increases the cost of special minerals. After you hit 7500 SMS factor, the cost of special minerals significantly increase at an extreme exponential rate with each spawn, so limit your SMS factor to 7500 in your automation settings to prevent accidentally starting the extreme exponential cost that can stall progress for days.

SMS Factor	Base Spawn Cost
0	10
7	11
61	12
89	13
111	14
130	15
148	16
163	17
178	18
191	19
204	20
Special Minerals can be merged but - unlike Common Minerals - merging does not improve the effect of Special Minerals (a Level 2 Special Mineral has the same effect as two Level 1 Special Minerals). Merging Special Minerals is therefore only useful to reduce the amount of space they take up within the grid, and for Special Minerals Progression to increase the Base Level (aka spawn level) of Special Minerals.

It is strongly recommended that you do not merge Special Minerals until Special Minerals Progression is unlocked, otherwise you will not gain progress towards increasing the Base Level of Special Minerals.

Special Minerals Progression (Refine Node 46)
By default, Special Minerals spawn at Level 1, regardless of the level entered into the Spawn Lvl. box. The spawn level of Special Minerals can only be increased through Special Minerals progression, which is unlocked by purchasing Refine Node 46, which costs 200,000,000 RfP.

Once unlocked, a new Special Minerals progression option appears in the Refine Tree section. Within this new section, players can:

Upgrade the efficiency of each Special Mineral at the cost of RfP

Special Minerals Progression
View progress towards increasing the Base Level of each Special Mineral

This page is a stub. You can help Revolution Idle Wiki by expanding it.
Reason: SPM Efficiency Cost Scaling, Effect details

The Base Level of each Special Mineral is increased by merging a certain number of that type of Special Mineral. Once a Special Minerals reaches Base Level 2, it will spawn at Level 2, effectively doubling the spawned amount of Special Minerals of that type.

Reaching Base Level 2 (for each type of Special Mineral) requires 100 merges, which is equivalent to having a Level 7, Level 6 and two Level 3 Special Minerals of that type (104 Special Minerals). Getting a Special Mineral to Base Level 2 is the requirement for Achievement 320, which was the final Achievement in the game in version 1.034.

It takes a maximum of 928 Special Mineral spawns to have at least one Special Mineral reach Base Level 2, for a total maximum of 55,509 Runes of each type. On average it is more likely to need around 800 spawns, or about 40,000 Runes of each type. Credit: @niedzielan in the Oni Gaming Discord, for calculating the number of spawns/Runes required.

Sacrifice Special Minerals (Refine Node 85)

This page is a stub. You can help Revolution Idle Wiki by expanding it.
Reason: Needs Sacrificed Minerals Effect Scaling/level, sacrifice dust req./level


The Special Minerals Sacrifice tab
Refine Node 85 adds a new tab to Minerals, accessible from the base Minerals tab. Destroying 100 Level 20 minerals of each Special Mineral type through deletion or The Tower or macros allows you to sacrifice it, granting a level 27 version of that mineral within the Sacrifice tab. Once a special mineral has been sacrificed, the player can only spawn the other special mineral types until all have been sacrificed. Sacrificing all special minerals unlocks Sacrifice Dust, which is granted through spawning special minerals instead of actually spawning a special mineral. This Dust can be used to level up sacrificed minerals.

Sacrifice Special Minerals Level-Up Requirements
Level	Sacrifice Dust Requirement
27	Destroy 100 Level 20 minerals
28	1,000,000 Sacrifice Dust
29	4,000,000
30	81,000,000
31	4.1e9
32	3.91e11
33	6.05e13
34	1.38e16
35	4.4e18
36	1.85e21
n	(lvl-27)^((lvl-28)×2)×1,000,000(not confirmed)
Sacrificed minerals, in addition to having the effect of the original Special Mineral, also have a second effect that scales much slower.

Elements

The 4 elements.
Elements are unlocked through Refine Tree Node 55, which adds a new subtab to the Unity tab.


There are 4 elements which are Fire, Earth, Wind and Water.

Each of the elements are boosted by their own zodiac element. Fire zodiacs (Aries, Leo, Sagittarius) boost Fire generation for example.

You can only generate one element at a time.

The Elements tab also has Local game speed just like the Minerals tab. The base local game speed is ^0.05 to minerals local game speed, but it can be increased via Tarot cards.


Contents
1	Element Factors
2	Element Upgrades
2.1	Fire
2.2	Earth
2.3	Wind
2.4	Water
Element Factors
The factors are what multiply your element generation rates, and they are based off of many things. You start with one factor for each element, but eventually it can be increased to three factors.

Description	Unlock Condition
Fire	Factor 1	Score	None
Factor 2	Score Exponent	King of Wands card
Factor 3	Asc. Power	Fire Node 11
Earth	Factor 1	Infinities	None
Factor 2	Gen Exp.	King of Pentacles card
Factor 3	IP	Earth Node 10
Wind	Factor 1	Eternities	None
Factor 2	Supernovae	King of Swords card
Factor 3	EP	Wind Node 12
Water	Factor 1	Luck	None
Factor 2	Zodiac Quality	King of Cups card
Factor 3	Ach 29 Reward	Water Node 11
The third factor for each element is unique as it raises the element generation to a power (^) instead of giving a flat multiplier. There is also a global element multiplier stat at the botton of the tab, which multiplies or divides all element generation, which the base is diving all elements by 1e10.

3	3	Attack level past 3600 boosts Fire generation
