Tarot

The Tarot tab (late-game)
Tarot is an endgame mechanic and part of the gameplay loop of Unity. It's unlocked after acquiring Refine Tree Node 70.


Contents
1	Spawn
2	Suits
2.1	Swords
2.2	Wands
2.3	Pentacles
2.4	Cups
2.5	Arcans
3	Tarot Upgrades
4	Tarot Challenges
5	Artifacts
5.1	Parts
5.2	LOGPOW
6	Trivia
Spawn
To earn a tarot card draw, you discard Black Gems, rare Special Minerals that initially have a 1% chance to spawn. To start, you will need at least ten level 1 Black Gems, as they give 0.10 draws. After drawing 4 cards, earn Achievement 365 which grants the Chariot Arcana, which lets you decrease your SMP to spawn more Special Minerals.

Suits
There are four common suits: Swords, Wands, Pentacles, and Cups. Each suit contains 14 cards. The Ace of each suit generates Tarot resources, while the higher cards boost the resource generation effect of previous cards in addition to providing passive bonuses to earlier game stages. The Arcana suit contains special cards with usable skills that have instant or timed effects, followed by a cooldown period, and has a total of 22 cards, but the cards become a passive effect after completing their respective Tarot Challenge.

Cards in the common suits are drawn in order; for example, you cannot get the three of cups without already having the two of cups. The spawn chance for each suit is listed in their header, along with the chance to draw a new card of that suit if pulled.

Swords
This suit mainly buffs Attacks.

Tarot Upgrades

Tarot Upgrades (late-game)
After getting Achievement 390 (1,000,000 of each Tarot resource i.e. Swords, Wands, Pentacles and Cups), the Tarot Upgrades menu is unlocked.

Here you can spend resources earned from common suits (generated by the Ace of each suit) on various upgrades which provide bonuses.

Each Card suit has its own set of upgrades, and these cost the respective Tarot resource generated by cards of that suit.

Upgrade 4 for each suit does in fact affect itself, which allows for seemingly infinite scaling of it and the previous upgrades, until it caps at 49.

Tarot Challenges

Tarot Challenges tab (late-game)
After getting Refine Tree Node #89, you unlock Tarot Challenges.

Each Tarot card has it's own respective challenge. For each of the five suits (including arcans), you must complete the previous challenge before unlocking the next one (i.e. you need to complete the challenge for the Ace of Pentacles before you can unlock the Two of Pentacles Challenge, and you need to complete The Chariot challenge before unlocking the Justice challenge.)

Each Challenge provides a boost to the respective cards effect, and a boost to the first effect of every card in that suit. All of your Tarot Challenges combined boost Sacrifice Dust gain.

These boosts can be increased via Tarot Upgrades and Relics.

Swords Tarot Challenges
Challenge	Penalty	Completion Requirement
Ace of Swords	Resets Attack Levels. Attack and Damage mults are logarithmed. You cannot gain them during the challenge.	Attack Level 1500
Two of Swords	Game Speed, Attack, and Damage mults are put to double Log10 of their values. You cannot gain them during the challenge. (Double Log10 means Log10(Log10(X)).)	Attack Level 400
Three of Swords	Game Speed, Attack and Damage mults are logarithmed, raised to ^0.05, then divided by 10. You cannot gain them during the challenge.	Attack Level 200
Four of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and attack mults are set to 1. Game speed and other mults are logarithmed. Mult gain is put to double log10.	Attack Level 2000
Five of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and attack mults are set to 1. Game speed and other mults are logarithmed. Mult gain is logarithmed and ^0.2.	Attack Level 2026
Six of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and gold and attack mults are set to 1. Game speed and other mults are logarithmed. Ascension power is reduced to double Log10. Gold gain is logarithmed.	Attack Level 2240
Seven of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and gold and attack mults are set to 1. Game speed and gold gain is logarithmed. Other mults are reduced to double Log10. Ascension Power is heavily nerfed, but the penalty weakens based on Attack Level.	Attack Level 2525
Eight of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and attack mults are set to 1. Ascension Power and all other mults are set to 1. Mult gain only comes from Attack Prestige.	Attack Level 7350
Nine of Swords	Resets Attack levels and mults. Only attack mult from score is effective, and it is ^(1/1e10). Game Speed is reduced to double Log10.	Attack Level 850
Ten of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and sets Attack mults to 1. Game speed and other mults are logarithmed. Mult gain is set to Sword's LOGPOW.	Attack Level 3200
Page of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and sets Attack mults to 1, Game speed and other multipliers are logarithmed. Mult Gain is set to Sword's LOGPOW twice.	Attack Level 2500
Knight of Swords	Resets Attack Levels and sets Attack mults to 1, Game speed and other multipliers are logarithmed. Mult Gain is heavily nerfed, but this penalty becomes weaker based on the spread power of your equipped Endoplasmic Reticulum.	Attack Level 2560
Queen of Swords	Resets Attack Level and sets Attack mults to 1, Game speed and other multipliers are logarithmed. Mult Gain is heavily nerfed, but this penalty becomes weaker based on your current Singularity Mult	Attack Level 3250
King of Swords	Requires 1.8e308 Atoms to enter. Arcana Challenges are much more unique as they're centered around the respective Arcan card. Each challenge provides a boost to each card, a boost to Sacrifice dust gain and turns it into a passive effect instead.

Artifacts

Tarot Artifacts (late-game)
Artifacts are unlocked through Refine Tree Node 99, purchased with 1e1000 RfP. There are four different artifacts, one for each card suit. Each Tarot resource can be converted into their golden form using the Flush button on the right side of the tab. Doing so will grant you the respective amount of golden resources, but a ^0.001 resource generation penalty will be incurred, though this will weaken gradually over 10 hours. The Flush button will also be on cooldown for 10% of this duration. This cooldown can be reduced with levels in Refine Tree Node 100, and Relic 58, among many other means.

Here are the four artifacts:

A complete Crystal Mirror artifact, for Swords
A complete Crystal Mirror artifact, for Swords
 
A complete Magic Skull artifact, for Wands
A complete Magic Skull artifact, for Wands
 
A complete Prosperity Engine artifact, for Pentacles
A complete Prosperity Engine artifact, for Pentacles
 
A complete Emotional Vessel artifact, for Cups
A complete Emotional Vessel artifact, for Cups
Parts
Each Artifact has many parts, each obtained by getting a certain amount of Golden Resources. Each part can also be upgraded with Golden Resources as well. Upgrading a part will NOT spend your resources, you only need the required amount to upgrade it.

Here is a list of all the Artifact parts:

Artifact Parts and their Effects
The cost scaling for these parts doesnt seem to follow any simple formula, so it is not listed here.

LOGPOW
Part 7 for each Artifact unlocks the LOGPOW stat for the respective suit, which, in effect, raises the exponent of the amount of resource you have to a certain power instead of affecting the entire value.

Later Tarot Challenges use a LOGPOW function for a significant penalty, but even slight increases in the LOGPOW stat can drastically weaken these penalties.

Trivia
When you get a level of a tarot card, for Suits, it increases the effect of it. For arcanas, it both reduces its cooldown and boosts its effect.

Tip: If you use a (DRAW ALL CARDS) block in your macro while in the Wheel Of Fortune challenge the challenge can’t be completed.

Tip: Use the Arcan specified in an Arcan Challenge in a seperate Macro.

Tip: Before Doing the Nine of Swords Challenge, beat Eight of Swords.

Plague

Work in Progress!

This page documents newly released content and may be incomplete. Additional information will be added as it becomes available.


The Plague tab
Plague is the 7th subtab in Unity, and can be unlocked through Refine Tree Node 103.


Contents
1	Infection
1.1	Stages
1.1.1	Immunity
1.1.2	Cure
1.2	Statistics
1.3	Naming
2	Endoplasmic Reticulum
2.1	Crafting
2.2	Dissolving and ER Upgrades
Infection
In order to release your plague, you must click the “INITIATE INFECTION” button at the bottom of the tab, which allows you to begin the infection. Your plague will spread slowly at first, based off of the “spread speed” number displayed.

Stages
There are 6 main levels, with the first 5 having 12 stages each, and the last one having one. Each stage has a certain number of people that must all be infected by your plague in order to complete that stage. Completing a stage will reward you Plague Generators, or PlG. Each PlG will produce a certain amount of Plague Points, or PlP, per second, which is shown by your PlP per PlG stat.

Plague Stages

Immunity
Each level will also have an Immunity stat, which basically slows down your plague the higher it is.

Immunity Stats
Level	Name	Immunity
1	Houses	1
2	Towns	2
3	Islands	4
4	Countries	10
5	Planets	160
6	Universes	1,000,000
Cure
After you reach Islands, the healthy population of your current Plague Stage will begin slowly working on a cure. Their progress will slow down the less healthy people remain, and after a certain point, the cure and all of their research will be destroyed by your plague. However, if the cure is completed, it will begin decreasing your spread speed, and may even make it go negative, in which case the sick people will be cured and will become healthy again.

Statistics
There are 6 different statistics, and each provide significant boosts.

Plague Statistics
Statistic	Boost
Max Stage Completed	Gold Gain ^
Max Infected	Tarot Resources Gain ^
Total Infected	Black Gem mult
Max PlP	Golden Resources Gain mult
Max PlG	Singularity mult
Max Infected in Universe	PlP per PlG mult
Naming
In the top left, where the animated viruses are, the player has an option to name their plague, and doing so will grant Achievement 529.

Endoplasmic Reticulum

Endoplasmic Reticulum section
Endoplasmic Reticula (ERs) are unlocked through Refine Tree Node 104. Here, you can craft ERs by flushing an amount of your current PlP, anywhere from 1% to 100% at a time.

Crafting
You generate Endoplastic Reticulum Points (ERP) based on your flushed PlP. This conversation rate starts at 1% (i.e. your ERP generation per second is equal to 1% of your flushed PlP). Once your ERP amount satisfies the requirement, you will receive a new ER at the cost of your ERP and flushed PlP. Each ER will have a rarity tier, type, and level. The level can be increased by simply increasing the craft level, though this increases the ERP requirement quite a lot and you need to have crafted an ER of the previous level.

There are 6 rarity tiers:

F: Trash
D: Common
C: Uncommon
B: Rare
A: Epic
S: Legendary
Increases in rarity will significantly boost the effectiveness of your ER.

Each ER also falls into one of 5 types:


This page is a stub. You can help Revolution Idle Wiki by expanding it.
Reason: Needs icons

ER Types
Icon	Type	Notes
Outbreak	Excellent Spread Power, but weaker Infectivity and Stealth.
Latent	Average Spread Power with exceptional Stealth
Virulent	Average Spread Power with very high Infectivity
Strain	Strong Spread Power with only slightly weaker secondary stats
Decay	Weak overall stats, but yields a high amount of VE when dissolved.
Dissolving and ER Upgrades
Unwanted ERs can be dissolved for Virus Essence, or VE. Similarly to how PlG generates PlP, VE also generates Virus Points, or ViP, which can be spent on ER upgrades.

There are 7 ER upgrades.

ER Upgrades
#	Name	Effect	Effect Scaling	Base Cost	Cost Scaling
1	Base Spread Power	<==	+ 0.025/level	1000	x2/level
2	Base Infectivity	<==	+ 0.02/level	1000	X2/level
3	Base Stealth	<==	+ 0.02/level	1000	x2/level
4	Conversion Rate	Increases PlP to ERP conversion rate			
5	Dissolve Efficiency	Increases base VE from dissolving ERs			
6	Crafting Mastery	Increases chances of crafting better ERs			
7	Essence Density	Increases ViP/s per VE

Guide:Minerals

This is a Guide page.
This page will walk you through a specific progression stage or game mechanic.
For relevant information about this mechanic, check out Minerals.


Contents
1	Minerals
1.1	Automations
1.1.1	Revolution page Automations
1.1.2	Infinity page Automations
1.1.3	Eternity Page Automations
1.2	Basics of Minerals
1.3	1337 Attacks to 1650 Attacks
1.4	1650 attacks to Polish Prestige
1.5	Polish to Lv.70 Mineral(WIP)
Minerals
You will unlock Minerals at 1337 attacks with achv 240, by now you should already be cycling between 2 builds, the Gold farm and GS build. These 2 builds will be used the most during your runs.

Game Speed:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Scorp	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc


Gold:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aqua	Aqua	Aqua	Libra	Scorp	Scorp	Canc	Aries	Canc	Aries	Aries	Aries


Automations
Revolution page Automations
Automate buyables colors: all on 100%

Automate Ascensions: all on

Automate Prestige: Min.P Gain(+): 0 | Min.P Gain(x): 1.01 | Min time(s):0

Automate Promotions: have all 4 Promotions set on lvl 1 and 0 s

Infinity page Automations
Automate infinity and Ip Adjuster: turned off

Autobuy Infinity Tree Points: turned on

Automate Generators: all on

Automate Stars: all on

Eternity Page Automations
Automate Eternity: off

Automate Slowdown: set on 10

Autobuy Animal Points: all 3 on

Autobuy Animals: on, all animals enabled

Autobuy Laboratory Upgrades: all 3 on

Research Point Redistribution: on, must be setted up like this: 0%:0%:0%:0%:0%:100% | aka only 6th, common exponent has Points.

Automate Supernova: on

Autobuy Dilation Tree Points: any number past 65 is fine.

Autobuy Dilation Upgrades: all 9 on

Basics of Minerals
Each minerals cost Gold, each mineral has a base cost, cost will fall based on the fall/per hour. Each mineral will produce VP/s, When you Merge 2 minerals of the same lv your new mineral will produce more VP/s. Each lvl at the beginning will produce x3 more VP/s than the last so its always best to merge asap. The starting price for lv1 mineral is e110 gold. The base(minimum) price will multiply by 10(at the beginning of minerals that is) per increase in mineral spawn level. For every mineral spawned,the price(NOT base price) will multiply by 100 (this rate is multiplied by 1.44 per lvl).The price falling per hr starts at /1000 before hitting the base price. The Minerals tab has its own local Game Speed (in the beginning it will have a conversion of ^0.1). VP production is affected by your local GS(Mineral tab GS)

You can buy 8 different upgrades with vp initially

Max Spawn lv
Row space
Columns space
Cost fall per hour
Luck mult
Quality mult
Gold mult
Common exponent mult
1337 Attacks to 1650 Attacks
When you first get to attacks get your first mineral asap, you will be switching between the gold and GS builds a lot. Get to the point where you can afford the next mineral.

When you can merge, do it asap, the boost in VP will help a ton. To merge two minerals simply drag one on top of another with the same level.

The whole gameplay loop at this point is just farm gold-> use GS to try for more mult and afford the next mineral-> Merge-> Repeat.

(note: GS build is not that powerfull as score build at upgrading attacks but it is enough for minerals you dont need score build unless stated otherwise Time Flux and Offline Flux help alot with this process)

!IMPORTANT! When buying VP upgrades ALWAYS PRIORITIZE MAX SPAWN LV. Never buy VP upgrades that have a cost above 1e12 (1T) until Relic 23 is unlocked (except for fall speed, should be the second priority).

(Optional) While making your VP grow you can get quality and luck boost. If you feel stuck, try upgrading your zodiacs when you get these boosts.

For your relics priority it will be the same as when you were getting to 1337 but have a large emphasis on the new relics (see Relics Priority in the Attacks Guide).

REMEMBER TO RAISE YOUR MAX SPAWN LV AND CHANGE IT TO THE HIGHEST YOU CAN (you can js spam 99)

For example:When you can get ~20 SpTg gold/unity, you can do 5 rounds and set the spawn level to 7, since level 7 mineral costs 100 SpTg gold.

1650 attacks to Polish Prestige
Your playthrough steps should be about the same here, keep swapping in between Gold and Game Speed with Zodiac Quality every now and then, but here we are going to have a major focus on the minerals and VP.

When you are focusing on minerals its important that you switch to the GS build, this way you can maximize your VP production and your cost fall rate to afford more minerals.

!IMPORTANT! While you're making progress you will unlock attacks prestige. Don't do it until you have some progress in polish prestige.

Before you get to Polish Prestige, some of your relics will reach level 301. Once a relic reaches level 301 you can sacrifice them, but you should wait until you have Polish Prestiged for the first time. After "Sacrifice" appears on some relics, the progress will speed up, from ~1800 to ~2000 quickly within a few hours.

When you sacrifice a relic you will have to gain the sacrificed levels back, 1 level for every 1,000 attack levels you gain, and 1 level for every 10 mineral levels of each mineral you spawn. After the first Polish Prestige you will be able to spawn minerals more often and this is a much quicker method to regain sacrificed levels than attack levels.

To unlock Polish Points you have to reach mineral level 30 so focus more on the GS build and merging minerals.

Polish to Lv.70 Mineral(WIP)
When you get here, you would have some relics ready for sacrificing. When you unlock polish you should immediately do it, using all PP you got to increase swords level.

After that, you can get to level 30 quite fast so you should farm PP by doing fast runs to mineral 30 until you can easily reach lv.35, so that you can get even MORE sword levels by fast polish runs to level 35, continue doing runs like that mainly focusing on sword, but dont forget about other weapons too.

Every mineral level gives more PP, every 5 mineral levels give even more PP, and mineral levels past 50 gives exponentially more PP (~2x and then scales per level), and increasing VP increases your mineral level, that's why sword is better than others.

meow meow meow something somthing wip no one change this

The last few levels (after lv.66~67) are very hard to push, but the only way here is brute force. That's because the price scaling on weapon level 50 is strong. That will take you a few days (or just use time flux no ones waiting that long). Keep autospawn on. After that, you unlock refining, a new mechanic.

Guide:Refinement

This is a Guide page.
This page will walk you through a specific progression stage or game mechanic.
For relevant information about this mechanic, check out Refinement.


Contents
1	Introduction
1.1	Automations
1.1.1	Revolution page Automations
1.1.2	Infinity page Automations
1.1.3	Eternity Page Automations
1.1.4	Unity Page Automations
2	Beginning of refinement (N1-18)
3	Middle of Refinement
4	End of Minerals update
5	Refine Macro
Introduction
You will unlock Refinement after obtaining a level 70 mineral.

Buy Node 1 and repeat a bunch of 1 RfP runs.

Refinement is a very long stage you will be stuck at 1 RfP per run for quite long time so do 70s runs and dont go more. Only when you will get to 80 and other minerals that /10 in reasonable amount of time you should go for it.(outdated)

After tarot update RfP gain was increased,so lv.72 mineral will give 2 RfP and it's worth to push it after RN4 lv2+.

Automations
Revolution page Automations
Automate buyable colors: all on 100%

Automate Ascensions: all on

Automate Prestige: Min.P Gain(+): 0 | Min.P Gain(x): 1.01 (If you want, enter 1 and it will automatically set to 1.01) | Min time(s):0

Automate Promotions: have all 4 Promotions set on lvl 1 and 0 s

Infinity page Automations
Automate infinity and Ip Adjuster: turned off

Autobuy Infinity Tree Points: turned on

Automate Generators: all on

Automate Stars: all on

Eternity Page Automations
Automate Eternity: off

Automate Slowdown: set on 10

Autobuy Animal Points: all 3 on

Autobuy Animals: on, all animals enabled

Autobuy Laboratory Upgrades: all 3 on

Research Point Redistribution: on, must be set up like this: 0%:0%:0%:0%:0%:100% | aka only 6th, common exponent has Points.

Automate Supernova: on

Autobuy Dilation Tree Points: any number past 65 is fine.

Autobuy Dilation Upgrades: all 9 on

Unity Page Automations
You will unlock them along the way while completing refinement. Auto sell Zodiacs/Auto Sacrifice Zodiacs + Automate Unity: whenever you are going to switch loadout, you can set the Auto Zodiac Sell/Auto Sacrifice Zodiacs auto sell/sac all zodiacs but the ones you need for the next loadout, for Auto unity you can set it wait until it reaches the max reachable attack level to maximise the zodiac stats + you can set a priority for what zodiacs the auto unity to aim for.

Autobuy Mineral Upgrades: Everything on

Automerge Minerals: on

Automate Attack Buyables: all on, all on 100%

Automate Attack Ascensions: all on

AutoBuy Polish Upgrades: all on

Autoset Mineral Spawn Level: on

Beginning of refinement (N1-18)
The most important nodes in the beginning of refinement are: 1, 4, 10 they boost your vp gain a lot and MUST be upgraded anytime you have enough / or you can even push more to get them.


here you can see where beginer nodes are placed
You can upgrade nodes based of their numbers, but some skips can be done.

While upgrading refinement tree, you also can get other polish milestones, and you will see that you did unlock insane trials: {Guide: Insane Trials}.

(nodes that are not mentioned below are slight buffs or automations)

Node 1 is a very good buff to VP.
Node 2 helps you get to max attack lvl fast.
Node 4 is a good buff to VP.
Node 5 unlocks merge level. Upgrading it boosts number below the grid which boosts your VP.
Node 6 unlocks automerge. If you prefer an afk gamestyle, this node is good help.
Node 7 is a big boost to Gold.
Node 10 boosts PP gain which can get you more polish levels.
Node 12 - after buying it you don't spend VP on VP upgrades, which means VP amount doesn't go down after buying.
Be wary! Node 16 is a quite low buff to VP. Upgrade it only if you have spare RFP.

Node 18 is a pinnacle of progression because it unlocks magnets, a new powerful mechanic.
Middle of Refinement
How magnets work: you get magnets when you merge a mineral. Total chance of getting magnet on spawn can be seen on VP upgrade (image on the right).

Magnet chance.png
Magnets unlock very powerful upgrades. The best of them are: VP buff, PP buff, and more magnets.

At this point progression becomes quite hard you MUST get Node 4 and Node 10 to their max levels before proceeding further.

Your Progression is tightly correlated with merge lvl you need it to get more vp and higher spawn; getting merge lvl and magnets is the main way of upgrading in middle and late Refinement.

Get more polish milestones if you see that you're close. Try to push them because every milestone have its purpose.

Best PP milestones to get for now is Sword 100 and Spear 25.

After you get max Node 4 and max Node 10 you must progress further in Refine tree until Node 24.

n21 worth mentioning. It gives you not gold/s it increases the amount of gold you will get after unity based on how much you got for last attack lvl.

Example: for last attack lvl you got +4 to gold with n21 you will get +4 to gold after unity per second.

After unlocking n24 you should upgrade it. Basically, n24 is a VP buff, it is a little bit worse than n1 but if you can't buy n1, n24 is your top priority.

Before proceeding further, you should at least complete 3 IT(you can actually complete 4 if you upgrade n24 enough)

I recommend getting 5 lvls in n24 before upgrading anything else.

There is 2 ways to go now: more afk way and active.

Afk route: upgrade n27 (right after n26). N27 will allow you to spawn higher Minerals (after mineral 124 gold cost of spawn is massively increased) you can get 127 or even 128 spawn using it. Therefore, you can afk for higher mineral and get more RFP.

Active route: upgrade n29 (right after n25). N29 boost your VP A LOT. After buying n29, n24 becomes very powerful so you should prioritize it instead of n1.

in active route, after getting n29, start doing fast m130 runs it will be a lot faster than doing 140 runs.

Regardless of the way you went you need both n27 and n29.

Then you have both n27 and n29 you can make fast runs to m140 that's the main way to get RFP.

Don't forget about milestones spear lvl 100 is a huge buff to your gold.

After getting both nodes you should go to n34(after n29). It makes you not spend any magnets on upgrades. And unlocks magnets auto buy.

You should unlock n31(after N28) its buff allows you to spawn higher Minerals.

(N28 unlocks polish enhance while it seems tempting to buy it, you shouldnt for now, so buy when the cost is pretty low, for now at least)

If you prefer afk A LOT more, go for n36 but its cost is big and buff is not that great.

After getting n31 you should go for n38, path is n33-n37-n38. N33 will give you gold you would get on unity every second. N38 gives you a lot of rfp while in game description may confuse you here is how it works. Your rfp is multiplied by 1.01^(knuckles lvl). At lvl 80 (around this lvl you would get n38) your rfp will be multiplied by 2.21.

End of Minerals update
As you could see there is n35 which unlocks runes more detailed guide: [Guide:Runes and Special Minerals] . But for now, all you must know is that runes are pure afk.

Going for runes generation is considered a good move but some tests say that upgrading moon n7 buff at least 2 times is very powerfull.

Late tree progression is tightly correlated with IT.

Buy N30 it helps you get more gold.

The main progression in late tree is n40, n36 and n24. Upgrade them as much as you can.

You MUST go for n45 path is n39-n44(worthless node, upgrade with spare points after spm unlock)-n45.

N45 boost your PP generation and while you didn't complete IT5 it isn't very good but using it you can get to IT5 milestone easily.

After completing IT5, n45 buff becomes significant using it you can farm higher Minerals.

You can buy n50(after n32) wich boost your rfp but it costs a lot.

Basically, all you do now is MAXING n24, n36, n40 after maximizing them you can go for IT6.

After IT6 you should unlock spm.

Later you will unlock elements and other buffs. (for more detailed info: Guide:Elements)

Refine Macro
Usage:

Adjust PPAfterPrestige thresholds to Polish levels you can achieve quickly. (33e186 is Spear 110)
Adjust MaxMineralLevelThisRun to your target Mineral level to optimize RfP Gain. (m130, m140, etc.)
WaitForSeconds(1.0)
PolishPrestige()
WaitUntil (PPAfterPrestige >= 1.00e174)
PolishPrestige()
WaitUntil (PPAfterPrestige >= 33.0e186)
PolishPrestige()
WaitUntil (MaxMineralLevelThisRun >= 170)
RefinePrestige()

Guide:Insane Trials

This is a Guide page.
This page will walk you through a specific progression stage or game mechanic.



If you want to see the Easy–Medium–Hard Trials, here’s the link: Guide:Trials

These Trials work differently compared to previous ones, as they must be completed in order. To unlock an Insane Trial, you must reach the required level for that trial on your Weapons through Polish Prestige, the required Weapons levels can be found under Unity → Minerals, and you must obtain a Level 30 mineral to unlock Weapon polishing itself.


Contents
1	Insane Trials Information
1.1	Insane Trial 1
1.2	Insane Trial 2
1.3	Insane Trial 3
1.4	Insane Trial 4
1.5	6th Difficulty Eternity Challenges
1.6	Insane Trial 5
1.7	Insane Trial 6
Insane Trials Information
Fun fact, they are called Insane Trials not because they are insanely hard, but because you would be insane by the time you unlock them!

Trial	Unlock	Goal	Handicap	Reward
Insane Trial 1	Level 25 Knuckles	Buy 65 DTP	First DU is useless and dilation penalties are ^2 stronger.	You get 10 more EC from trials
Insane Trial 2	Level 50 Spear	Unite with e5.8M+ score	Common Exponent is divided by 1M	Now you keep all of your EC on Unity Reset. Trials reward is changed to PP gain mult.
Insane Trial 3	Level 50 Knuckles	Reach Unity	IP and EP gains are much worse now. DP Gain is multiplied by Min(1, Eternities amount). Reset eternites amount to 0 on Unity Reset.	Get additional 100% of IP and EP per second passively
Insane Trial 4	Level 100 Axe	Reach 2.018e705+ EP and 8.14e2025+ IP	All EC rewards are much worse now and actually are penalties.	You can complete 1 more difficulty for each EC. EC past 50 boost your magnet gain chance
Insane Trial 5	Level 100 Bow	Reach 65 DTP	DU1 is disabled. Dilation Penalties and DP gain scales much harsher.	Dilation's best score is automatically set to your current score raised to DU Power Penalty
Insane Trial 6	Level 100 Knuckles	Reach Stage 100	Attack Divider and Level Scalings are harsher, R13 is inverted, Gold and Ascend Strength is logarithmed. Attack Stats are wiped on Unity.	Passively gain 1 Unity per second, multiplied by Minerals' Game Speed
From now on, you will need an exact Zodiac loadout, not just a few specific Zodiacs placed in certain positions like before.

You must set up the Zodiacs in the exact order they are unlocked, following their order of unlocking them.

If stuck before eternity:

- Make sure you have Sacrificed a full set of Zodiacs recently, yes, all 12 of them.

- Your current Mineral run should be close to your maximum Value Points.

- If getting past the first red circle is not the issue in insane trial 4, you may be able to replace a Leo with a Taurus.

- Ensure all your Research Points are fully used by Laboratory 6.

- Make sure that your macro isn’t stuck in an Infinity or Eternity Challenge, and that no other Trials are currently active.

Insane Trial 1
You must have at least e100k Multi Gain Sacrifice. The required Zodiac Loadout you need to use is:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
aqua	Aries	aqua	libra	aqua	libra	Leo	Aries	Leo	Aries	Aries	Aries


Insane Trial 2
Use the same loadout as Insane Trial 1, but you should also have e2,000,000 Multi Gain Sacrifice.


Alternative loadout, could work if your sacrifice stat is low:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aries	Aries	Aries	libra	libra	libra	libra	Aries	Aries	Aries	Aries	Aries


Insane Trial 3
You must have e5,000,000 Multi Gain Sacrifice, e14 Quality on each Zodiac (100 trillion in standard notation), and a Common Exponent Boost of 1.20 from the Minerals tab.

Standard Zodiac Loadout:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
libra	libra	libra	libra	libra	libra	aqua	tau	tau	tau	tau	tau


Alternative Loadouts for Insane Trial 3:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
libra	libra	libra	libra	libra	libra	libra	tau	tau	tau	tau	tau


Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
libra	libra	libra	libra	libra	Gem	Tau	tau	tau	tau	tau	tau


If EP is stuck at 1e2462-2465, enable auto eternity at EP 1, min time 0 to get the last EP required to Unite.

Insane Trial 4
You must have a minimum of e18 Quality on each Zodiac (1 quintillion in standard notation), though e19+ Quality is preferable (10 quintillion in standard notation).

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
gem	Aries	tau	tau	gem	gem	Leo	Aries	Leo	tau	Aries	tau

Alternative builds:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
gem	gem	gem	tau	tau	tau	Leo	Aries	Leo	tau	tau	tau


Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
leo	gem	gem	tau	tau	tau	Leo	Aries	Leo	tau	tau	tau


6th Difficulty Eternity Challenges
Eternity Challenge 1-6 is quite hard, but fortunately, we have this build:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
sagit	leo	leo	libra	leo	leo	Leo	Leo	Sagit	aries	leo	leo


Alternative build for EC 1-6:



Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo



Eternity Challenges 2–6 to 10-6 are very easy and can be completed with any score build. Here’s an example of a score build:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
sagit	aries	sagit	libra	leo	libra	Leo	aries	Leo	aries	aries	aries


Insane Trial 5
The Challenge is very easy, just use a full cancer build.

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc	canc
No cost too great.

If the cost doesn't exist.

Insane Trial 6
BE CAREFUL, do not Refine before completing Insane Trial 6, you need to have over e1025 Value Points and atleast 1e20+ Qualty (5e20 is preferrable).

Use a basic score build. If the Challenge doesn’t complete itself in seconds, get more Value Points or improve your Sacrifices/Zodiacs.

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
sagit	aries	sagit	libra	leo	libra	Leo	aries	Leo	aries	aries	aries

Guide:Runes and Special Minerals

This is a Guide page.
This page will walk you through a specific progression stage or game mechanic.
For relevant information about this mechanic, check out Runes.

Runes
You unlock runes after Refine Node 35.

Best rune is a moon rune, its buffs are better than sun one.

Your first runes should be gained from Secret #038 before waiting for that slow progress bar to fill.These 2 runes of each type can help you progress faster.

You can decrease rune generation time using RFP. Buy it as soon as you can!

Best upgrade in both sun and moon is generation rate. It basically increases amount of runes you get per cycle.

At the beginning of runes buying 2 moon n7 upgrades is a good move, buying it you boost gold and max spawn by A LOT.

You should use your sun runes to upgrade refine n1. You can upgrade magnets but only if you have spare sun.

Moon runes must be used on n7 upgrade. Buy some magnet upgrade if it is cheap enough.

There is n47 and n48 they decrease the time to generate rune.

When you can get hundreds of billions of RfP per refine,you can push for "Full network(before next update)".This is another achievement that if done at the start of rune will speed rune progress a lot.

In this way,you can get Elements at the beginning of SpM.

SpM
SpM - special Minerals. They unlock with n41 buying spm costs 10 sun and 10 moon runes. After unlocking spm it is recommended to NOT buy any special minerals until you reach Generation Rate Amount(GRA) 5 for both Sun and Moon Runes. This should be your priority and it will take a long time to reach. While leveling your Rune GRA its recommended to keep your Moon Rune upgrade for N7 to half the cost of your GRA upgrade.

Once you reach the GRA 5 upgrade for both Sun and Moon runes you can start spawning spm. The recommended upgrades from here are:



-Buy GRA 5

-Obtain lvl 6 white gem/300 spm spawn factor

-Buy GRA 6

-Obtain lvl 7 White gem/650 spm spawn factor

-Buy GRA 7

-Buy spm until reaching Immortal+1000 zodiacs/1100 spawn factor



If you're having a hard time reaching Immortal+1000 zodiacs, try increasing spm amount or Wind element.

Once you reach Ethereal zodiacs your progress should sky rocket.


Buying spm increase its own base cost, aka the more you buy the more it cost.

Spm cost decrease over time same as common Minerals but spm also permanently increase its own cost the more you buy it.

Best spm you can get is white, yellow, red. (purple and light blue are good too but not that powerfull)

All spm related achievments are pure luck and time based.

After getting your first specials buy n46 to get SMP(special minerals progression).

To increase base in SMP requires you to upgrade 1 of your spm to base 2. to get it you must merge spm of the correlated color. you need 104 spm of same color.

While you collect spm it is a wise move to farm RFP/upgrade runes generation, n1 buff and n7 buff.

By upgrading you will eventually get to n55 and unlock elements. Guide:Elements

General path of progression
You unlock Elements by building Refine Node 55.

You can get every element at the beginning except for water, because it needs a lot of luck. You should firstly get as much VP as you can upgrade your wind for quite a lot until you get wind 3 node and don't forget about water loadout.

Every time you can buy something in the Elements Tree, you should buy it, unless you're trying to buy 1 million upgrades. If you are, you should wait for 2-3 millions before buying. Whenever you buy anything, you should also get back the amount you lose from buying it.

A lot of upgrades in the Elements Tree scale with the amount of the respective element you have, that's why buying an upgrade and then getting back the elements is the core of progression.

With Elements Tree upgrades, you can get a lot higher tier Zodiacs. Don’t forget to check if you can get stronger Zodiacs, and if you can, always change them and sacrifice them.

The best way to get higher Zodiacs is the 12 Pisces build, just use them and set Auto Unity to your max Pisces Attack Level and max Pisces Zodiac Level.

Don’t forget to buy relics and Refine Nodes while you wait.

If you feel stuck, try upgrading your:

Runes
Polish Enhance
SPM progression
Zodiacs
Relics
Refine Tree Nodes
Making more Earth for gold
Making more Water for higher luck
Making more Wind for higher Water → higher luck
Making more Fire for higher quality and attacks
When you get 3 × +1000 Immortal Zodiacs, you MUST merge them into an Ethereal Zodiac. They provide an enormous boost and you MUST change all your Zodiacs and sacrifice all of them. Use an Ethereal Zodiac loadout for Fire.

Ethereal Zodiacs have +_rarities too, so sometimes check if you can get a higher rarity Zodiac, If you can, upgrade all Zodiacs and sacrifice all Zodiacs again.

Also keep in mind, to actually reach tarots you need a minimum of base 3 of all special minerals, push your Generation Amount for all of them, buy the upgrades offer you and keep pushing them until the spawn factor is 7500, after that the cost will be horrible.

With that info, you should proceed until Node 70 and unlock Tarot.

Automations
Revolution page Automations
Automate buyables colors: all on 100%

Automate Ascensions: all on

Automate Prestige: Min.P Gain (+): 0 | Min.P Gain (x): 1.01 | Min time (s):0

Automate Promotions: have all 4 Promotions set on lvl 1 and 0 s

Infinity page Automations
Automate infinity and Ip Adjuster: turned off

Autobuy Infinity Tree Points: turned on

Automate Generators: all on

Automate Stars: all on

Eternity Page Automations
Automate Eternity: off

Automate Slowdown: set on 10

Autobuy Animal Points: all 3 on

Autobuy Animals: on, all animals enabled

Autobuy Laboratory Upgrades: all 3 on

Research Point Redistribution: on, must be setted up like this: 0%:0%:0%:0%:0%:100% | aka only 6th, common exponent has Points.

Automate Supernova: on

Autobuy Dilation Tree Points: any number past 65 is fine.

Autobuy Dilation Upgrades: all 9 on

Unity Page Automations
Auto sell Zodiacs/Auto Sacrifice Zodiacs + Automate Unity: whenever you are going to switch loadout, you can set the Auto Zodiac Sell/Auto Sacrifice Zodiacs auto sell/sac all zodiacs but the ones you need for the next loadout, remember once you unlock ethereal to enable ethereal selling as by default its turned off, for Auto unity you can set it wait until it reaches the max reachable attack level to maximize the zodiac stats + you can set a priority for what zodiacs the auto unity to aim for.

Autobuy Mineral Upgrades: Everything on

Automerge Minerals: on

Automate Attack Buyables: all on, all on 100%

Automate Attack Ascensions: all on

Autobuy Polish Upgrades: all on

Autoset Mineral Spawn Level: on

Autobuy Relics: on, enable all relics to be on

Autospawn Special Minerals: on, set Max cost(+base) to 0, it will make the autobuy wait until the cost falls to base, disable it when you want to buy rune upgrades.

Automerge Special Minerals: all on

Autodelete Minerals: preferably Max spawn (-from current mineral level) set to 2.

The Recycle Method (main game loop)
The gameplay loop of Elements is pretty simple, and here you will see a step-by-step explanation of the entire process.

Global Setup
When creating Pisces-based builds, set automation to:
Highest Zodiac Level
Highest Attack Level
Early elements may show no gains before ~1M points.
If an Elemental Node is purchased, re-farm that element.
Rebuild Pisces only if improvement thresholds are met.
Element Scaling
Fire → Score → Pisces Quality
Earth → Infinities
Wind → Eternities
Water → Luck
Optional Farm Phase
Run only when explicitly called.

Source: Pisces Loadout

Farm Astrodust (10–30 minutes)
Auto-Sacrifice (5–10 minutes; once per +1 Ethereal or +100 Immortal)
Buy / sacrifice Relics (~10 minutes)
Check Refinement (nodes, minerals, polish, runes)
Phase 0 — Pisces Baseline
Build: Full Pisces

Recycle only if Quality or Rarity improves
If Zodiac strength increases after recycling, recycle once more (rare)
Phase 1 — Fire
Build: Fire Loadout

Using: Full Pisces Loadout
Farm: Fire Elements using new Fire Loadout

Time: 10–60 minutes
Watch: Score, Relics, Attack Prestige
Buy Elemental Nodes if available; re-farm Fire to regain loss
Stop:

When gains to Score and Fire slow
After:

Run Optional Farm Phase if useful
Rebuild Pisces if Quality improved
Phase 2 — Earth
Build: Earth Loadout

Using: Full Pisces Loadout
Farm: Earth Elements using new Earth Loadout

Time: 10–60 minutes
Watch: Relic upgrades
Buy Elemental Nodes if available; re-farm Earth to regain loss
Stop:

When gains to Relics and Earth slow
After:

Run Optional Farm Phase if useful
Rebuild Pisces if thresholds are met
Phase 3 — Wind
Build: Wind Loadout

Using: Full Pisces Loadout
Farm: Wind Elements using new Wind Loadout

Time: 10–60 minutes
Watch: Eternities
Wind may show little or no gains
Buy Elemental Nodes if available; re-farm Wind to regain loss
Stop:

When Wind gains slow
After:

Run Optional Farm Phase if useful
Rebuild Pisces if thresholds are met
Phase 4 — Water
Prerequisite:

Avoid Water until Wind Node 3 is unlocked
Build: Water Loadout

Using: Full Pisces Loadout
Farm: Water Elements using new Water Loadout

Time: 10–60 minutes
Watch: Overall stat gains
Buy Elemental Nodes if available; re-farm Water to regain loss
Stop:

When overall gains slow
After:

Run Optional Farm Phase if useful
Rebuild Pisces if thresholds are met
Loop
Repeat from Phase 0 — Pisces Baseline.

What Each Elemental Loadout Will Boost
Fire - by increasing your score you get more fire/sec
Boosts relic 26 (quality donut)
Asc power Increase based on VP
Atk lvl past 3600 boosts Fire gen.
Boosts relic 31 (Mineral Cost Lowered)
Earth - by increasing your amount of infinities you get more earth/sec
Boosts RN 1 (Increase Mineral Base)
Boosts gold gain
Boost relic 32 (PP gain)
Wind - by increasing your amount of eternities you get more wind/sec
SPM cost decrease
PP past e308 boost wind gen.
Boosts relic 25 (Luck donut) - helps start Water
Boosts relic 33 (RfP gain)
Water - by increasing your luck you get more water/sec
Boosts RN 18 (magnet chance)
Boosts relic 24 (Game Speed donut)
Boosts relic 34 (Rune Speed)
You should try getting elements in order they are placed.

Use the respective build for every element, they are listed below.

Zodiac Loadouts
These loadouts are made to help in progression during the element grind stage of the game and it is highly recommended to use these loadouts.

To import a Planet Loadout, copy the code; head to Zodiac page, press the 3 dots and 3 lines next to the inventory, choose a Loadout, press the 3 lines and press import.

How to get Draws
You need to throw away Black Gems to get Card Draw Progress. Once you reach 1 total draw amount, you can start receiving cards. Your first 5 cards are always predetermined.

(After that, Card 6 and onward become random.)

Important: It’s recommended to let your Black Gem reach a decent level (around 5 or 6) before you start throwing them away.

Do not throw away level 1 Black Gems. This allows Special Minerals Experience to build up on them, and having a higher base level is very valuable here.

P.S.: Before any effects are applied to Black Gems, they give this amount of Tarot Draws per trashed Black Gem level:

Level 1: 0.10
Level 2: 0.20
Level 3: 0.40
Level 4: 0.80
Level 5: 1.60
Level 6: 3.20
And it continues by doubling for higher levels.

How to Reduce Your Special Minerals Base Cost
The Special Minerals Base Cost is based on your spawn factor, which is how many Special Minerals you have spawned. After SMS factor 7500, the exponential cost becomes extremely high, so you need to reduce it as soon as possible.

To start reducing it, you must have Refine Node 68. This node lowers the SPM factor by 1 for every 100,000 attacks. With Auto Unity on, that takes roughly 18 seconds of Unscaled Playtime per 1 SMS factor reduction.

You can lower this time by going to the Time Flux tab and setting the game speed to 3×. This makes each Unity take 0.333 seconds instead of 1, resulting in about 6 seconds per 1 SMS factor reduction.

Make sure you have Auto-Spawn Special Minerals enabled, and set Max Spawned Factor to at least 7500 so you don’t get hit by the exponential cost shenanigans.

What to do if your SpM grid is full
First, Throw away low level Green gems, White Gems and Blue Gems. They don't do very much for you at this point.

It's safe to throw away any of them that are decently lower than your highest ones. like if you have 14, throw away 9 and lower for all 3 Special gems.

Warning!
Using Tower regularly can cause high punishment if it eats your high lvl SpM, while you will need to use it eventually, don’t use it recklessly.

The general path of waiting progression
When you unlock Tarots, your SMS factor is set to 5000 (or –1000 if you had less than 6000). Using that reduction, you should spawn Special Minerals until SMS reaches 7500 and hope for Black Special Minerals.

You need to get your first 4 Tarots, because as an achievement reward you will receive the Chariot Major Arcana card.

To get enough for 4 Tarot spawns, you need a level 6 + level 4 Black Special Minerals. You shouldn’t Trash Specials Minerals before you have enough for 4 draws, because you would lose experience for level 2 base (Special Mineral Progression).

(But realistically, most players will want to get Tarots earlier. It is acceptable to do that, your progress won’t be much slower as long as you delete Special Minerals only when it's at least level 5.)

After struggling waiting long enough, you will get the Chariot Arcana, it decreases your SMS factor by 1000 every 12 hours (affected by Tarot local game speed).

All you have to do now is use Chariot as soon as its cooldown reaches 0. Whenever it becomes available, use it and then (hopefully) spawn more Black Special Minerals.

IMPORTANT: Don’t forget about Node 68.

Reducing your SMS factor using n68 + 3× Time Flux becomes even better now, because you also reduce the Chariot cooldown while doing it.

Now you have a clear idea of how to get more tarots, you should get your Black Special Mineral to level 2 base (Special Mineral Progression), because it is very powerful with Black Special Minerals.

For now you must continue spawning tarots.

All Tarots give buffs to earlier game stages, so if you get a new Tarot or upgrade an existing one, you should upgrade the related stat.

For example: Ace of Swords gives you +^1000 to attack multiplier gain from VP, so you should upgrade Attack Prestige.

Keep spawning more Tarots to earn more achievements until you get 1 million of each resource (shown at the top of the Tarot page).

If you get the Judgement Major Arcana, you can speed up the resource waiting time if your minor arcana cards didn't draw equally.

If you get the Tower Major Arcana, you can loose your time significantly if you use it just for fun. DO NOT USE TOWER until everything left on board is lvl 17 SpM except for black, black must be lvl12. If you just randomly use it you can stop your progress for quite some time.

If you get the Devil Major Arcana, you can speed up the resource waiting time significantly, you need to use it whenever possible the only true negative debuff is addiction(+66 SMS) other than what is minor debuffs or even buffs at some circumstances.

After getting 1 million of each resource, you unlock Tarot Upgrades.

You need to buy every first upgrade, because they drastically increase Tarot local game speed.

After that all you have to do is just wait for chariot/n68 and buy more tarots: hoping for devil to upgrade your resource gain and buying new tarot upgrades / getting new minor cards (especially) kings to massively boost elements.

Base 5 is very hard to get, if you don't have it for all basic(except black) SpM you can get it by sacrificing lvl 17 SpM to tower. To get base 4 for black SpM you need lvl 12 black SpM sacrificed to tower.

Each King Unlocks you the Second factor for each elements, that means that now there are 2 factors that boosts your Element gain.

Some loadouts become different once you unlock their Second Factor, re-check the Elemental Loadouts below once you have unlocked Second Factor.

After Unlocking the Second Factor for Earth element, put all your RP into lab 5 ONLY when using the Earth loadout for Earth element.

At this point we assume you already got full 78 cards deck.

Now you need to continue pushing your cards with more lvls of Chariot getting new cards became easy.

Your main gameplay loop now is using devil/fool/chariot-hermit/upgrade zodiacs/upgrade elements.

One of the best upgrades you can get is Wind node 10(e800) it massively increases your draws.

Refine node 79 is also very powerful.

After getting SpM to lvl20 you should use tower method again to increase your base. You can get base 6 without relic 45 and even more with it. (lvl 20 common SpM and lvl 16 black)

With this info you should be able to get to ach 425.

Arcans
Tierlist

Tier	Arcanes
S	Fool, Chariot, Hermit, Death, The Sun, The Devil
A	Strength, The Star, The Emperor, The Hierophant, The Lovers
B	The Magician, The High Priestess, The world, Empress
Garbage	The Moon, Wheel of Fortune, Justice, Temperance, The Hanged Man
Specific	The Tower, Judgement
Major arcana combinations
Star and after that Magician → boosts Water element after gaining more Luck through Star.
Lovers + High Priestess → to get more VP than using them alone.
Hermit and after that any Major Arcana → to get more buffs.
Strength + Fire build → gives a lot more Attack levels.
Star and after that Temperance → to get stronger Pisces.
The World and after that any Major Arcana → reduces cooldowns.
Star and after that Empress → gives A LOT of astrodust use it push your donuts.
How all tarots work and best way to use them
Terminologies:

Cyclical: An effect type where a value is first increased, then decreased by the displayed value over an even amount of time.

0 - The Fool
Effect: Spawn a level Smp lvl +1 Black Gem on your Special Mineral Grid. Your grid must have free space to use this.

Base CD: 3h

A straightfoward Arcana, it lets you periodically spawn a black SM for free, with no downsides. Just remember to use it when it's up.

I - The Magician
Effect: Gain Elemental Charge of the currently selected element equal to X seconds of production.

Base CD: 4h?

Basically a time skip to elemental gain for your element, but is best used for Water while The Star is active to massively jump its charge amount.

II - The High Priestess
Effect: Multiply Refine Node 1 (Mineral Base) by X for Y seconds.

Base CD: 2h

Boosts VP, but not that significant of a boost later on.

III - The Empress
Effect: Cyclically increase and decrease Astrodust (Currency for Donuts) Multiplier by X for 1 minute.

Base CD: 4h

Useful for donut pushing, although a bit limited as the increase period is only up for ~45s before dropping lower than average.

IV - The Emperor
Effect: Cyclically increase and decrease Mineral Cost exponent by X for 30 seconds.

Base CD: 4h

Drastically reduces common mineral costs while on its windup, but completely useless otherwise if you are VP capped instead.

V - The Hierophant
Effect: Immediately add X levels to all Common Minerals on your grid. A mineral cannot go above a cap, which is equal to [Max Spawn Level + 50]

Base CD: 7h

Mainly used to gain RFP from the very high minerals you level with the effect, albeit slowly. Best for long RFP runs.

VI - The Lovers
Effect: Cyclically increase and decrease R28's effect (Merge Level Multi) by X for Y seconds.

Base CD: 3h

A quick and easy way to gain Merge Levels for Relic 3 (from RN30, which boosts Zodiac Level to Damage Mult conversion) and VP.

VII - The Chariot
(Guaranteed as your 5th Tarot Card)

Effect: Immediately reduce your SM Spawn Factor by X.

Base CD: 12h

The bread and butter of Tarots. Always use it whenever it's up as it's the core tarot for progression.

VIII - Justice
Effect: Set the cost of spawning SMs to 0 for X seconds

Base CD:  3:30h

A double-edged tarot that allows spawning SMs for free, but still causes Spawn Factor to rise. (use it right after chariot or for ach385, other than that it shouldn’t be used)

IX - The Hermit
Effect: Multiply the effect of all Major Arcana by X for Y seconds.

Base CD: 9h

A boost to all Arcana effects, always use this first before any other Arcana card to get the most out of this.

X - Wheel of Fortune
Effect: Flip a coin. If heads, gain X draws. If tails, lose X draws. You must have a positive non-zero amount of draws to use this.

Base CD: 30m

Gamba. That's it. (really painful for some unlucky individuals, use it with the awareness of the fact you can either lose 11 times in row or win 11 times in row, whatsoever, the chance is 50/50)

XI - The Strength
Effect: Cyclically increase and decrease your Attack Exponent by X for Y seconds.

Base CD: 5h

Lets you squeeze in extra attack stages during its increase wind-up.

XII - The Hanged Man
Effect: Temporarily increase Knuckles' effect equal to adding X levels for 30 seconds,

Base CD: 5:30h

Gives a small speed boost, but due to the Mineral Speed and Knuckle effect softcaps, the effect is not that strong later on.

XIII - Death
Effect: Gain an X RFP boost, at the cost of randomly triggering refines for 1 minute.

Base CD: 3h

Best used after a long run while casting Hierophant, multiplying the RFP gain from the high mineral you've leveled even more.

XIV - Temperance
Effect: Gain X copies of the zodiac equipped in Fortune Pars, but with stats equal to the ones on Unite.

Base CD: 2h

Best used while The Star is up to immediately print copies of a zodiac while the luck multiplier is still up, preferably printing Pisces.

XV - The Devil
Effect: Gain an effect based on the odds below:

66% chance - Permanently increase all Minor Arcana's effects by X

33% chance - Apply one random debuff listed in Help > Unity > The Devil

Shadow Self: Reduces game speed to ^0.666 for 66 seconds
Attachment: Removes 6.66% of your Tarot resources and draws
Addiction: Adds 66 to SMS factor
Restriction: Prevents using Arcana cards for 6 minutes
Secret Garden: Displays a moving red-shaded chadium on your screen for 66 seconds
1% chance - Lose 1 Soul, gain SA49 (only triggers once)

Base CD: 11:06min (666s)

A tarot card that allows permanent effect scaling to all Minor Arcana, but requires long investment for it to be noticeable. The only debuff to look out for is Addiction (+66 SM Spawn Factor, other 3 is manageable , and one is a buff if you have negative draws

XVI - The Tower
Effect: Destroy a random SM on your grid, then grant the destroyed color SMP EXP equal to [(X^Gem LVL)*R45 Effect].

Base CD: 8h

A powerful card that massively gives SMP EXP, but comes with a cost of potentially causing slowdowns if it hits a high value gem.

(Most reliable way to use it is deleting whole board and leaving just high gems so tower would guarantee hit it. Do it only if you sure you will get enough SMP to next base)

XVII - The Star
Effect: Gain a Luck Multiplier equal to [Infinity Stars^X] for 1 minute.

Base CD: 2:30h

An arcana meant for pushing zodiacs and Water, due to its massive Luck boost. May cause issues to zodiacs past e1000 Luck.

XVIII - The Moon
Effect: Multiply the quality of the equipped zodiac on the Moon by X, reduced if it's past e1000 quality.

Base CD: 4h

The quality boost is far too weak compared to its counterpart, the Star, especially considering it only boosts one zodiac. (use every time you can but it wont be very powerful)

XIX - The Sun
Effect: Grant a random tarot card you own X levels.,

Base CD: 10h

Grants free levels to a card, with no downsides. Use as often.

XX - Judgement
Effect: Averages the amounts between all 4 types, then raise the amount to X.

Base CD: 10h

Mostly used to pick-up Tarot Upgrades sooner if one resource is generating far more than the other types.

XXI - The World
Effect: Reduce the cooldown of all Major Arcana by X seconds.

Base CD: 24h

A late scaling tarot that eventually allows recasting of important Tarots as its level increases.

Earth Loadout After Getting the Card "King Of Wands" Import to import the Planet Loadout, copy the code below, head to Zodiac page, press the 3 dots and 3 lines next to the inventory, choose a Loadout, press the 3 lines and press import.
