Introduction
You unlock tarot challenges after buying Refine Node (Rn) : 89.

To get there you need to farm more gold, wich can be done by getting more yellow SpM. Gettin a lot more gold will increace your relic levels, wich will give you a lot more Rfp.

The Road to Refine Node 89 (Tarot Challenges)
Firstly, you'll need to grind your Attack Levels to 10000 for Relic 46, which provides a huge boost to SMP EXP gains. Then, using The Tower for even faster progression, you can get your Special Minerals to Base Level 17 or more, allowing you to spawn Level 20 gems very quickly (with the loss of time flux, mass producing Level 20 gems is much more difficult than before. Fortunately, you can likely push up to RfP 1e150 with only Base Level 9 gems). This should give enough resources to push your RfP up to 1e150, which shouldn't take too long. Then, you can easily unlock Refine Node 85.

Refine Node 85 unlocks Special Mineral Sacrifice, which gives a massive boost to your progression. You must destroy lvl 20 SpM (by trashing it or using The Tower) to get 1 sacrifice dust automatically contributed to the respective Special mineral color. Once a mineral has been sacrificed, a level 27 version of that mineral will be granted within the Sacrifice menu, and that gem color can no longer be spawned anymore. Once all minerals have been sacrificed, you will get Sacrifice Dust on spawning minerals instead, which can be used to level up sacrificed Minerals. With a bit more grinding (and the Death tarot card), you should be able to get 1e228 RfP, for Refine Node 89.

Remember to merge 3 Ethereal+1000 Zodiacs together for Amazing Zodiacs when you can.

Order of sacrifice
Here's the most optimal order for which sacrificed minerals to level up first.


A chart for sacrifice leveling order
For Level 28: Yellow > Light Blue > Red > Blue > Dark Purple > White > Green > Black > Orange > Pink

Level 29: Yellow > Light Blue > Blue > Red > Dark Purple > White > Green > Black > Orange > Pink

Level 30-31: Light Blue > Yellow > Blue > Red > Dark Purple > White > Green > Black > Orange > Pink

Level 32-34: Yellow > Light Blue > Blue > Red > Dark Purple > White > Green > Black > Orange > Pink

Level 35: Yellow > Blue > Light Blue > Red > Dark Purple > White > Green > Black > Orange > Pink

Level 36: Yellow > Blue > Red > Dark Purple > White > Light Blue > Green > Black > Orange > Pink

After Level 36, just use the Level 36 order from then on.

Tarot Challenges (1-26)
Make sure to upgrade Refine Node 90 whenever possible; gives a great boost to Sacrifice Dust gain. Each time you level up your gems, upgrade this as much as possible for when you level up your gems the next time.

Tarot challenges is mostly waiting with some milestones so here we will provide order of challenges with progression milestones in between.

Note: Just because a challenge is right after another that doesn't necessarily mean it can be attempted right away, it simply means that this is your next goal to aim for. If a challenge can be attempted right after another, that will be pointed out in the guide.

1. The Fool Challenge:
Set up a macro on loop mode with UseArcan(1) and run it for 20 minutes or so. Leave your time flux on 3x if you want to, but it isn't needed.

2. Ace of Wands Challenge:
Can be done with water zodiacs pretty much instantly (use arcane spam macro listed below)

3. Ace of Cups Challenge:
Use Earth zodiac setup (for zodiac setups look Guide:Tarot)

You should get your gems to level 28 after this.

4. Ace of Swords:
With a level 28 Red Gem, this challenge is free.

5. Ace of Pentacles:
Use Fire zodiac setup (doesnt matter that much but I found it best), should just take a few minutes. If it’s not getting there, use The Hierophant spam

6. Two of Swords:
Using a loop mode macro with only "useArcan(strength)" and then timewarping 30 minutes 4 times will get you there with Fire zodiac build.

7. Two of Wands:
Timewarp with each element will get you to 1e9.

Sacrifice Check-in: You should have Yellow and Light Blue gems at Level 29.

8. Two of Cups:
You should have enough luck to spawn an epic here, just check in the astrology tab in the luck menu for about 2.79 luck. You will need Pentacles upgrade 5 for this one.

9. Three of Cups:
This one can be done relatively soon after 2 of cups as well, the amount of luck needed isnt much, although you might need to automate it with auto-unity and auto-sell zodiac.

You should get all of your gems to level 29 if you haven't already.

10. Two of Pentacles:
Use a macro to spam The Lovers and you'll get it quickly.

Sacrifice Check-in: You should have all gems at level 31, and Refine Node 91 is highly recommended.

11. Three of Wands:
Use about half an hour of time flux (more if needed) per element; this one's mostly a waiting game.

12. Four of Cups:
This challengs isn't as crazy as it seems, just have auto unity and auto sell zodiacs enabled and you'll get it soon enough, also use the star tarot card

13. Five of Cups:
Do a round of sacrificing/selling zodiacs, then switch to the standard wind build (Guide:Tarot) and you'll finish it instantly

Sacrifice Check-in: This is one of the biggest timewalls so far. You'll have to grind a lot of RfP for Refine Node 90, preferably up to level 17. Then, grind for level 33 for Yellow Gem, and optionally get Refine Node 90 to level 19 for all the other colors. With all gems at level 33, you may proceed to The Magician.

14. The Magician:
Each use of the magician makes the next one weaker, so you have to use it in a specific order. The most optimal order is: water > earth > earth > wind > wind > water > fire > fire. Remember to switch to the appropriate zodiac loadout and go all out on lab-5 for earth.

15: The High Priestess:
Set up a macro that uses The High Priestess on loop mode and time warp about an hour

16: The Empress:
use empress manually (no macro) and do the timing on 67 (lol)

17. Three of Pentacles:
Start the challenge, use The Emperor and wait a few minutes

18. Four of Pentacles:
Start the challenge and you'll finish it nearly instantly

19. Five of Pentacles:
start the challenge and time warp about 50 minutes, you'll finish it quickly

20. Six of Pentacles:
Start the challenge, wait 15 seconds, use the hierophant and the emperor, and you'll be done

Sacrifice Check-in: you should have all gems at Level 34.

21. Four of Wands:
should be possible quite quickly with high level blue gem

22. Five of Wands:
Same as before

Sacrifice Check-in: You will need all gems at Level 35, maybe even a level 36 gem or two. Also, make sure you have Refine Node 93 for the next challenge.

23. Six of Wands:
This one's a waiting game, it might take a large amount of tf/afking.

24. The Emperor:
Start the challenge, start a macro with only emperor in it in loop mode, use a 10 minute timewarp, and then wait or use more time flux, it shouldnt take too long.

If this fails, it could be because time flux is interfering with automation, especially on lower-end hardward. Try using x3 game speed without spending additional time flux.

25. The Hierophant:
Can be done right after emperor, turn off auto delete minerals automation, then simply time warp 10 minutes four times, and then a 5th 3 minute timewarp, should be easy

26. The Lovers:
Set up a macro with automatic lovers and wait for 10 or so minutes without time flux; this one can be done right after The Hierophant.

The Runes Timewall
This is the biggest timewall so far, and probably the most difficult grind so far as well. For this, you'll need to unlock Relic 53, which requires Attack Level 20600. Make sure your arcana macro has The Strength in it and keep it on for the time being. Then, grind all of your gems to Level 36. Getting to Level 37 gems will take too long, so instead you'll primarily need to grind Moon Runes and the RN7 efficiency upgrade to do this. Here's a checklist of the what you'll likely need for this:

RfP
This should be at least 1e518, though ideally 1e526 or more.
You should get your SMP Efficiency to ^1.75, and ^1.35 for Black Gem minimum.
Make sure Polish Enhancing is upgraded as much as possible, too.
Runes
Moon Runes matter the most here; get the RN7 Efficiency upgrade to 11.8x.
In some instances, this was possible with a 11.6 multiplier paired with the Three of Swords challenge completion. See below for info regarding this.
Elements
1e48000 for all elements (except water) is required, with 1e46000 to 1e47000 for water being ideal here.
Remember to adjust your RP distubution to 100% in Lab-5 (Gen. Exponent) for Earth.
At some stages, you might only be getting x1e10 to 1e50 to each element, this is normal.
Tarot
Get all Tarot Upgrades up to Pentacles-6, level 1. Double check that you have all previous upgrades at max level.

Tarot Resources should be at ~1e350 for all of them.
Total Card Levels should be ~2e19.
Make sure you've completed all of the previous Tarot Challenges.
Note on Three of Swords:
It is possible to complete the Three of Swords challenge before reaching Attack Level 20600, as the boost to Attack Exponent is equal to about one RN7 Moon Rune upgrade. However, due to rounding with Attack damage values, predicting the total required time is difficult. 0.03 Attack Damage is recommended if you decide to go this way; requiring 5 to 20 hours of Time Flux.

Astrology
Zodiacs should be around Amazing+40 to Amazing+50 rarity.
All Donuts should cost around 1e108 to 1e110 for the next upgrade.
Remember to do a round of Sacrificing Zodiacs
After finally getting Attack Level 20600 and Relic 53, there should be a VERY satisfying push to Level 37 gems, and then almost instantly to 38, and then 40.

Tarot Challenges 27-40
27. The Chariot:
Instantly completed upon getting all gems to Level 40, basically after getting Relic 53.

28. Justice:
Can be attempted right after chariot.

Ensure Autospawn Special Minerals is ON with Max Base Cost is set to a small but nonzero number (for example, 100) and Base Cost is clear (not set to zero, actually blank), and also set up a macro to activate Justice on repeat, then begin the Challenge.

Should take about 1 hour of using 3x TF (don't use timewarping)

29. Three of Swords:
just a little waiting, should only take about 15 minutes or so

Sacrifice Check-in: Use Cups Upgrade 6 to get Level 41 gems. Get the yellow one first; all of the other colors come instantly after that.

30. Four of Swords:
This one should complete itself quite quickly, remember to use strength on the macro

31. Five of Swords:
This can be attempted right after Four of Swords using the same thing as before; it shouldn't take too long to complete.

Sacrifice Check-in: You should push for Relic 54 at this stage. The following are recommended stats: 1e649 RfP, 2e370 for each Tarot Resource, 1e20 Total Card Levels, 1e71000 to 1e73000 for all elements, and 14.4x on the Refine Node 7 effieciency Runes upgrade. Afterward, push the Moon runes RN7 upgrade even further and grind all gems to level 42.

32. Seven of Wands:
Wait on each element with the respective builds and lab settings. It should take about 52 minutes for water, 65 minutes for earth, 75 minutes for wind and 65 minutes for fire

33: Six of Swords:
Once again, use the arcana spam macro with Strength in it with short bursts of 10 minute timewarps; should get you there in about an hour of time flux.

Sacrifice Check-in: Grind for Level 43 gems. The Refine Node 7 effieciency Moon runes upgrade makes this grind shorter than the one for level 42 gems.

34. Six of Cups:
Level 43 gems should give you more than enough. If not, grind Astrodust a little, and it'll get you there. This one should be pretty quick.

Sacrifice Check-in: Grind for Level 44 gems, and buying RN7 efficiency with Moon Runes makes this grind even shorter. Zodiacs Check-in: It's recommended to refresh your zodiacs if you haven't done that in a while. Your zodiacs should be at Amazing+1000 rarity at this point. Merge three of these to get a Prime rarity zodiac, then refresh all of your setups with Prime rarity zodiacs.

35. Seven of Cups:
Just enable auto unity and afk for a few minutes. Even if it looks like it wont get there, it will.

Sacrifice Check-in: You now have to push to Level 45 gems, which requires getting 1e800 RfP for Refine Node 95. To do this, grind common minerals until the "Refine Points after Prestige" stat says 5e791 RfP or more, and use a high enough level Death arcana card. Simply cycling elements and gettin the 1e400 Tarot Resource upgrades should get you there (with a little bit of time flux). This will also get you e100,000 for all elements.

36. Seven of Swords:
This one's a waiting game with The Strength on your macro

Progress Check-in: You now have to push attacks for Relic 55. Then, you'll need the 1e418 Wands upgrade and the 1e424 Cups upgrade. Then, use the arcana spam macro on loop mode to grind for 1e875 RfP for RN96 and grind Sacrifice Dust for Level 46 gems. This will quickly get you 1e900 RfP and 1e450 Tarot resources, which will get you Level 47 gems very quickly and allow you to unlock Relic 56.

37. Eight of Cups:
Use auto-unite to see how close you are. You'll need to get to about 3.69-3.73 luck in the challenge, or about e8515 luck outside the challenge. For this, push the luck and quality donuts and rn7 efficiency up to 23.2-23.4x (can be done quite a bit before those levels of rn7 efficiency, but that will speed it up if you cant do it yet)

RfP Push: You now have to push for 1e950 RfP, which means getting the "Refine Points after Prestige" to about 8.5e940. which will get you to 1e950 with the Death card. This is a LONG timewall; everything must be cycled and regular minerals have to be pushed as far as possible. RN7 efficiency has to be pushed to about 23.6x, Tarot resources to 1e470, and elements to about e116k for fire, e113k for earth, e113k for wind, and e111k for water.

38. Seven of Pentacles:
This will take a while, between 1-3 hours (depending on how lucky you are). After the first spawn, youll be spawning about level 83-84 common minerals, and that should get you to level 105 after a while. This one's mostly just a waiting game.

39. Eight of Wands:
Pretty much the longest challenge so far; will take about 24-25 total hours. Water should produce about 1.5e10/s, fire about 8.5e9/s, earth about 3.8e9/s and wind about 3.5e9/s. This one takes a long time.

Sacrifice Check-in: Push up to Level 48 gems, shouldn't take too much time flux. Then, push your "Refine Points after Prestige" to about 8e990, and the Death arcana card should get you to 1e1000 RfP. With 1e1000 RfP, you can buy RN99 and unlock artifacts, and move on to Guide:Artifacts. Unlocking Artifacts will also allow you to get Level 49 gems pretty much instantly.

Artifacts
Progressing in Artifacts is actually really simple: flush your regular Tarot resources to get their golden versions, check for any Artifacts upgrades, then any regular Tarot upgrades, and repeat. The first part for each artifact is VERY good for getting Sacrifice Dust, since it provides a x10000 (1e4) multiplier per level, and there are 4 artifacts with this exact bonus. Also, as you gain more Golden resources, make sure to check the Refine Tree to see if you can afford any levels in RN100, since that drastically reduces the Artifacts cooldown.

Things to remember:

Golden resources increase exponentially with Tarot resources, so ALWAYS wait the full cooldown before flushing.
Purchasing Artifact parts will not spend your golden resources; you only need the required amount to get these upgrades.
Do not underestimate the power of these artifact upgrades! They are extremely powerful and are a necessity for completing these next Tarot Challenges.
Some of these challenges will require a LOGPOW stat, which is unlocked with your 7th Artifact part for each artifact. Level these up as much as possible; if a challenge involving LOWPOW related penalties isn't doable, then its pretty obvious why.
Tarot Challenges after Artifacts (40-52)
40. Eight of Swords:
Requires about 10 of each golden resource within the Artifacts tab. Do an Attack Prestige if needed beforehand; should complete itself instantly.

Sacrifice Check-in: Push up to Level 52 gems, it shouldn't take long at all if you have 20 of each golden resource. While grinding to 20 of golden resources, it is recommended to max out all Refine Tree upgrades up to the 100th Refine Upgrade.

41. Eight of Pentacles
Can be completed right after Eight of Swords, should complete itself instantly. (If needed, get your golden resources to 20 of each)

Artifacts Check-in: Make sure you have Crystal Mirror artifact's 4th part, Focal Point, up to around 7~8 levels. It shouldn't be a problem once you have the golden resource automation.

42. Nine of Swords
You should have at least 7 Focal Point levels for this challenge. Use the score build as it's the only way to boost your attack power.

43. Nine of Cups
If you can easily beat Nine of Swords, then this shouldn't be a problem. Just make sure you have enough Teeth levels, you'll likely need them at ~level 5.

44. Nine of Wands
This should be attempted right after Nine of Cups, as the Water element alone can beat this challenge instantly with the water build.

45. The Hermit
This challenge takes a while at the start, so use around 80 minutes of TF to skip the 8 hour cool down timewall. After that, you can use a bit more TF, or just wait it out.

46. Wheel Of Fortune
This is pure luck, but you'll get there eventually.

47. Nine of Pentacles
You can do this right after the previous challenge. It will take around 20 minutes of TF to get to mineral 270.

You should have SML 60, and golden resources required for LOGPOW before proceeding.

48. Ten of Pentacles
It is recommended to have enough wind element to spawn level 548 common minerals. If you have enough, a few minutes of TF will complete the challenge.

49. Ten of Swords
This one should complete itself quickly. Just make sure you have the Memory Crystal Artifact piece.

50. Ten of Cups
You need Authenticity Glaze level 2 to beat this challenge. But once you do, it's basically instantly completed.

51. Ten of Wands
Just like the previous challenge, you need enough LOGPOW to beat this challenge, level 2 should do the job. In the challenge, spend 3 minutes of TF for each element and you will beat the challenge. As of April 2026, this leaves one challenge left, and it is...

This is the final push for this update. Now, you should be able to upgrade your special minerals to level 61. Don't forget to buy all tarot upgrades as much as you can.

52. The Strength
Once you get the red special mineral to level 61, you can attempt this challenge. You will have to use 30~40 minutes of TF to build the multiplier, and then wait a few Strength cycles to reach attack level 2000.

The Page challenges are much more difficult than they seem, so for now, its time to push your Artifacts. Remember to level up Special minerals, Tarot upgrades, and cycle elements until you get 1e2425 RfP to get RN103, and move on to Guide:Plague!

Tarot Challenges After Plague (53-65)
Challenges below should be tried after you get some progress in plague page.

53. Page of Cups
Can be completed instantly with about 30 million of each golden resource.

54. Page of Wands
You need to reach e2026 as a multiplication of all elements, which means around e510 on each. You should be able to finish this quickly after you complete at least stage 2-1 in plague.

55. Page of Swords
Depending on the amount of Golden Resources you have, you’ll have to reach a different plague stage to beat this challenge. This will take a bit of time or time flux; if you have 30 million golden resources, you’ll need to have finished Plague Stage 2-9. if you have 40 million, you’ll need to have finished stage 2-7.

Artifacts Check-in: get 84m Golden Resources on swords and 98 million on wands.

Plague Check-in: It’s about time to set up a VE farm. Use auto-craft ERs and set it to a level where you can craft it in around 100ms. Then, spam click sell ERs and get up to 5m VE. once you get 5m VE, stop buying ER upgrades and wait for 1e10 ViP. Once you get 1e10, you'll get Achievement 549 which lets you auto-dissolve ERs. Setup your farm just like last time, and this will push your ViP very fast, letting you get Level 67 Special Minerals quickly, with 111m Golden Resources on swords and 130m on wands.

56. Page of Pentacles
Needs about 89-105m Golden Resources, should be quick.

Plague Check-in: Remember to keep pushing Plague stages; stage 4-5 should be good.

57. The Hanged Man
Use a single-arcana macro of the hanged man, should complete instantly.

58. Death Challenge
Enter, activate the Death card, then wait a bit to Refine Prestige. This may take a few times. (Tip: wait at least 10 seconds to Refine Prestige, this will boost your chances a bit.)

59. Temperance
Start the challenge and place the Temperate rarity Zodiac in your Part of Fortune slot. Then, set up a macro that repeatedly spams the Temperance card, and place your new Zodiacs accordingly. You can find the correct zodiacs to put in a specific slot by clicking on that planet.

Or, if you want a plain list:

Sun: Leo
Mercury: Gemini or Virgo
Venus: Taurus or Libra
Moon: Cancer
Mars: Aries or Scorpio
Jupiter: Sagittarius or Pisces
Saturn: Capricorn or Aquarius
Uranus: Aquarius
Neptune: Pisces
Pluto: Scorpio
Chiron: Taurus or Libra
Part of Fortune: Any
The challenge will instantly complete upon satisfying these requirements. Exit after and sell all of the Temperate Zodiacs. (Note: you have to do this by hand.)

60. The Devil
Activate The Devil, and click all 10 chads. (Note: interestingly, the chads within the challenge seem to move slower than they do outside the challenge.)

Plague Check-in-in: go for a Latent-type ER with around ^0.357 stealth or more

61. Knight of Pentacles
May take a while, easier with a ^0.36 stealth ER.

62. Knight of Wands
Requires about ^0.37-0.38 Infectivity, Virulents are best for this.

63. Knight of Swords
Can be done right after Knight of Wands; try pushing Spread Power if you can’t complete it.

64. Knight of Cups
Farm Plague stages you can easily beat to increase your Total Infected population, and this challenge should be easy.

65. The Tower
Your chance of completing this challenge scales with Relic 67 chance, so try to push those levels as much as possible beforehand. For this challenge, you should have that chance at 7-10% minimum (from 3 or 4 Relic 67 levels), but pushing this number higher is very difficult.

Tip: Complete the Bonus Trial before you try to complete this challenge, so you don’t have to deal with the progress setbacks.

This challenge is unfortunately random, so it is very likely you lose the first time. If you do lose, remember to push ERs as much as possible to get back to 20000 infected in Universe quickly.

Tarot Challenges after Singularity (66-75)
66. Queen of Swords
This one is pretty simple; and requires about 35.5 Singularity Mult. Also, try getting Singularity 20-22 beforehand.

66+1. Queen of Pentacles
This one requires a multiplier of 70-75 to clear, though you can wait for merge cycles if needed. Additionally, your Mineral Level is actually affected by the Hierophant card, which from RN105, is increased by your ER Level. So, try to get a level 60-62 ER equipped before attempting.

68. Queen of Wands
This one is based off of both Singularity Count and Mult. So, the higher your Singularity count, the less Mult you need, and vice versa.

Challenge Requirement
Sing. Count	Sing. Mult
20	x90
22	x80
24	x60
26	x50
Overall, you should try and complete this challenge with 22 Singularities and about a x80 Mult.

69. Queen of Cups
Your Zodiac Exp during the challenge is solely based off your current "Next Singularity Mult" (The number below the "On Next Singularity" table where it says "Total x (number)"). To complete this challenge, you need 32 Zodiac EXP for a level 6 Zodiac, which means about a x1.9874 total (equal to the 5th root of 31). Your stats roughly should be 22-25 Singularities, 80-100 Mult, Singularity Tree Node 1 at Ascension 5, RN125 at level 5, which will let you clear the challenge at ~e600 Atoms.

Tip: Since this takes a couple hours, you should try getting Singularity 25 beforehand, and claim Singularity 26 while doing this challenge.

70. The Star
Should be done as soon as you unlock houses and singular zodiacs at Singularity 32, but it is pure RNG so before doing it, you can try getting some decent Singular Zodiacs first, and boosting RN133. You should have about a 9.4% uncommon chance with a water build and The Star active.

NOTE: When you Singularize for the Zodiac, you must re-enter the challenge since it automatically exits the challenge.

This will definitely take multiple tries and many hours.

Singular Zodiacs Check-in: If you dont already have one, try getting a Pisces singular Zodiac. Then, farm Singularity Mult as much as you can for these next few challenges.

Note on Kings Challenges
Each Kings challenge will add a Singularity mult gain factor when completed, based on the amount of Tarot challenges you've completed for that respective suit at x1.35.

While it is fun to theorize about multiple completions, it is going to be capped at x1.35 for now.

71. King of Cups
Almost all kings challenges can be done right after you get a pisces singular zodiac. The amount of mult you have is basically how long you will need to wait so higher the better. For this one, its best to have around x250-500 mult. Just enter the challenge and Unite with higher level Zodiacs as their levels increase. Additionally, using an earth or wind Zodiac loadout has been shown to increase your Zodiac level a bit. The current meta loadout is:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Tau	Tau	Tau	Gem	Tau	Gem	Gem	Tau	Gem	Tau	Tau	Tau

(Taurus - Taurus - Taurus - Gemini - Taurus - Gemini - Gemini - Taurus - Gemini - Taurus - Taurus - Taurus (TTT-GTG-GTG-TTT))

72. King of Swords
This one doesnt really have many tricks to it, other than more mult = faster completion. x300 mult takes ~10 hours, x500 = ~9 hours, x1000 = ~6 hours, x3000 = ~4.5 hours. It's best to complete this one at about x500 mult, shortly after cups.

Tip: avoid using higher Time Flux speeds because of frame-locked mechanics, so the higher speeds wont do you justice.

73. King of Wands
This one is based on two things: Singularity Mult, and Fire Factor 3. You should try and get a decent Singular Aries before attempting, and using this build can also be very helpful:

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Sagit	Leo	Leo	Libra	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Leo	Aries	Leo	Leo

(this build is to help maximise Fire's factor 3.)

Also, make sure your Lab distribution is something like 1% Lab 2, and 99% Lab 6.

This challenge is best done at around x1000-1500 mult, which should take ~9 hours.

74. King of Pentacles
This challenge is very easy to figure out. You need to spawn Mineral level 64 to be able to clear at e308 Atoms; any more time is basically a waste due to the harsh e308 Atom penalty.

You need x3969 mult with no Time Flux, x3600 mult with x8 speed, and x3364 mult with x32 speed.

75. The Moon
This challenge takes a LONG time, probably not as bad as Eight of Wands, but its still take quite a bit to complete. You should be able to finish this after the Kings challenges with x3600 mult, which should take 7-9 hours.

Important things to remember when doing this challenge: DO NOT change the zodiac on the Moon slot; it doing so will reset the quality of that zodiac to 1, and DO NOT touch Elements. Your macro should be UseArcan(18) and ONLY this. Since Macro blocks activate once per frame, including the macro looping, with 60 FPS and x60 Speed, you shouldn't miss any Moon card usages. to be safe, its probably better to fill all of your 30 (or more) block slots with UseArcan(18), and if you do consistently get lower FPS, you should set your Time Flux speed around there to be safe.

Guia : Artefatos
Introdução... O próprio guia!
Você desbloqueia artefatos comprando o nó de refinamento 99.

Ao esgotar seus recursos de tarô, você pode obter recursos dourados que serão usados ​​para comprar artefatos.

Ao esgotar seus recursos, você sofre uma penalidade no ganho normal de recursos, que diminuirá ao longo de 10 horas, mas esse tempo é afetado pela velocidade local dos artefatos e pode ser reduzido refinando o nó 100.

Assim que você desbloquear os artefatos, deve usar todos os seus recursos e obter mais sacrifícios de SpM.

Continue fazendo isso até conseguir 5 recursos dourados.

Quando você tiver 5 recursos dourados, aprimore os Artefatos, pois os aprimoramentos são requisitos, não custos.

Com 8 recursos dourados, você desbloqueará a segunda peça do artefato...

Isso é tudo!

Não, sério, é só isso mesmo, continue descartando seus artefatos para obter mais recursos dourados até conseguir comprar algo.

Veja ou edite este modelo

Guide:Plague
Plague introduction
Plague is a mechanic you unlock by buying refine node 103.

To get it you need to farm golden resources something around 350k (3.5e5) in each is enough, tho for smoother progression you shouldnt slack on your golden resources and should get them as much as you can.

The moment you unlock plague you should start infecting you first house 1-1, each first completion gives you an exponential boost to gold gain.

Max infected is a stat that scales with amount of people you infect. Note: this bonus doesnt scale between defferent places to increase it you need to actually infect more people in ONE place. For example beating 1-1 2 times wont increase your max but beating 1-2 once will.

Total infected is stat that scales with OVERALL amountof infected people so unlike previous stat you can complete same stage a few times to increace it.

Max PlP is stat that scales with your maximum PlP you have, PlP is a thing that you get each second and increases using PlP boosts and you PlG.

Max PlG is stat that scales with with your maximum PlG, you get PlG by beating locations with reward increasing accordingly to your location

Max infected in universe is a stat that unlocks after you beat all stages. To get it you must enter 61st location and infect people there, amount of people you infect is a thing that increases that stat.

The plague gameplay loop consists of entering stages and beating them consistently. Keep beating as many stages as you can throughout the guide.

To actually beat stages you need to upgrade and craft new Endoplasmic Reticulums.

Plague guide
All you have to do is beat stages, dont worry if beating a stage takes a lot of time.

Note: infection starts slow, speeds up in the middle, and slow downs even more near the end.

At first stages you wont get much resistance you will just have to wait to complete stage and occasionaly craft new endoplasmic reticulums, and sell ones that are less powerfull, getting new resource: VE that works same as PlG but provides ViP that you can use in endoplasmic page to buy boosts for endoplasmic creation.

At the same time while you complete your first stages you should try to complete pages tarot challenges, buy new artifact levels, new refine nodes, upgrade elements, get new sacrifice SpM levels and some times update your zodiacs.

For new tarot challenges and rough check inst of progression refer to [Guide:Tarot Challenges].

A way to singularity
To unlock singularity you need to buy refine node 121 but it costs too much.

to decrease its cost you need to finish bonus trial, tower challenge, and buy tarot upgrade pentacles 11 because all 3 of these decrease cost of refine nodes starting from 121.

pentacles 11 is an easy thing all you need to do is buy that upgrade, to get eonugh resources do the usuall cycle of elements zodiacs sacrifices etc but with plague included.

Bonus trial requires you relic 67 right after you get it enter 5-5 plague and just complete it untill achievment 600 completes after that enter bonus trial: get endoplasmic - decay lvl 25 (if 25 is not 50% of your max you need to increace your max) and prob rarity at least C-B.

Tower challenge is hardest of them to complete it you need to have decent level of relic 67 and at least 20k of universe infection refer to tarot challenges for it.

After you complete all 3 you will get a lot of cost decreases and you can buy refine node 121.

After that get 1e17000 tarot recources and press singularity button.

Zodiac Loadouts
(Redirected from Guide:Zodiac Loadouts)
A Zodiac Loadout or Zodiac Build refers to how Zodiacs are assigned to Planets for specific tasks. There is a vast number of different Loadouts for various stages of the game.


Contents
1	Early Attack Builds
2	Late Attack Builds
2.1	Quality Build (ht6 done)
2.2	Game Speed Build (ht6 done)
2.3	Score Build (500+) and IT2 build
2.4	Gold Farm Build (ach 240 done)
2.5	Astrodust Farm Build
3	Postgame Builds
3.1	Score Build (1400+)
3.2	Level Build
3.3	Luck Build
4	Leaderboard Builds (obsolete)
5	Leaderboard Builds (Minerals Update)
5.1	Level Build (2000+): Max. Eternities, IP, EP, Stars, Lab Level
5.2	Score Build (3500+): Max. Score, Common Exponent
5.3	Infinity Build (3000+): Max. Infinities
5.4	DP Build: Max. DP, Supernova
6	Element tab
6.1	Fire Focused Planet Loadouts
6.1.1	Before Unlocking Etherials Rarity Fire Loadout
6.1.2	Fire Loadout Alternative
6.1.3	Fire Loadout After Unlocking Etherial Rarity
6.2	Earth Focused Planet Loadouts
6.2.1	Earth Loadout
6.2.2	Earth Loadout Alternative
6.3	Wind Focused Planet Loadouts
6.3.1	Wind Loadout
6.4	Water Focused Planet Loadouts
6.4.1	Water Loadout
6.4.2	Water Loadout Alternative
7	Tarot and beyond
7.1	Fire Loadout
7.2	Wind Loadout
7.3	Water Loadout
8	See Also
Early Attack Builds
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Scorp	Canc	Pisc	Scorp	Canc	Scorp	Canc	Canc	Scorp	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc

Full water: will push attacks the fastest at the start



Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aries	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Aqua	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc

Mostly Pisces: Higher quality zodiacs



Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Scorp	Pisc	Aqua	Canc	Pisc	Scorp	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc

Water + wind: easier to get through unity, still great for attacks

Late Attack Builds
Quality Build (ht6 done)
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc

Full Pisces gives you a significant increase in Zodiac quality which overshadows the decrease in Zodiac level.

Game Speed Build (ht6 done)
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Scorp	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc

Minimizes the amount of Zodiacs you would need to farm, while also maximizing all Game Speed buffs.

Score Build (500+) and IT2 build
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aqua	Aqua	Aqua	Libra	Libra	Libra	Leo	Aries	Leo	Aries	Aries	Aries


Gold Farm Build (ach 240 done)
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aqua	Aqua	Aqua	Libra	Scorp	Scorp	Canc	Aries	Canc	Aries	Aries	Aries

Gets close to maximizing score and planet buffs with a large increase to your GS allowing you to get very close to where you were before, but speeding up the grind by a lot. Use all Gemini with the Unity Farm macro to reach up to 12 Unities per second, but this is obsolete.

Astrodust Farm Build
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Aries	Pisc	Pisc

Maximizes zodiac sale price with speed, side note the Aries can go anywhere as Pisces and Aries are the same season.

Postgame Builds
Score Build (1400+)
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aqua	Aries	Aqua	Libra	Aqua	Libra	Leo	Aries	Leo	Aries	Aries	Aries


Level Build
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aqua	Libra	Aqua	Libra	Gem	Libra	Gem	Libra	Gem	Gem	Gem	Gem


Luck Build
Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Canc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Pisc	Aqua	Pisc	Pisc	Scorp	Pisc	Pisc

Useful for pushing zodiac level for getting achievements like #188 (lv.30), #190 (lv.40), #205 (lv.50), etc.

Leaderboard Builds (obsolete)
EP max

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aqua	Aries	Aqua	Libra	Aqua	Libra	Leo	Aries	Leo	Aries	Aries	Aries

IP max

Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune

Unity • Page 1 of 5

Entering Unity
Your first Unity reset and the Zodiac system

Unity Requirements
Unity is unlocked when you reach 1.08e2,466 Eternity Points. This happens naturally through long Animal Points runs at DTP 40+. After completing all 50 Eternity Challenge difficulties and pushing your Dilation Tree, Unity becomes accessible.

When you hit the EP threshold, the Eternity button will show an additional option to claim 1 Unity. This is a major reset that brings you back to the beginning, but with powerful new mechanics.

What Unity Resets
Unity is a complete reset. Here's what gets wiped:

Everything from Dilation
Dilation Points, Max Score, Dilation Upgrades, Dilation Tree Points

Everything from Eternity
Eternity Points, Eternities count, Laboratory, Animals, Supernova (unless noted by trials)

Everything from Infinity
Infinity Points, Infinities count, Stars, Infinity Upgrades, Generators

Everything from Revolution
Score, Colors, Prestige levels, Promotion levels

What Persists Through Unity
Not everything is lost! The following persist permanently:

•
Achievements & Achievement Bonuses - All your hard-earned achievements stay
•
Soul Shop Purchases - Any Souls spent remain spent
•
Automations - Your automation settings and unlocks persist
•
Animal Points (CRITICAL) - AP is PERMANENT and never resets, even through Unity
•
Statistics & Progress Tracking - Your stats continue accumulating
📝
Animal Points Are Forever

Unlike everything else, Animal Points are completely permanent. They persist through all Unity resets and continue to provide their multiplier bonuses. This is why AP farming was so important in Dilation!
Your First Zodiac: ALWAYS Pick Aries
When you claim Unity, you'll be presented with 4 Zodiac options to choose from. Each Zodiac provides powerful bonuses when equipped to planets.

Aries
CRITICAL: First Unity Zodiac Choice
On your first Unity only, you are guaranteed to see a Level 3 Rare 
Aries
Aries with two specific bonuses:

• Common Exponent (multiplies your Power)
• Mult Gain (increases multiplier per revolution)
ALWAYS pick Aries Aries for your first Unity. It has bonuses you normally can't get at this stage (Level and Rarity boosts). All other Zodiacs will be significantly worse for progression.

After your first Unity, Zodiac options become randomized. You'll need to make strategic choices based on what bonuses appear and what Element you need.

Unity Bonuses
Every Unity you complete grants permanent, stacking bonuses to progression. These bonuses are not tied to Zodiacs - they're passive rewards for accumulating more Unities.

Unity Bonus Formulas:

IP Gain = [1 + Unities] ^ [2 + log(Unities)]

EP Gain = [1 + Unities] ^ [1 + 0.25 × log(Unities)]

Eternity Gain = [1 + Unities]

DP Gain = [1 + Unities × 3] ^ [0.5 + 0.125 × log(Unities)]

These bonuses compound quickly. Your 5th Unity will be significantly faster than your 1st. By Unity 10+, you'll breeze through early game in minutes.

The Planet System
Zodiacs must be equipped to Planets to activate their bonuses. You start with only Sun unlocked.

Sun (Start)
Available immediately upon Unity

Mercury
Unlocks when you place your first Zodiac on Sun (Achievement #165)

Moon
Unlocks after completing your first Trial (Achievement #166)

Mars
Unlocks after completing Easy Trial #4

Jupiter
Unlocks after completing all Medium Trials (Achievement #157)

Additional Planets
Saturn, Uranus, Neptune, Pluto, Chiron, Fortune Pars unlock through later achievements (Ach #188-205)

⚠️
CRITICAL: Moving Zodiacs Causes Unity Reset!

If you move a Zodiac from one planet to another, it will trigger a Unity reset! Only rearrange Zodiacs at the beginning of a Unity run, never in the middle. This is a common mistake that can waste hours of progress.
Planet Bonuses
Each planet provides an additional bonus based on the Season of the equipped Zodiac. The 12 Zodiacs are split into 4 seasons:

Spring:
Aries
Aries
Taurus
Taurus
Pisces
Pisces
Summer:
Gemini
Gemini
Cancer
Cancer
Leo
Leo
Autumn:
Virgo
Virgo
Libra
Libra
Scorpio
Scorpio
Winter:
Sagittarius
Sagittarius
Capricorn
Capricorn
Aquarius
Aquarius
Example: Placing 
Aries
Aries (Spring) on Sun gives: "Zodiac Quality ×1.5". Placing 
Aquarius
Aquarius (Winter) on Sun gives: "Eternity Rewards ^1.2".

These planet bonuses become strategically important later. For now, just know they exist and vary by season.

Your First Unity Run
After picking Aries and placing it on Sun, here's what to expect:

Adjust Automations: Make sure your automation settings are configured. Everything should still be set from pre-Unity.
Progress Through Infinity: With Unity bonuses, your first Infinity will take minutes instead of hours. Aries's Mult Gain bonus is huge early.
Push to Eternity: Your first Eternity post-Unity takes hours instead of days. The x4 IP Gain and x2 EP Gain from Unity bonuses accelerate everything.
Get Animals & Lab: Buy cheap animals with your permanent AP. Reach 7 Animals for Lab. Push Lab levels with EP.
Complete Eternity Challenges: Start working through EC again. Medium Trial #1-2 rewards will eventually auto-complete EC1-2 permanently!
Reach Dilation: Get 81 Animals, complete 60 EC. Dilation is much faster now.
Push DTP & EP: Get some DTP, push EP. Your goal is to reach 1.08e2,466 EP again to Unity for your 2nd Zodiac.
💡
First Unity Timeline

Your first Unity will likely take 1-3 days of real time (longer if you're unlucky with Zodiacs). This is normal and expected. Each subsequent Unity gets faster.
Zodiac Rerolls (Not Recommended Early)
You can reroll your 4 Zodiac options by spending Souls. This lets you see 4 new random options, but you cannot undo the reroll.

⚠️
Rerolls Are Expensive

Rerolling costs Souls and the value diminishes as you progress. Early Unities take days, so a reroll might save you some time if your options are truly terrible. Later, when Unities take minutes, rerolling isn't worth the Soul cost. Only reroll if all 4 options are genuinely bad (no Fire Elements, terrible bonuses).
Reaching Your 3rd Unity: Trials Unlock
When you complete your 3rd Unity and equip all 3 Zodiacs (Achievement #165), you'll unlock Trials - Unity's version of Eternity Challenges.

Trials are crucial for progression. They provide:

•
Permanent upgrades and quality-of-life improvements
•
Exp Factors that increase Zodiac levels
•
Auto-completed Eternity Challenges (2 per Trial completed)
•
Access to Planet Shop, automation, and advanced mechanics
Your goal for the first 3 Unities is to collect Fire Element Zodiacs:

Aries
Aries
Leo
Leo
Sagittarius
Sagittarius
Fire bonuses are the most useful for Easy Trials since they boost early game mechanics.

Critical Warnings
WARNING #1: Moving Zodiacs
Moving a Zodiac from one planet to another causes Unity reset. Plan your planet assignments carefully!

WARNING #2: Game Speed Bonus
Avoid Zodiacs with Game Speed bonus until after completing Medium Trials. Game Speed slows down your fastest Infinity and Eternity times, which is actually a handicap for Medium Trial completion requirements!

WARNING #3: Zodiac Inventory Limit
You have limited inventory slots. Until you unlock Planet Shop (Easy Trial #6), you cannot sell Zodiacs. Focus on claiming only Fire Element Zodiacs until the shop unlocks to avoid filling your inventory with useless Zodiacs.

🏁
Ready for Unity?

You're ready to claim your first Unity when you hit 1.08e2,466 EP! Pick Aries, place it on Sun, and start the grind back through the game. Your goal: collect 3 Fire Zodiacs and unlock Trials at Unity #3. Good luck!

Unity • Page 2 of 5

Zodiac Basics
Understanding the 12 Zodiacs, Elements, and Planet bonuses

The 12 Zodiac Signs
There are 12 Zodiac signs in Revolution Idle, split into 4 Elements (Fire, Earth, Wind, Water) and 4 Seasons (Spring, Summer, Autumn, Winter). Each Zodiac provides up to 4 unique bonuses when equipped to a Planet.

🔥 Fire Zodiacs
Aries
Aries
Leo
Leo
Sagittarius
Sagittarius
🌍 Earth Zodiacs
Taurus
Taurus
Virgo
Virgo
Capricorn
Capricorn
💨 Wind Zodiacs
Gemini
Gemini
Libra
Libra
Aquarius
Aquarius
💧 Water Zodiacs
Cancer
Cancer
Scorpio
Scorpio
Pisces
Pisces
Key Zodiac Properties:

•
Element: Determines which bonuses the Zodiac can have (Fire, Earth, Wind, Water)
•
Season: Determines Planet bonus received (Spring, Summer, Autumn, Winter)
•
Level: Increases bonus strength (based on Zodiac Exp Factors)
•
Quality: Multiplies bonus effectiveness (raised to ^1.25 power)
•
Rarity: Determines base strength and number of bonuses (2-4 bonuses)
Zodiac Rarity System
Zodiac Rarity determines how many bonuses a Zodiac provides:

Garbage → Epic Rarity
Provides 2 bonuses

Legendary → Godly Rarity
Provides 3 bonuses

Divine → Immortal Rarity
Provides all 4 bonuses

Immortal +X Rarity
Unlocked at ~550-600 Luck (Achievement #220). Provides all 4 bonuses with higher base stats. The "+X" continues scaling infinitely with Luck.

💡
Rarity Progression

Early game: You'll see mostly Rare/Epic. Mid-game: Legendary/Mythic become common. Late-game: Divine/Godly with high Quality. Endgame: Immortal +5, +10, +20+ with insane Quality values.
Fire Element Zodiacs
Fire Zodiacs focus on early-game progression bonuses. They are essential for Easy Trials and speed up Infinity/Eternity.

Aries
Aries (Spring)
• Mult Gain - Increases multiplier per revolution (BEST early bonus)

• Promotion Power - Multiplies Promotion bonuses

• Ascension Power - Multiplies Ascension bonuses

• Common Exp. Power - Multiplies Common Exponent (your main Power stat)

Best for: Easy Trials, early Unity runs. Mult Gain is the most important early-game stat.

Leo
Leo (Summer)
• Lap Speed - Increases revolutions per second

• Promotion Power - Multiplies Promotion bonuses

• Ascension Power - Multiplies Ascension bonuses

• Common Exp. Power - Multiplies Common Exponent

Best for: Speed farming, late-game Score builds

Sagittarius
Sagittarius (Winter)
• Lap Speed - Increases revolutions per second

• Slowdown Power - Increases Slowdown effectiveness (UNIQUE)

• Ascension Power - Multiplies Ascension bonuses

• Common Exp. Power - Multiplies Common Exponent

Best for: Easy Trials (Slowdown trivializes them after 5 Unities)

Earth Element Zodiacs
Earth Zodiacs focus on Infinity mechanics - IP gain, Infinity count, Generators, and Stars.

Taurus
Taurus (Spring)
• IP Gain ^ - Powers IP Gain (very strong)

• Infinity Gain × - Multiplies Infinities earned

• Generator Exponent × - Multiplies Generator scaling

• Mult/bought Gen × - Multiplies Generator multipliers

Best for: IP farming, Infinity count achievements

Virgo
Virgo (Autumn)
• Star Base × - Multiplies Star effectiveness

• Infinity Gain × - Multiplies Infinities earned

• Generator Exponent × - Multiplies Generator scaling

• Mult/bought Gen × - Multiplies Generator multipliers

Best for: Star-heavy builds, mid-Eternity farming

Capricorn
Capricorn (Winter)
• Star Base × - Multiplies Star effectiveness

• Stardust Exponent × - Multiplies Stardust scaling (UNIQUE)

• Generator Exponent × - Multiplies Generator scaling

• Mult/bought Gen × - Multiplies Generator multipliers

Best for: Star optimization, specific Trial strategies

Wind Element Zodiacs
Wind Zodiacs focus on Eternity and Dilation mechanics - the most important bonuses for mid-to-late game.

Gemini
Gemini (Summer)
• Eternity Gain × - Multiplies Eternities earned (BEST for Unity farming)

• EP Gain ^ - Powers EP Gain

• Supernova Req ^^ - Reduces SN requirements (tetrated)

• Lab Mult Power × - Multiplies Lab scaling

Best for: Zodiac Level builds, fast Unity farming, Eternity achievements

Libra
Libra (Autumn)
• DP Gain ^ - Powers DP Gain (CRITICAL for Score builds)

• EP Gain ^ - Powers EP Gain

• Supernova Req ^^ - Reduces SN requirements

• Lab Mult Power × - Multiplies Lab scaling

Best for: Hard Trials, Attack progression, Score builds

Aquarius
Aquarius (Winter)
• DP Gain ^ - Powers DP Gain

• Free Lab Levels + - Adds free Lab levels (UNIQUE)

• Supernova Req ^^ - Reduces SN requirements

• Lab Mult Power × - Multiplies Lab scaling

Best for: Medium Trials (Free Lab bypasses restrictions), Score builds

Water Element Zodiacs
Water Zodiacs focus on meta-progression: Zodiac Quality, Luck, Game Speed, and Achievement bonuses.

Pisces
Pisces (Spring)
• Luck + - Increases Zodiac Rarity chance

• Zodiac Quality × - Multiplies new Zodiac Quality

• Achievement Reward × - Multiplies Achievement #29 bonus

• Game Speed × - Multiplies game speed

Best for: Zodiac farming (THE best build for getting better Zodiacs). Full Pisces = Maximum Quality + Luck.

Cancer
Cancer (Summer)
• DTP Cost ^ - Reduces DTP costs (UNIQUE)

• Center DTU Effect × - Multiplies Dilation Tree CENTER node

• Achievement Reward × - Multiplies Achievement #29 bonus

• Game Speed × - Multiplies game speed

Best for: Game Speed builds, specific Dilation strategies

Scorpio
Scorpio (Autumn)
• Luck + - Increases Zodiac Rarity chance

• Center DTU Effect × - Multiplies Dilation Tree CENTER node

• Achievement Reward × - Multiplies Achievement #29 bonus

• Game Speed × - Multiplies game speed

Best for: Game Speed builds (Neptune Autumn bonus), Zodiac farming support

⚠️
Game Speed Warning

Avoid Game Speed Zodiacs until after completing Medium Trials! Game Speed slows down your fastest Infinity and Eternity times, which is required for Medium Trial #1-2 rewards. Only use Water Zodiacs after Medium Trials are done.
Planet Bonuses by Season
When you equip a Zodiac to a Planet, you receive the Planet's Season Bonus based on the Zodiac's season. This creates strategic depth - the same Zodiac can give different Planet bonuses!

Example: Sun Planet Bonuses
Spring on Sun: Zodiac Quality ×1.5

Summer on Sun: Luck Mult ×1.15

Autumn on Sun: Unity Rewards ^1.1

Winter on Sun: Eternity Rewards ^1.2

Strategic Implication: You want to match seasons to planets for optimal bonuses. For example, placing Pisces (Spring) on Sun gives "Zodiac Quality ×1.5" - perfect for Quality farming!

💡
Planet Bonus Optimization

Don't worry too much about Planet bonuses early. Focus on getting the right Zodiac bonuses first. Planet optimization becomes important for Hard Trials and Attack pushing, but early game just focus on Fire Zodiacs for Trials.
Common Early Zodiac Builds
Here are some standard Zodiac loadouts for different goals:

Easy Trials Build (Full Fire)
Aries
Aries
Leo
Leo
Sagittarius
Sagittarius
All Fire Zodiacs for maximum early-game power

Goal: Complete all 6 Easy Trials. Prioritize Aries with Mult Gain, Sagittarius with Slowdown.

Medium Trials Build (Wind + Fire)
Aquarius
Aquarius
Libra
Libra
Aries
Aries
Mix of Aquarius (Free Lab Levels), Libra (DP/EP), with some Fire for speed

Goal: Complete Medium Trials. Free Lab Levels bypass Lab restrictions. Supernova Req^^ helps reach 20 Animals milestone.

Quality Farming Build (Full Pisces)
Pisces
Pisces
× 12 planets
All 12 planets with Pisces for maximum Quality + Luck

Goal: Farm better Zodiacs. Use after completing Hard Trials. This is THE endgame farming build.

Game Speed Build (Pisces + Scorpio)
Pisces
Pisces
Scorpio
Scorpio
Mostly Pisces with 1-2 Scorpio for maximum Game Speed multiplier

Goal: Speed up Unity runs, Attack Mult farming. Use after Medium Trials completion.

Score Build (Wind + Fire)
Aquarius
Aquarius
Libra
Libra
Aries
Aries
Leo
Leo
Aquarius, Libra (DP Gain), Aries, Leo for maximum Score/Attack damage

Goal: Push Attack Levels, Hard Trials progression. Requires 6+ planets unlocked.

Level Build (All Gemini)
Gemini
Gemini
× 12 planets
All 12 Gemini for maximum Zodiac Level from Eternity Factor

Goal: Maximize Zodiac Levels for specific optimization strategies.

Zodiac Stat Priorities
When choosing which Zodiac to keep/farm, prioritize in this order:

Element Match: Do you need Fire for Trials, Wind for Score, Water for Quality?
Bonus Match: Does it have the specific bonus you need? (Mult Gain, DP Gain^, Luck, etc.)
Rarity: Higher rarity = more bonuses. Divine+ gives all 4 bonuses.
Quality: Multiplies bonus effectiveness. 100 Quality vs 10,000 Quality is huge!
Level: Determined by Exp Factors. Higher level = stronger bonuses.
Early game: Element + Bonus > everything else. Late game: Quality > Level > Rarity for optimization.

🏁
Zodiac Summary

Zodiacs are complex but learnable. Focus on collecting Fire Zodiacs (Aries, Leo, Sagittarius) for Easy Trials, then branch out to Wind (Libra, Aquarius, Gemini) for Medium/Hard Trials. Full Pisces becomes your go-to farming build later. Good luck!

Unity • Page 3 of 5

Easy Trials
Completing your first 6 Trials to unlock core Unity features

Trials Overview
Trials are Unity's version of Eternity Challenges. They unlock at your 3rd Unity when you equip all 3 Zodiacs (Achievement #165).

There are 6 Easy Trials, and each one provides:

•
A permanent reward (QoL improvement or new mechanic)
•
+2 EC completions for all future Unities
•
Progress toward unlocking Medium Trials
💡
Trial Mechanics

To attempt a Trial: (1) Lock the Trial, (2) Press Reset Unity button, (3) Reach the goal. There's NO penalty for failing - you can retry as many times as needed. Always LOCK the trial before resetting or you'll do a normal Unity by accident!
Easy Trial Requirements
All Easy Trials have the same goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score). This means Unity bonuses and most Zodiac bonuses won't help - you need early-game bonuses.

Critical: Fire Zodiacs Only

Aries
Aries
Leo
Leo
Sagittarius
Sagittarius
Only Fire Element Zodiacs provide useful bonuses for Easy Trials. Wind/Earth/Water bonuses affect Infinity+, which doesn't help you reach Infinity.

⚠️
Zodiac Inventory Management

Until you complete Easy Trial #6 (which unlocks Planet Shop), you cannot sell Zodiacs. If you fill your inventory with non-Fire Zodiacs, you'll be stuck. Only claim Fire Zodiacs until the shop unlocks!
Recommended Trial Order
Complete Easy Trials in this order for smoothest progression:

Easy Trial #2 (EASIEST - do first!)
Easy Trial #1
Easy Trial #3
Easy Trial #4
Easy Trial #5
Easy Trial #6
Trial #2 is by far the easiest and can often be completed with just your starting Aries. Trials increase in difficulty as you go down the list.

Easy Trial #2: Can't Promote 3 & 4
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score)

Handicap: You can't buy Promotion #3 and #4

Reward: Unlocks Trials Exp Factor

Why do this first: This is the easiest Trial. You only need Promotions #1 and #2 to reach Infinity, which is trivial with Fire Zodiacs.

Strategy
• Use your starting Level 3
Aries
Aries (Mult Gain bonus)
• Disable Auto Promotions for #3 and #4
• Enable Auto Promotions for #1 and #2 only
• Should complete in under 1 minute
⚠️
Bug Workaround

There was a bug with Trial #2. To avoid issues, disable ALL auto Promotions and only manually promote #1 and #2. This ensures you don't accidentally fail.
Completion Time: <1 minute with 1-3 Fire Zodiacs

Easy Trial #1: Only First 6 Colors
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score)

Handicap: You can only buy the first 6 colors

Reward: ×1.4 to base Zodiac Quality

Recommended timing: Attempt after Trial #2. You need at least 3 Fire Zodiacs to complete comfortably.

Strategy
• Aim for 2+
Aries
Aries with Mult Gain bonuses
•
Leo
Leo or
Sagittarius
Sagittarius work as substitutes
• 6 colors is enough to reach Infinity with good Mult Gain
• Unity bonuses help significantly by Unity 3-6
Completion Time: 1-20 minutes depending on Zodiac quality. If it takes longer than 20 minutes, do more Unities to get better Fire Zodiacs.

Note: Some players complete this BEFORE Trial #2 if they're patient. It's worth doing earlier if you have good Aries Zodiacs.

Easy Trial #3: Common Exponent ×0.6
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score)

Handicap: Common Exponent is multiplied by 0.6

Reward: Common Exponent multiplied by 1.05 permanently

Recommended timing: Attempt after Trials #1-2. Slowdown makes this significantly easier.

Strategy
• If you have
Sagittarius
Sagittarius with Slowdown: Enable Auto Slowdown at 10.0
• Slowdown grants free Ascensions, compensating for lower Common Exponent
• Without Slowdown: Need 3+ Fire Zodiacs with good bonuses
• Farm 1-2 more Unities if struggling
💡
Slowdown Power

Sagittarius
Sagittarius with Slowdown bonus trivializes Easy Trials #3-6. If you have it, these trials become minutes instead of grinding. Highly recommended to get Sagittarius before attempting the later Easy Trials.
Completion Time: Minutes with Slowdown, 10-30 minutes without

Reward note: Unlocking Mars gives you a 4th planet slot, which helps for future Trials by letting you equip more Zodiacs simultaneously.

Easy Trial #4: Can't Promote 2/4 or Prestige
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score)

Handicap: You can't buy Promotion #2 and #4, and you can't Prestige

Reward: Unlocks Mars (4th planet slot!)

Recommended timing: Attempt immediately after Trial #3 with the same Zodiac setup.

Strategy
• Slowdown is extremely helpful again
• You still have Promotions #1 and #3, which is enough
• No Prestige means you need good Ascension bonuses
• Same Fire Zodiac setup as Trial #3 works
Completion Time: 5-30 minutes depending on Slowdown availability

Reward note: Unlocking Mars gives you a 4th planet slot, which helps for future Trials by letting you equip more Zodiacs simultaneously.

Easy Trial #5: Common Exponent ≤0.7
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score)

Handicap: Common Exponent can't be greater than 0.7

Reward: Unlocks Max Score Factor

This is a stat check. The 0.7 cap on Common Exponent is harsh. You need strong Fire Zodiacs, preferably with high Mult Gain.

Strategy
• Stack as many Aries with Mult Gain as possible
• Slowdown helps significantly
• If you're stuck, farm 1-2 more Unities
• Higher Unity count = better bonuses to bypass the cap
📝
When to Attempt

Most players complete this around Unity 6-9. If you attempt at Unity 3-4 and it's taking forever, just do more Unities. Each Unity makes this trial easier.
Completion Time: Varies widely. Can be minutes or 30+ minutes depending on Zodiac strength.

Easy Trial #6: No Prestige/Promote/Ascend
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Infinity (e308 Score)

Handicap: You can't Prestige, Promote, or Ascend any buyable

Reward: Unlocks Planet Shop (can finally sell Zodiacs!)

Hardest Easy Trial. You can't manually reset anything - you're stuck with base circle bonuses.

Strategy
• Sagittarius with Slowdown is CRITICAL here
• Slowdown provides free Ascensions without manually Ascending
• Leo with Lap Speed helps trigger Auto Slowdown
• Without Slowdown: Very difficult, requires many Unities
• Attempt around Unity 9-12 if you don't have Slowdown
⚠️
Slowdown Requirement

This Trial is exponentially harder without Slowdown. If you don't have Sagittarius yet, keep doing Unities until you get one with Slowdown bonus. It's worth the wait.
Completion Time: 5-15 minutes with Slowdown. 30+ minutes without (or requires more Unities).

Reward note: Planet Shop unlock is HUGE. You can finally sell unwanted Zodiacs for Astrodust and buy upgrades!

Easy Trials Completion Reward
When you complete all 6 Easy Trials, you receive a massive reward:

Permanent Benefits
✓ Keep Revolutions don't take away Score - Score never resets on Prestige/Promote!
✓ Prestige & Promotions reset nothing - Free resets with no downside
✓ Medium Trials unlock - Access to the next tier of Trials
These QoL improvements are game-changing. Combined with the individual Trial rewards, Easy Trials transform your Unity runs dramatically.

Recommended Animals for Easy Trials
With your permanent AP, buy these animals for Easy Trial runs:

Priority Animals (Buy with Free AP)
• [1,1] Cat - Always your first animal
• [1,2] Dog - Lap Speed bonus
• [2,1] Mouse - Early bonus
• [2,2] Guinea Pig - Useful early multiplier
These 4 animals are cheap and provide enough bonuses to help with Easy Trials without wasting too much AP on animals you don't need yet.

General Easy Trial Tips
•
Always Lock the Trial before resetting Unity. Use the lock button so you don't accidentally do a normal Unity.
•
No Penalty for Failure - If you fail a Trial, just hit the Reset button and try again. Experiment freely!
•
Each Trial gives +2 EC completions - By the time you finish all 6 Easy Trials, you'll have 12 EC auto-completed on future Unities.
•
Farm Unities between Trials - If a Trial is taking too long (30+ minutes), do 1-2 more Unities to boost your bonuses.
•
Prioritize Fire Zodiacs - Only claim 
Aries
Leo
Sagittarius
 until Planet Shop unlocks. Fill your inventory with Fire only.
🏁
After Easy Trials

With all 6 Easy Trials complete, you have Planet Shop, Mars unlocked, massive QoL improvements, and 12 auto-completed EC. You're ready for Medium Trials! These require reaching Eternity instead of Infinity, and introduce new strategic elements. Time to shift from Fire Zodiacs to Wind Zodiacs!
Zodiacs
Medium

Unity • Page 4 of 5

Medium Trials
Reaching Eternity with strategic Wind Zodiac builds

Medium Trials Overview
Medium Trials unlock after completing all 6 Easy Trials. The goal changes from reaching Infinity to reaching Eternity (e308 Infinity Points).

Key Differences from Easy Trials:

•
Unity bonuses now matter! IP/EP multipliers actually help
•
Wind Zodiacs
 become essential:
Gemini
Gemini
Libra
Libra
Aquarius
Aquarius
•
Fire Zodiacs still useful but not as dominant
•
Rewards unlock major automation and QoL features
💡
Medium Trial Strategy

The "cheese" strategy for Medium Trials: Stack 
Aquarius
Aquarius with Free Lab Levels bonus. With enough Free Lab Levels, you can bypass many Trial restrictions and push to Eternity quickly. Aquarius becomes your MVP.
Recommended Zodiac Setup
For Medium Trials, you want a mix of Wind and Fire Zodiacs:

Aquarius
Primary: Aquarius (Winter Wind)
• Free Lab Levels + - The most important bonus for Medium Trials
• DP Gain ^ - Helps with Score
• Supernova Req ^^ - Reduces SN requirements (tetrated)
• Lab Mult Power × - Multiplies Lab scaling
Goal: Get 100+ Free Lab Levels total from multiple Aquarius Zodiacs

Libra
Secondary: Libra (Autumn Wind)
• DP Gain ^ - Powers DP Gain
• EP Gain ^ - Powers EP Gain
• Supernova Req ^^ - Critical for getting 20 Animals milestone
• Lab Mult Power × - Lab scaling
Goal: Get ^^0.67 or better Supernova Req reduction to cheaply reach Supernova milestones

Support: Fire Zodiacs
Aries
Aries
Leo
Leo
Sagittarius
Sagittarius
Still useful for speed, but not as critical as Wind Zodiacs. Use 2-3 Fire Zodiacs for lap speed and early Infinity pushing.

Planet Bonus Optimization
For Medium Trials, optimize your planet bonuses:

Recommended Season-Planet Pairs:

Sun: Autumn (Unity Rewards ^1.1 for IP Gain)

Mercury: Winter (Star Cost ^0.9) - Skip for MT #2

Venus: Autumn (Supernova Rewards ^1.02)

Mars: Autumn (Star Cost ^0.85) - Skip for MT #2

Jupiter: Summer (Supernova Rewards ^1.03)

Translation: Place Libra (Autumn) on Sun, Venus, Mars for optimal planet bonuses. Place Aquarius (Winter) on Mercury.

Medium Trial #2: No Stars
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Eternity (e308 IP)

Handicap: You can't buy Stars

Reward: Infinity & Infinity Challenges always 1ms (HUGE!)

Why do this first: Easiest Medium Trial and the reward is incredible. Fastest Infinity set to 1ms permanently makes future progression much smoother.

Strategy
• Use same Aquarius setup as MT #1
• 100+ Free Lab Levels bypasses Star restriction
• With enough Lab, Stars become irrelevant
• Should complete immediately after MT #1
📝
Planet Bonus Adjustment

For MT #2, avoid Winter Zodiacs on Mercury and Mars (they give Star Cost bonuses which are useless here). Use Summer or Autumn Zodiacs instead for this Trial.
Completion Time: <1 minute with good Aquarius setup

Medium Trial #1: Only First 2 Generators
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Eternity (e308 IP)

Handicap: You can only buy the first two Infinity Generators

Reward: ×1.337 to Luck

Strategy: Farm enough Zodiacs with Supernova Req^^ to reach 7 Animals milestone for Laboratory.

Key Steps
Stack Libra/Aquarius with strong Supernova Req^^ bonuses
Cheaply reach Supernova milestones to gain AP
Buy animals in order: [1,1], [1,2], [2,1], [1,3], [1,4], [2,4], [1,5]
Reach 7 Animals → Lab unlocks
With 100+ Free Lab Levels from Aquarius, push to Eternity
Completion Time: 1-20 minutes depending on Zodiac quality. The hardest part is getting enough Free Lab Levels Zodiacs.

💡
Completion Order

Most players do MT #2 right after MT #1 with the same Zodiac setup. Both use the "Free Lab bypass" strategy.
Medium Trial #3: No Prestige
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Eternity (e308 IP)

Handicap: You can't Prestige

Reward: Supernova resets nothing, unlocks Auto DP Upgrades & Auto DTP

Strategy: Same as previous trials - Free Lab Levels OR switch to Fire + Free Lab mix.

Two Approaches
Option A: Same Aquarius preset as MT #1-2. Free Lab bypasses Prestige restriction.

Option B: Mix Fire Zodiacs with Aquarius. Fire for early speed, Wind for Lab.

Completion Time: 5-15 minutes

Reward note: Supernova resetting nothing is a nice QoL. Auto DP/DTP makes Dilation runs much smoother.

Medium Trial #4: Only 2 Infinities
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Eternity (e308 IP)

Handicap: You can only Infinite twice

Reward: Unlocks Auto Unity

Strategy: Reach 20 Animals milestone for IP/second, then push IP passively to Eternity threshold.

Critical Steps
Make sure you have lots of Supernova Req^^ Zodiacs
Make sure you have Free Lab Levels Zodiacs
Do NOT claim 2nd Infinity until you have enough IP for Eternity goal
Disable Auto-buy for: Infinity Upgrades, Generators, Stars, AP
Reach 20 Animals → IP/second activates (Animal Milestone #5 + EC Milestone #4)
Wait for IP to accumulate passively to e308
Claim Eternity
⚠️
CRITICAL: Don't Spend IP!

If you claim your 2nd Infinity and accidentally spend the IP on upgrades, you won't be able to Eternity and will fail the Trial. Disable all auto-buyers before claiming! Remember to turn them back on after completion.
Completion Time: 10-30 minutes (mostly waiting for IP/sec to accumulate)

Medium Trial #5: Only 1 Infinity
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Eternity (e308 IP)

Handicap: You can only Infinite once

Reward: Unlocks DTP Count Factor

Strategy: Identical to MT #4, but even stricter. You MUST reach 20 Animals for IP/second.

Same Strategy as MT #4
• Lots of Supernova Req^^ and Free Lab Levels
• Reach 20 Animals for IP/sec
• Disable all auto-buyers before claiming Infinity
• Wait for passive IP accumulation
• You have more time than MT #4 since you can't waste your Infinity
Completion Time: 15-45 minutes

Note: Paradoxically easier than MT #4 for some players because there's less temptation to accidentally waste IP.

Medium Trial #6: Can't Prestige/Promote, Only 5 Colors
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Eternity (e308 IP)

Handicap: You can't Prestige/Promote and can only buy the first 5 colors

Reward: Keeps fastest Eternity at 5ms

Strategy: Free Lab + SN Req^^ trivialize this. Aim for 100 Free Lab Levels and ^^0.67 SN Req.

Recommended Stats
• 100+ Free Lab Levels (from Aquarius stack)
• ^^0.67 or better Supernova Req reduction (from Libra/Aquarius)
• With these stats, Trial completes very quickly
• Slowdown is useless here (can't get all colors)
Completion Time: 5-20 minutes with proper Zodiac setup

💡
Probably Easier Than MT #5

Many players find this easier than MT #5 despite being last. The "cheese" strategy of stacking Aquarius and Libra makes this straightforward.
Medium Trials Completion Reward
When you complete all 6 Medium Trials, you receive:

Permanent Benefits
✓ Keep IP/second - 100% IP/sec persists through Unity resets
✓ Max IP Factor unlocks - New Zodiac Exp Factor for Level scaling
✓ Hard Trials unlock - Access to the final Trial tier
Planet Unlock
Completing all Medium Trials unlocks Jupiter (Achievement #157) - your 5th planet slot!

Achievement #157: Unity 10
At your 10th Unity, you unlock Lab automation and RP distribution, which is huge for QoL.

You can now set everything to Lab #6 (Common Exponent) permanently and forget about it!

Post-Medium Trials: What's Next?
After completing all Medium Trials, your goals shift:

Farm Better Wind Zodiacs: Now that Planet Shop is unlocked, you can merge Zodiacs for higher Rarity. Focus on getting Divine+ Aquarius Aquarius and Libra Libra.
Unlock More Planets: Work toward Achievement #188 (Neptune) by continuing Unity farming and progression.
Start Water Zodiacs: You can now use Game Speed Zodiacs safely! Pisces Pisces becomes very valuable for Zodiac Quality farming.
Prepare for Hard Trials: Hard Trials require reaching Unity (e2,466 EP). You need DP Gain^ Zodiacs - lots of Libra Libra and Aquarius Aquarius.
🏁
Hard Trials Preparation

Hard Trials are significantly harder than Medium Trials. You'll need very strong Wind Zodiacs with high DP Gain^ bonuses, along with supporting bonuses like Free Lab Levels, EP Gain^, and SN Req^^. Start farming Zodiac Quality now by running Full Pisces builds between Unity runs. Next up: Hard Trials, Attacks, and endgame!
Easy
Hard
