Unity • Page 5 of 5

Hard Trials & Beyond
Completing Hard Trials, unlocking Attacks, and reaching endgame

Hard Trials Overview
Hard Trials are the final Trial difficulty. The goal is to reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP) - meaning you have to go through the entire game cycle with additional handicaps.

Key Differences from Medium Trials:

•
Must complete full Infinity → Eternity → Dilation → Unity cycle
•
DP Gain^ becomes the most important stat
•
Trials #1-3 are relatively manageable, Trials #4-6 require serious Zodiac investment
•
New Zodiac builds needed: Score Build for Attacks
⚠️
Hard Trial Difficulty Spike

Hard Trials #1-3 are doable with good Wind Zodiacs. Hard Trials #4-6 are MUCH harder and require specific preparation including Achievement #200-201, Planet Shop progression, and strong Attack/Relic progression. Expect to spend significant time between HT #3 and HT #4.
Recommended Planet Bonuses for Hard Trials
Planet optimization becomes critical for Hard Trials. Use these Season-Planet pairs:

Optimal Hard Trial Setup:

Sun: Winter (Eternity Rewards ^1.2)

Mercury: Winter (Star Cost ^0.9)

Venus: Winter (DU Power ×1.05) - MOST IMPORTANT

Moon: Autumn (Eternity Rewards ^1.15)

Mars: Autumn (Star Cost ^0.85)

Jupiter: Autumn (DU Power ×1.03)

Neptune: Summer (DU Power ×1.06) - Cancer best

DU Power bonuses from Venus, Jupiter, Neptune are incredibly important. They multiply the base strength of all Dilation Upgrades, making Dilation progression much faster.

Hard Trial #1: Can't Buy More Than 20 DTP
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP)

Handicap: You can't buy more than 20 DTP

Reward: Unlocks DP Amount Factor

Strategy: You need ~^2.5 DP Gain from Zodiacs. Use multiple Dilation Tree loadouts strategically.

Zodiac Setup & DTP Builds
• Stack Libra and Aquarius with DP Gain^ bonuses
• Supernova Req^^ as secondary stat
• DTP 20 limit means strategic tree usage:
- Supernova: C:1 T:1-1-5-4 B:1-1-1-5
- DP: C:5 M:4-1-5-5
- AP/EP: C:1 T:1-1-1-5 B:5-5-0-0
Completion Time: 30-90 minutes depending on DP Gain^ strength

💡
Not Too Bad

HT #1 is relatively easy compared to later Hard Trials. The 20 DTP limit is annoying but manageable with good DP Gain Zodiacs.
Hard Trial #2: Can't Buy Lab or RP Upgrades
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP)

Handicap: You can't buy Lab or RP upgrades

Reward: Unlocks Lab Level Factor

Strategy: Slot enough Free Lab Levels to get to Dilation. ~300 Lab Points is recommended.

Aquarius Strategy
• Need multiple Aquarius with Free Lab Levels bonuses
• 300+ Free Lab Levels makes this Trial easy
• Same DP Gain^ focus as HT #1
• Neptune unlocked (Ach #188) helps significantly
Completion Time: 30-60 minutes

Hard Trial #3: Can't Supernova
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP)

Handicap: You can't Supernova

Reward: Unlocks Autosell Zodiacs

Strategy: Supernova-related bonuses (SN Req^^) are useless UNLESS they also have DP Gain^. Focus on maxing DP^.

DP-Focused Build
• All Zodiacs must have DP Gain^ as priority
• Libra and Aquarius stack
• EP Gain^ helps as secondary
• Free Lab Levels still useful
Completion Time: 30-90 minutes

MAJOR MILESTONE: Macro Automation
Completing HT #3 allows you to fully automate Unities using Macros. This is game-changing for farming Zodiacs. You can set up a Full Unity Macro that runs EC → Dilation Tree → Unite automatically in a loop.

Major Unlock: Attacks (15 Trials Total)
After completing 15 Total Trials (all Easy + all Medium + 3 Hard), you unlock Attacks - a completely new progression layer!

What Are Attacks?
Attacks are a new revolution system with 5 Attack Circles. Instead of building Score, you deal Damage to enemies. Each enemy defeated increases your Attack Level.

When you Unity, you're rewarded with Gold based on your max Attack Level. Gold is used to buy Relics and upgrade Attack Circles.

Attack Damage Formula
Damage is based on your Revolution Current Score converted to Base Damage via a formula (improved by Relics 13 & 19).

This Base Damage is then multiplied by Attack Multipliers (from Attack Circle revolutions), Cumulative Multiplier (from Relics), and raised to Attack Exponent.

📝
Attacks Are Permanent Progress

Unlike Unity which resets, Attack Multipliers don't reset on Unity. They're permanent! This means farming Attack Mult by running Game Speed builds is a permanent investment in your Attack damage.
Hard Trial #4: ≤1500 AP, ≤SN 10
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP)

Handicap: You can't buy more than 1500 AP or get past Supernova 10

Reward: Unlocks AP Factor

This is where Hard Trials get HARD. You need ^3.5-4 DP Gain from Zodiacs.

Prerequisites
• Neptune unlocked (highly recommended)
• Strong Planet Shop progression (Quality/Luck Donuts)
• Good Relic progression (Attack Level 400+)
• Achievements #200, #201 completed
Zodiac Setup
• Venus: Aquarius (DU Power ×1.05) - CRITICAL
• Jupiter: Libra (DU Power ×1.03)
• Neptune: Cancer (DU Power ×1.06, DTP Cost^^, Center DTU)
• Rest: Libra/Aquarius with maximum DP Gain^
Completion Time: 1-2 hours or more depending on preparation

⚠️
The Wall

HT #4 is a major wall. If you can't complete it, you need to farm: (1) Better DP Gain^ Zodiacs, (2) Higher Relic levels, (3) More Astrodust for Donut upgrades. Don't get discouraged - this is intended to take time.
Hard Trial #5: 50 DU Each, 2 DTP
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP)

Handicap: You can only buy 50 of each Dilation Upgrade and 2 DTP

Reward: Divides all Relic costs by 1,000,000 (HUGE!)

Strategy: Full Fire build. Fire Zodiacs boost Attack damage.

Fire Build Requirements
• All Red (Aries, Leo, Sagittarius)
• Level 80+ Divine Rarity minimum
• Quality 255-550 range
• Can mix a few EP Gain^/SN Req^^ Wind Zodiacs if needed
Completion Time: 20-30 minutes with strong Fire Zodiacs

Note: According to Hacksocto (community member), you should have Relics upgraded to around 1e9 cost for this Trial. The Relic reward makes this Trial critical for progression.

Hard Trial #6: Can't Dilate or Enter EC
Trial Details
Goal: Reach Unity (1.08e2,466 EP)

Handicap: You can't Dilate or enter Eternity Challenges

Reward: Unlocks EP Factor (past e2,466) - Breaks Unity!

Strategy: Same Fire build as HT #5. With HT #5 Relic reward, Relics are much cheaper.

Same Setup, Easier
• Exact same Fire Zodiac build as HT #5
• Maybe add 1 Fire to Fortune Pars if unlocked
• With cheaper Relics, this is actually easier than HT #5
• Should complete quickly
Completion Time: 20-30 minutes

Hard Trials Completion Reward
When you complete all 6 Hard Trials, you receive:

Break Unity
The EP Factor reward from HT #6 breaks the Unity EP limit. You can now go beyond 1.08e2,466 EP! This allows you to push EP further for better Zodiac Factors.

Insane Trials Unlock
Completing all Hard Trials doesn't immediately unlock Insane Trials. Insane Trials unlock through Minerals progression (weapon levels in Polish Prestige).

Insane Trials are the final challenge tier and require specific Mineral milestones to unlock.

Beyond Hard Trials: The Endgame Loop
After completing all Hard Trials, your gameplay loop becomes:

Zodiac Farming with Full Pisces: Run Full Pisces builds (all 12 planets) to maximize Zodiac Quality and Luck. This is your main farming build for getting better Zodiacs.
Attack Progression: Switch to Score Build (Aquarius/Libra/Aries) to push Attack Levels. Farm Gold, buy Relics, upgrade Attack Circles.
Achievement Hunting: Work toward Achievements #230-233 (Element achievements), #238 (Immortal +7), #239 (Attack 1000), #240 (Attack 1337 - unlocks Minerals).
Astrodust Farming: Sell excess Zodiacs for Astrodust. Upgrade Quality, Luck, Game Speed, Zodiac Exp donuts in Planet Shop.
Sacrifice System: Sacrifice high-quality Zodiacs for permanent stat bonuses. All sacrifice stats help progression.
Minerals & Polish: Achievement #240 unlocks Minerals tab. New progression system with Polish Prestige, Refine Tree, and Weapon upgrades.
Important Endgame Achievements
Achievement #230-233: Element Achievements
Complete full element builds (12 of same element). Requirements vary but generally need Immortal +2 to +5 with Quality 50k-200k+. These unlock powerful bonuses.

Achievement #238: Immortal +7 Zodiac
Get a Zodiac with Immortal +7 Rarity. Requires Luck level ~2,581. Unlocks additional content.

Achievement #239: Attack Level 1000
Reach Attack Level 1000. Unlocks new Relic and improves Relic effectiveness based on max Attack Level.

Achievement #240: Attack Level 1337
Reach Attack Level 1337. Unlocks Minerals tab - the next major content layer with Polish, Refine, and Weapons!

Zodiac Builds Summary
Here are the core Zodiac builds you'll use post-Hard Trials:

Quality Build (Full Pisces)
All 12 Pisces for maximum Zodiac Quality and Luck. Your primary farming build.

Game Speed Build (Pisces + 1 Scorpio)
Mostly Pisces with 1 Scorpio on Neptune for maximum Game Speed. Used for fast Unity farming and Attack Mult farming.

Score Build (Attack 500+)
Aquarius, Libra (DP Gain), Aries, Leo. Maximizes Score for Attack damage. Used to push Attack Levels.

Gold Farm Build
Mix of Score Build with some Game Speed (Scorpio/Cancer). Balances Attack pushing with speed for efficient Gold farming.

Level Build (All Gemini)
All 12 Gemini for maximum Zodiac Level from Eternity Factor. Used for specific optimization.

Relic Priority Guide
After unlocking Attacks, here's how to prioritize your Gold spending:

Relic #14 (Palate Expander): Decreases ALL Relic costs. Always max this first.
Relic #20 & #21: Boost Attack Mult Gain massively. Critical for progression.
Relic #9, #16, #17, #18, #24: Gold Gain and Mult Gain supporting Relics.
Relic #3, #13, #19: Boost Attack damage moderately.
Everything else: Lower priority. Buy when cheap.
💡
Buy Relics at Max Attack Level

After Achievement #239, Relic effectiveness increases with your max Attack Level. Always wait until you hit max Attack Level before buying Relics for maximum efficiency!
🏁
Congratulations!

You've completed the Unity Guide! You now understand the full Unity progression from first reset through Hard Trials, Attacks, and endgame content. The journey continues with Minerals (Achievement #240), Insane Trials, and eventually Elements. Keep farming better Zodiacs, pushing Attack Levels, and progressing toward the true endgame. Good luck!
Medium
Minerals

Unlocking Minerals
Welcome to Minerals
Minerals is an endgame mechanic and part of the Unity gameplay loop. It unlocks after completing all Hard Trials and introduces a merge-based progression system that dramatically increases your Attacks power and Gold generation.

Prerequisites
Before unlocking Minerals, you should have:

Completed all Hard Trials - This is the unlock requirement
~615+ zodiac sacrifices (reference point)
~2,300+ total Unities (reference point)
Immortal +12 or higher zodiacs at level 121+
Maxed sacrifice stats - Sacrificing should give minimal gains
Core Mechanics
Spawning Minerals
Click "Spawn" to create a mineral at the base cost of 1.00e110 gold. The spawned mineral will match the level shown in the "Spawn Lvl:" box.

Key concepts:

• Next Mineral Cost: Decreases over time (cost fall) until reaching Base Cost
• Base Cost: Minimum cost for current spawn level (increases by +1e10 per level until level 50, then +1e1 per level)
• Cost Fall: Rate at which cost decreases, affected by game speed and Time Flux
Merging Minerals
Drag two minerals of the same level onto each other to merge them into a level +1 mineral. Higher level minerals produce significantly more VP.

Level 1 → Level 2
+200% VP production
Level 2 → Level 3
+200% VP production
Each level
×3 more VP than previous
⚡ Always merge to the highest level possible!

Value Points (VP)
Minerals generate VP passively. Your Max VP (not current VP) provides permanent boosts to Attack Multiplier Gain and Attack Ascension Power. VP can be spent on upgrades without losing these bonuses.

✓ Max VP never decreases - even after spending VP on upgrades
✓ Game speed and Time Flux both affect VP production
✓ Focus on increasing Max VP early for permanent attack bonuses

Initial VP Upgrades
Max Level
Increases spawn level by +1. Must manually adjust spawn level in the box above "Spawn" button
Fall Speed
Increases rate at which Next Mineral Cost decreases. Critical for faster progression
Gold Gain
Additional gold multiplier. Essential for affording higher spawn levels
Common Exponent Multiplier
Powerful multiplier affecting multiple systems
Grid Height/Width (Optional)
Only buy if you need more space. Not required early. Max: 9×9
Luck/Quality Bonus
Skip early. Only useful when farming zodiacs
Early Strategy (Levels 1-30)
VP Spending Warning
Do NOT buy unnecessary VP upgrades that cost more than 1e12 VP until you unlock Relic #23 (Attack Level 1500). Even 1e10 range is questionable except for "Fall Speed".

Reason: It's better to keep growing Max VP for permanent attack bonuses than spending VP that will set you back hours to recover. Be stingy with VP early!

Optimal Progression Loop
Use Speed Build to farm VP and Unities
Switch to Gold Build to buy minerals and relics when needed
Use Time Flux strategically to speed up mineral cost fall
Don't forget to buy upgrades in the donut shop (spend that astrodust!)
Cycle between Attacks Push → Mineral Push → Relic Upgrades
Using Time Flux Effectively
Early on, waiting for new minerals takes forever. Use saved Time Flux and 3× speed to make it less tedious:

Wait until spawn cost drops to affordable level
Activate Time Flux
Spawn mineral
Repeat process
Later you'll be able to spawn minerals in bunches before using Time Flux.

Build Usage Guide
Speed:
Primary build for VP farming and Unity runs. Fast game speed = higher VP production
Gold:
Switch to this when buying minerals and relics. Best gold generation rate
Attack:
Use when pushing relics and achievements. Highest attack but slow speed
Early Milestones
Lv 1-10:
Gold problems start. Use Speed build primarily, Gold build when buying minerals/relics
Lv 10-20:
VP becomes constant problem. Be very stingy with VP spending. Focus on Max VP growth
Lv 20-30:
Attack levels should start increasing from Max VP bonuses alone. Several stages gained just from VP/Unity farming
Lv 30:
UNLOCKS POLISH! Major progression milestone
Goal: Mineral Level 30
Your primary objective is reaching Mineral Level 30 to unlock Polish. This will be a linear grind - there's no special trick to speed it up. Use Time Flux, prioritize Max Level and Fall Speed upgrades, and stay in Speed build as much as possible. Achievements will happen naturally along the way.

Polish

Polish & Refine
⚔️ Weapon Enhancement Systems
Polish and Refine are two prestige layers that reset your minerals for powerful permanent upgrades. Polish unlocks at Mineral Level 30, Refine at Level 70. These systems dramatically accelerate your progression.

Polish Prestige (Level 30+)
What Gets Reset
❌ Reset on Polish:
• All Minerals
• All VP upgrades
• Value Points (VP)
• Magnets (if unlocked)
• Merge Level (if unlocked)
✓ Kept on Polish:
• Max VP (permanent bonuses)
• Weapon Milestones
• Special Minerals (if unlocked)
• Refine Tree upgrades
Polish Points (PP) Gain
PP gain is based on your highest Mineral level reached in the current run. The formula grows exponentially with each level.

Key Mineral Levels for PP:
Level 30:
First PP unlock
Level 40:
Decent PP gains start
Level 50:
Significant PP boost
Level 70:
Unlocks Refine
Level 90+:
Massive PP scaling begins
The Five Weapons
⚔️ Sword
TOP PRIORITY
Effect: Boosts VP Gain
Milestones:
Lv.10: Unlocks Autospawn (2.36 PP)
Lv.25: Unlocks VP Factor (13.57 PP)
Lv.50: Zodiac Enhance is better (6,107 PP)
Lv.100: Relics' effects ×1.03 (3.02e35 PP)
Strategy: Get Sword to Level 10 FIRST for massive VP boost and Autospawn QoL. Then aim for Level 20-25 before branching to other weapons. Sword directly speeds up every mineral run.
🪓 Axe
SECONDARY PRIORITY
Effect: Empowers Mineral cost fall
Milestones:
Lv.10: Start Unity with 1,000 Eternities (7.04 PP)
Lv.25: Luck Mult ×2 (101 PP)
Lv.50: VP Gain ×5 (1,298,494 PP)
Lv.100: Unlock Insane Trial #4 (3.93e54 PP)
Strategy: Level alongside Sword. Faster cost fall = spawn minerals quicker. Lv.100 is required for IT4, but that's very late game.
🔱 Spear
GOLD FOCUSED
Effect: Boosts gold gain
Milestones:
Lv.10: Relic 1 powered by max mineral level (15.48 PP)
Lv.25: Relic 9 powered by Spear level (513 PP)
Lv.50: Unlock Insane Trial #2 (135,723,805 PP)
Lv.100: Relic 17 ×1.05 stronger (8.76e71 PP)
Strategy: Essential for affording higher spawn levels. Spear 100 is a huge power spike for gold generation. Push for it around Mineral Level 141-142.
🏹 Bow
ATTACK BOOST
Effect: Boosts Attacks Ascension Power
Milestones:
Lv.10: First Mineral Upgrade scales slower (29.80 PP)
Lv.25: Relic 13 ×(1 + bow/10) (2,177 PP)
Lv.50: Don't spend EP for animals/lab (9.71e9 PP)
Lv.100: Unlock Insane Trial #5 (1.60e88 PP)
Strategy: Bow 100 requires Rune upgrades, Refine Nodes 36/40, and Node 45. Very late milestone but IT5 removes need to enter Dilation.
👊 Knuckles
SPEED MULTIPLIER
Effect: Boosts minerals' local speed exponent
Milestones:
Lv.10: Sword Polish 20% more effective (103 PP)
Lv.25: Unlocks Insane Trial #1 (62,358 PP)
Lv.50: Unlock Insane Trial #3 (4.68e14 PP)
Lv.100: Unlock Insane Trial #6 (1.57e131 PP)
Strategy: Knuckles level directly affects RfP gain through Refine Node 38. Very important for late game. All Insane Trials unlock through Knuckles milestones.
📊 Polish Priority
Sword to 10 → Autospawn + massive VP boost
Sword to 20-25 → VP Factor unlock
Axe levels → Cost fall for faster spawns
Knuckles to 25 → Insane Trial #1
Spear levels → Gold for higher spawns
All weapons as high as possible before major milestones
Refine Prestige (Level 70+)
⚠️ Major Reset Warning
❌ Reset on Refine:
• All Minerals
• All VP upgrades
• All Magnet upgrades
• ALL Polish weapons
• Value Points & Magnets
• Merge Level
✓ Kept on Refine:
• Max VP (permanent bonuses)
• Weapon Milestones
• Special Minerals
• Previous Refine upgrades
Refine Points (RfP)
RfP are earned based on highest Mineral level. Growth is VERY slow early - Level 70-76 still only gives 1 RfP! Massive scaling starts at Level 90 (every 10th level gives big RfP increase).

Level 70-76:
1 RfP per run
Level 77-79:
2 RfP per run
Level 80:
4 RfP (first spike)
Level 90:
20 RfP (major spike)
Level 100+:
Exponential growth
Refine Tree Strategy
🎯 Golden Rule
ALWAYS prioritize Nodes 1, 4, 10 - Buy other nodes only if they cost less than half the price of these three.

Priority order: Node 1 > Node 4 > Node 10

Phase 1: Foundation (1-100 RfP)
Node 1:
Increases mineral base VP (starts at 3, each level +1). CRITICAL for VP gain
Node 2:
Bulk buy Attacks (×10). Makes attack grinding 10× faster
Node 3:
Attacks Prestige slightly stronger
Node 4:
Polish Sword ×1.1 per level (max ×2). Massive VP boost
Node 5:
Unlocks Merge Level (1 exp per merge, multiplies VP)
Node 6:
Unlocks Automerge (every 600 game seconds). Huge QoL!
Node 7:
Gold Gain upgrade powered to ^2. Big gold boost
Node 10:
PP gain ^(1 + 0.1×lvl). Max 10 levels for ^2 PP gain
Node 12:
VP upgrades don't spend VP! Game changer
Node 16:
SKIP early - minimal VP buff. Only buy with spare RfP
Phase 2: Magnets (100-500 RfP)
Node 18:
TOP PRIORITY! Unlocks Magnets - ultra powerful new mechanic. Get ASAP!
Node 21:
Gold on Unity multiplied by seconds since last Unity. Good passive gold
Node 22:
Spawning minerals doesn't decrease gold. Huge QoL
Node 23:
Buying Polish doesn't decrease PP. Nice QoL
Node 24:
Relic 23 ×(1 + 0.1×lvl). PRIORITY - get 5 levels before continuing!
Phase 3: The Big Choice (500-20k RfP)
🏃 Active Route (Recommended):
Node 29:
Relic 23 ×(1 + 0.01×Axe). INCREDIBLY POWERFUL! After buying, Node 24 becomes ultra strong
Do fast Mineral 130 runs - much faster than 140 runs. Node 29 turbocharges progression!

😴 AFK Route:
Node 27:
Base mineral cost ^0.93. Allows spawning 127-128 level minerals (gold cost spike at 124+)
Better for AFK but slower overall. Still need Node 29 eventually!

Note: Regardless of route, you need BOTH Node 27 and Node 29. Active route just gets Node 29 first for faster progression.
Phase 4: Game Changers (20k-100k RfP)
Node 31:
Relic 14 decreases base mineral cost. Spawn higher levels!
Node 33:
Get 100% Unity gold every second. No need to Unity for gold anymore!
Node 34:
MASSIVE! Magnet upgrades don't spend magnets + auto-buy unlocked. Changes 1hr magnet farm into 2 minutes!
Node 35:
Unlocks Runes - new resource system. Important for late game
Node 37:
Polish Knuckles 20% more effective. ~Doubles mineral speed based on Knuckles level
Node 38:
HUGE! RfP ×1.01^(Knuckles lvl). At Knuckles 80 = ×2.21 RfP gain!
Priority order: 31 → 33 → 34 → 35 → 37 → 38. These dramatically accelerate progression!

Phase 5: Late Game Mastery (100k+ RfP)
Nodes 36, 40, 24:
MAX these three - they're the main late game progression nodes
Node 41:
(50M RfP) Unlocks Special Minerals - permanent bonus minerals
Node 45:
(100M RfP) PP from Trials much bigger. Needed for Bow 100 milestone
Node 46:
(200M RfP) Special Minerals Progression - increase base spawn level
Node 50:
(1e9 RfP) RfP gain ×2^lvl (max ×1,024). Very expensive but powerful
❌ Node to SKIP
Node 28 (Polish Enhance): RfP sink early game! Only 1% additive bonus per RfP spent. Unlock it to access nodes behind it, but don't invest heavily until late game when you have spare RfP.
Node 16 (Merge Level Bonus): Minimal VP buff early. Only buy with spare RfP or in very late game.
🎯 Refine Strategy Summary
Stuck at 1 RfP per run for a while - do fast Level 70 runs, don't push higher
Max Nodes 4 and 10 before moving deep into tree
At Level 80/90/100 milestones, push for the big RfP spike
Active play: Get Node 29 ASAP, do fast Mineral 130 runs
Get Node 34 to eliminate magnet farming grind
Node 38 doubles your RfP - critical milestone
Endgame: Max Nodes 24, 36, 40 → Complete IT6 → Buy entire tree
Unlock
Magnets

Magnets & Merge Level
🧲 Advanced Progression Systems
Magnets and Merge Level are unlocked through the Refine Tree and provide powerful permanent multipliers. Magnets unlock a new currency and upgrade system, while Merge Level rewards active merging with exponential VP scaling.

Magnets (Refine Node 18)
How Magnets Work
After unlocking Node 18, you have a chance to gain 1+ Magnets every time you spawn a mineral. Base chance starts at 1% and can be increased through upgrades.

Base Magnet Chance:
1% per spawn
Trigger:
On mineral spawn
Persistence:
Reset on Refine only
Magnet Upgrades
💎 Magnet Chance Mult
Increases chance to get magnets per spawn

Cost: VP (not magnets)
🎲 Chance to get x2 magnets
When you get a magnet, chance to get 2 instead

Cost: VP (not magnets)
⚡ VP Gain Mult
TOP PRIORITY! Massive VP multiplier

Cost: Magnets
🔨 PP Gain Mult
Increases Polish Points gained from prestige

Cost: Magnets
⬆️ Spawn Mineral Lv+1
Chance to spawn a mineral one level higher

Cost: Magnets
⬆️⬆️ Merge gets Lv+2
Chance merged mineral jumps 2 levels instead of 1

Cost: Magnets
🧲 More Magnets
Increases base amount of magnets gained per proc

Cost: Magnets
🎯 Magnet Farming Strategy
Build up mineral level normally through several Polish runs to get VP production and cost fall speed up
Switch spawn level to 1-15 and turn on Autospawn
Goal: Maximize spawns to collect more magnets and increase Merge Level
Cost fall should reach base value before automerge refills all inventory spaces
Leave top 10 gold upgrades "On" in Autobuy, turn magnet upgrades off (buy manually) or only enable what you currently want
Magnet Upgrade Priority
Early Strategy:
More Magnets to ×3 - Get more magnets per proc first
VP Gain Mult - Start investing heavily in VP multiplier
Goal: Reach Mineral Level 120+ for Refine upgrades
Mid Strategy (Level 130-140):
More Magnets to ×5-6 - More is better but diminishing returns
VP Gain Mult - Buy at least the 200 magnet cost upgrade (more is better)
Merge Level should be ~30+ by now
When you can spawn Level 117+, switch to highest level minerals and merge normally
Late Strategy (Level 140+):
More Magnets ×5-7 - Enough for efficient farming
VP Gain + PP Gain - Max these for progression
Consider some Spawn +Lv and Merge +Lv upgrades for higher mineral levels
⚠️ Refine Node 34: Game Changer
Node 34 (12M RfP): Buying magnet mineral upgrades doesn't spend your magnets + Unlocks autobuy

Impact:
• Changes 1 hour of magnet farming into 2 minutes
• Eliminates tedious manual magnet grinding
• Enables autobuy for all magnet upgrades
• Magnets become permanent multipliers instead of consumable currency
This is one of the most impactful QoL upgrades in the entire Minerals system. Prioritize getting it!

Merge Level (Refine Node 5)
How Merge Level Works
Every time you merge minerals (drag one onto another), the merge bar fills by +1 EXP (mineral level doesn't matter). Each level of the merge bar multiplies your VP gain.

EXP per merge:
+1 (any mineral level)
Merge Level 1:
×1 VP multiplier
Merge Level 3:
×3 VP multiplier
Merge Level 10:
×10 VP multiplier
Reset:
On Refine Prestige only
Merge Level Formula
EXP required to level up:

10 × (Merge Level + 1)²
Beyond Merge Level 101:

[10 × (ML + 1)²] × (1 + (ML - 100) / 100)
Example Requirements:
Level 1 → 2:
40 EXP (40 merges)
Level 10 → 11:
1,210 EXP
Level 50 → 51:
26,010 EXP
Level 100 → 101:
102,010 EXP
Refine Node 16: Merge Level Bonus
Node 16 (up to 98 levels): Increases the VP multiplier per Merge Level

Base: Each Merge Level = ×that level (ML 3 = ×3)
Node 16: Multiplies VP bonus by ×(1.0 + 0.5 × Node Level)
Max (98 levels): ×50 multiplier to Merge Level effect
Note: Node 16 was considered low priority early, but becomes significant in late game with high levels

🎯 Merge Level Strategy
For Fast Merge Leveling:
Set Spawn Level to very low (1-5)
Enable Autospawn
Enable Automerge (Node 6)
Let it run - each merge gives +1 EXP regardless of mineral level
Great for farming Merge Level during long AFK sessions
For Balanced Play:
Build mineral level naturally through Polish runs
Merge Level increases passively as you progress
Don't specifically farm Merge Level early - it happens naturally
Later when you have Node 34, can farm efficiently during magnet farming
📊 Milestone Achievements
Merge Lvl 1:
Achievement #272 - "Keep merging..."
Merge Lvl 5:
Achievement #275 - "So lacking of automerge!"
Merge Lvl 50:
Achievement #286 - "Halfway There"
Merge Lvl 69:
Achievement #290 - "Nice... Merge Level"
Merge Lvl 100:
Achievement #295 - "Century Merger"
✨ Combined Power
Magnets and Merge Level work together synergistically. Farming low-level minerals for magnets also increases Merge Level. High Merge Level multiplies the VP gains from magnet upgrades. After unlocking Node 34, both systems become permanent multipliers that accelerate every aspect of Minerals progression.

Insane Trials
Insane Trials Overview
Insane Trials are the most challenging content in Minerals. They unlock after reaching specific milestones and requires specific zodiac builds, proper timing, and maxed upgrades. Completing them grants powerful permanent bonuses.

General Preparation
Before ANY trial: Do Attack Prestige and sacrifice all possible relics
Max out your mineral Common Exponent Multiplier upgrade
Use Hotkeys (default "A" and "B") to quickly buy attack levels
Make sure you have the required Polish weapon milestone
Use Enhancement on zodiacs if struggling (costs Astrodust)

Insane Trial 4 Alternative Method
⚠️ Warning
This method is ONLY for when you can't complete the Trial despite all your efforts. It has not been tested recently, so don't get your hopes up too high.

Enter Eternity Challenge 10-4 to boost your progress.
When the red circle is almost complete, leave the challenge and WAIT until it finishes outside of the Eternity Challenge.
Once the red circle completes outside the challenge, you should have finished the Insane Trial.
If you are slightly short on EP, try Eternating a few times. Keep repeating this as long as your Eternity Points continue to increase until the Trial is completed.
Eternity Challenges Difficulty 6
After completing Insane Trial #4, you unlock Difficulty 6 for all Eternity Challenges. These should be doable immediately when they unlock (or very shortly after). If struggling, get Spear 100 first.

Runes & Special Minerals
🌙☀️ Endgame Progression Systems
Runes and Special Minerals are late-game systems unlocked through expensive Refine nodes. Runes are AFK-generated resources used for powerful upgrades and spawning Special Minerals. Special Minerals provide permanent bonuses that persist through all prestiges.

Runes (Refine Node 35)
Unlock Requirements
Node 35:
20,000,000 RfP - Unlocks Runes and Rune upgrades
Recommended:
Have Node 34 first for magnet efficiency
Two Types of Runes
☀️ Sun Runes
Generation: Slower but consistent
Cost: Lower upgrade costs
Base Rate: 1 per 10 days
Focus: Mineral VP and Magnet upgrades
🌙 Moon Runes
Generation: Faster production
Cost: Higher upgrade costs
Base Rate: 1 per 10 days
Focus: Gold and Spawn level boosts
Important: Generation affected by local game speed and Time Flux. Both start at same base rate but Moon has more expensive but powerful upgrades.

Rune Upgrades
☀️ Sun Rune Upgrades
Node 1: VP Multiplier
Increases mineral base VP value
Priority: HIGH - Direct VP boost
Node 2: Magnets ×2
Doubles magnet amount gained
Priority: MEDIUM - Nice multiplier
Generation Amount
Increases runes generated per cycle
Priority: HIGH - More runes = more upgrades
🌙 Moon Rune Upgrades
Node 7: Mineral Spawn Level +2
BEST RUNE UPGRADE! Basically increases mineral starting level by +2
Priority: CRITICAL - Huge boost to gold and max spawn
Node 8: Gold Multiplier
Multiplies gold gain
Priority: MEDIUM - Good but Node 7 is better
Generation Amount
Increases runes generated per cycle
Priority: HIGH - Costs more but generates faster
🎯 Rune Upgrade Strategy
Early Runes (First 100-200 Runes)
Buy Moon Node 7 upgrade 2 times - MASSIVE boost to gold and spawn levels
Upgrade Sun Node 1 for VP if affordable
Some magnet upgrade if very cheap
Start investing in Generation Amount for both types
Community testing shows 2× Moon Node 7 early is extremely powerful and changes the game

Mid-Game Runes
Focus on Generation Amount upgrades for sustainable rune income
Continue leveling Sun Node 1 (VP) and Moon Node 7 (spawn level)
Buy magnet multiplier when affordable
Don't overspend - save runes for Special Minerals
Late-Game Runes
Max Generation Amount for both types
Continue upgrading Node 1 and Node 7
Unlock Refine Nodes 47 & 48 to speed up generation time
Farm runes for Special Mineral spawns
Refine Nodes for Runes
Node 47:
(450M RfP) Sun runes generation 1.2× faster
Node 48:
(550M RfP) Moon runes generation 1.2× faster (Currently bugged - affects both types)
Special Minerals (SPM)
Unlock Requirements
Node 41:
50,000,000 RfP - Unlocks Special Minerals spawning
Node 46:
200,000,000 RfP - Unlocks Special Minerals Progression (Base Level system)
Recommended:
Have at least 4-5 generation of Sun and Moon runes before buying SPM
⚠️ Important SPM Notes
Special Minerals persist through ALL prestiges - Polish AND Refine
Initial cost: 10 Sun Runes + 10 Moon Runes per spawn
Cost increases permanently the more you spawn (doesn't reset)
Cost also falls over time similar to regular minerals
Merging does NOT improve effects - Level 2 SPM = same bonus as 2× Level 1
Don't merge before Node 46! You need merges for progression
Grid starts at 3×3 - Node 44 increases up to 9×9 (6 levels)
The 9 Special Minerals
❤️ Red Gem
Effect: 3.00× VP Gain
Priority: HIGH - Direct VP multiplier
💛 Yellow Gem
Effect: Gold gain ×10
Priority: HIGH - Major gold boost
🤍 White Gem
Effect: Game speed +0.05× faster
Priority: HIGH - Speeds everything up
💙 Blue Gem
Effect: Cost Increase ^0.99
Priority: MEDIUM - Slows cost scaling
💚 Green Gem
Effect: Cost fall ^1.01
Priority: MEDIUM - Faster cost decrease
💗 Pink Gem
Effect: Luck and Quality +0.05
Priority: LOW - Zodiac farming only
💜 Dark Purple Gem
Effect: 1.50× PP gain
Priority: MEDIUM - Good PP boost
💠 Light Blue Gem
Effect: +×0.05 RfP Gain
Priority: MEDIUM-LOW - Small RfP boost
🧡 Orange Gem
Effect: 1.01× Magnet Chance
Priority: LOW - Minimal magnet boost
🎲 Secret 10th Special Mineral
There is actually a 10th Special Mineral (Black Gem) that cannot be spawned normally. It is unlocked much later through Refine Node 70 and has a 1% chance to spawn. It has unique mechanics related to the Tarot system.

🎯 SPM Strategy
Best SPM to Get
White Gem - Speeds up EVERYTHING (VP gen, rune gen, mineral spawns)
Yellow Gem - Massive gold boost for higher spawns
Red Gem - Direct VP multiplier
Others are less impactful but still provide bonuses
Early SPM Phase (Before Node 46)
Don't spawn too many - rune costs increase permanently
DO NOT MERGE! You need merges to count for Base Level progression
Focus on farming RfP and rune generation upgrades first
Only spawn if you have abundant runes (5+ generation amount)
After Node 46 (SPM Progression)
Now you can merge SPM to increase Base Level
100 merges needed to reach Base Level 2 for any SPM type
Base Level 2 = spawns at Level 2 (double effectiveness)
Requires 104 SPM of same color (1× Lv7 + 1× Lv6 + 2× Lv3)
Can increase SPM efficiency with RfP in progression menu
Achievement #320: The Final Achievement
Reach Base Level 2 for any Special Mineral. This is currently the final achievement in the game (as of v1.034). Requires approximately 800-928 SPM spawns (40,000-55,000 runes of each type). Takes 2-4 weeks of active play.

📊 Progression Timeline
Node 35 (20M):
Unlock Runes → Buy Moon Node 7 twice → Farm runes
Node 41 (50M):
Unlock SPM spawning → Wait until 4-5 rune generation
Node 46 (200M):
Unlock SPM Progression → Start merging for Base Level
Endgame:
Farm ~40k runes each → Spawn ~800 SPM → Get Base Level 2 → Achievement #320
✨ Why SPM Are Powerful
Never reset - Persist through Polish AND Refine
Permanent multipliers - Effects stack with all other bonuses
Compounding benefits - White Gem speeds rune generation which gets more SPM
Base Level scaling - Eventually spawn at Level 2+ for double/triple effectiveness
Efficiency upgrades - Can enhance effects with RfP in progression menu
Special Minerals represent the true endgame permanent power in the Minerals system. Every SPM you spawn makes all future runs faster and more powerful forever.

Strategy & Progression
📈 Complete Progression Overview
This guide provides a comprehensive roadmap through the entire Minerals system, from unlock to endgame. Follow this progression path for optimal efficiency and minimal grinding.

Phase 1: Early Minerals (Lvl 1-30)
Goal: Unlock Polish
Reach Mineral Level 30 to unlock the Polish prestige system. This is a linear grind with no shortcuts.

Key Strategies
Use Speed Build (Gemini/Cancer) most of the time
Switch to Gold Build (Pisces/Leo) when buying minerals and relics
Be VERY stingy with VP spending - don't buy upgrades > 1e12 VP
Focus on: Max Level, Fall Speed, Gold Gain only
Use Time Flux strategically to speed up cost fall
Continue buying relics and donut shop upgrades
Timeline
Several hours to a few days depending on activity level
Phase 2: Polish Era (Lvl 30-70)
Goal: Reach Mineral 70 & Unlock Refine
Grind Polish runs to level up weapons, especially Sword. Push for Mineral 70 to unlock Refine prestige.

Polish Priority
Sword to 10 - Huge VP boost + Autospawn (2.36 PP)
Sword to 20-25 - Continue for VP Factor milestone
Axe levels - Faster cost fall
Knuckles to 25 - Unlock Insane Trial #1 (62,358 PP)
Spear levels - Gold for higher spawn levels
Key Milestones
Mineral 30: First Polish - Max Sword immediately
Mineral 40-50: PP gains increase, can afford more weapon levels
Relic #23 (Atk 1500): Can now spend VP more freely on upgrades
Knuckles 25: Complete Insane Trial #1 (easy with Gold build)
Spear 50: Complete Insane Trial #2 (easy with Attack build)
Timeline
1-3 days of active play, can be done more AFK if desired
Phase 3: Early Refine (Lvl 70-100)
Goal: Build Refine Tree Foundation
Do MANY Level 70 Refine runs (1 RfP each) to build up early Refine nodes. Push to Level 80-90 for RfP spikes.

⚠️ The 1 RfP Grind
You'll be stuck at 1 RfP per run for a LONG time. This is normal! Do fast Level 70 runs, don't push higher until you have significant upgrades. It's not worth the time investment.

Refine Priority (Follow EXACTLY)
Node 1 (1 RfP) → Node 2 → Node 3 → Node 4 → Node 5
ALWAYS max Nodes 1, 4, 10 before buying others
Buy other nodes only if they cost < 50% of Nodes 1/4/10
Node 12 (64 RfP) - Game changer: VP upgrades don't spend VP
Node 18 (2,500 RfP) - CRITICAL: Unlocks Magnets!
Continue maxing Node 4 and 10
Node 24 (100,000 RfP) - Get 5 levels before continuing
Level 80/90 Strategy
Once you can reasonably reach Level 80 or 90, push for these milestones as they give BIG RfP increases:

Level 80: 4 RfP (first major spike)
Level 90: 20 RfP (massive spike)
Level 100: Even more RfP
After 90, every 10th level gives huge RfP increase
Timeline
2-4 days of grinding 1 RfP runs, then progression accelerates
Phase 4: Mid Refine (Lvl 100-140)
Goal: Unlock Node 29 & Build Magnet System
This is where progression really takes off. Node 29 is a game-changing upgrade that turbocharges everything.

Critical Path
Node 29 (2.5M RfP) - MASSIVE: Relic 23 boost based on Axe level
After Node 29, Node 24 becomes ultra powerful - prioritize it!
Do fast Mineral 130 runs (much faster than pushing 140)
Node 27 (1M RfP) - Spawn higher minerals (eventually needed for both routes)
Work toward Mineral 140 for next set of nodes
Magnet Farming
Build up minerals through Polish runs normally
Switch spawn to Level 1-15, enable Autospawn + Automerge
Farm magnets: Get "More Magnets" to ×3-5 first
Then max VP Gain Mult (buy at least 200 magnet cost upgrade)
Merge Level should reach ~30+ naturally
Polish Milestones to Push
Axe 100: Unlock IT4 (need prep) - 3.93e54 PP
Spear 100: Huge gold boost - 8.76e71 PP
Sword 100: Relic ×1.03 - 3.02e35 PP
Insane Trial #3
Knuckles 50: Unlocks IT3 - Hardest trial so far!

Do VERY long mineral buildup first
Max Common Exponent Mult VP upgrade
Use 6 Libra + 6 Taurus build
May need Enhance (Astrodust) for quality boost
Enable Auto Eternity for ~2 min at end if needed
Timeline
3-5 days - Progression much faster after Node 29
Phase 5: Late Refine (Lvl 140-170)
Goal: Get Node 34, 38 & Complete IT4-6
These nodes eliminate grinding and multiply RfP gains. Focus on the critical late-game nodes.

Priority Nodes
Node 31 (4M): Relic 14 decreases base mineral cost
Node 33 (10M): Get 100% Unity gold per second - no Unity needed!
Node 34 (12M): GAME CHANGER! Magnets don't spend + auto-buy (1hr farm → 2min)
Node 35 (20M): Unlocks Runes
Node 37 (16M): Knuckles 20% more effective (~doubles mineral speed)
Node 38 (24M): HUGE! RfP ×1.01^Knuckles (Knuckles 80 = ×2.21 RfP)
Insane Trials
IT4 (Axe 100): 6 Leo + 6 Virgo - Rebuild VP before starting! Finishes instantly with prep
IT5 (Bow 100): All Cancer - Easy! Removes Dilation requirement!
IT6 (Knuckles 100): 3 Aquarius/3 Libra/6 Aries OR All Pisces - Final trial
Rune Strategy
Moon Node 7 × 2: TOP PRIORITY - Spawn level +2
Invest in Generation Amount for both types
Sun Node 1 for VP when affordable
Don't spawn Special Minerals yet - need 4-5 generation first
Timeline
1-2 weeks - Major power spikes from nodes and trials
Phase 6: Endgame (170+ / Special Minerals)
Goal: Max Tree & Get Achievement #320
Complete the Refine Tree, farm Special Minerals, and work toward the final achievement.

Final Node Priorities
Nodes 36, 40, 24: MAX these - main late game progression
Node 41 (50M): Unlocks Special Minerals spawning
Node 45 (100M): PP from Trials much bigger (for Bow 100)
Node 46 (200M): Special Minerals Progression (Base Level system)
Node 50 (1e9): RfP gain ×2^lvl (max ×1,024) - Very expensive
Nodes 47/48 (450M/550M): Rune generation 1.2× faster
Special Minerals Path
Farm runes until 4-5 Generation Amount for both types
Unlock Node 41, start spawning SPM (prioritize White, Yellow, Red)
Don't merge before Node 46! Need merges for progression
Unlock Node 46 (200M RfP) - SPM Progression available
Farm ~800-928 SPM of ONE color (40k-55k runes each type)
Merge to reach 100 merge count = Base Level 2
Achievement #320 - Current final achievement!
Timeline
2-4 weeks for Achievement #320 (AFK friendly with rune generation)
💡 General Tips & Tricks
Progression Cycle
Attack Push (gain stages for relics)
Relic Upgrades & Sacrifice (permanent bonuses)
Mineral Push (higher level for more PP/RfP)
Attack Prestige (recover faster next cycle)
Repeat!
When to Refine vs Polish
Polish: When mineral level increase won't give more PP
Refine: When at RfP milestone (70, 80, 90, 100, etc.) or can buy important node
Rule of thumb: Don't Refine just for 1-2 more RfP unless at milestone
AFK Optimization
Enable Autospawn, Automerge, Autobuy after unlocking
Set spawn level low (1-15) for magnet/merge farming
Rune generation works 24/7 - very AFK friendly
After Node 34, magnet farming becomes instant
Special Minerals never reset - perfect for long AFK sessions
Common Mistakes to Avoid
❌ Spending VP >1e12 before Relic #23
❌ Buying Node 28 early (it's a trap!)
❌ Skipping Node 29 - it's THE most powerful node
❌ Spawning Special Minerals before 4+ rune generation
❌ Merging Special Minerals before Node 46
❌ Not maxing Nodes 1, 4, 10 first
❌ Pushing for higher mineral levels when 1 RfP runs are better
Know When You're Done
As of version 1.034, Achievement #320 (Special Mineral Base Level 2) is the final achievement. After completing all Insane Trials, maxing key nodes, and getting #320, you've beaten the Minerals endgame!

From there, you can continue optimizing, but the main progression is complete. Time to wait for the next update!

🎉 Congratulations!
If you've made it through this entire guide, you now have a complete roadmap for the Minerals system. Remember that while this guide provides an optimized path, Revolution Idle is about finding what works for you. Experiment, have fun, and enjoy the journey through one of the game's most complex and rewarding systems!

Runes

Instructions
•
Create a Fire loadout from your full Pisces loadout
•
Select Fire element in the Elements tab
•
Farm - 15 minutes to 2 hours (I often use time flux) 15-120 min
•
Watch for boosts slowing down - that's when to move on
•
Check Elemental Nodes - if you can buy one, farm a bit more before and after purchasing (you need those elements for stats gains from elements nodes)
Important: Early elements (before 1 million points) may not boost anything - this is normal!
Your goal will be at first to add 0,01 or 0,02 (Yeah, don't cry please) to your nodes boosts and then continue to the next step!
What Fire Boosts
Relic 26
Asc Power
Attack 3600+
Relic 31
Generation: Increasing your score increases fire/sec
Check and Upgrade
👁️
Watch Relics: Relic 26 and 31 for upgrades
👁️
Monitor Attack Prestige: Fire boosts should make it upgradeable
💎
Elemental Nodes: Check if any Fire nodes are purchasable

Switch back to full Pisces loadout
•

• At least 1e more Quality, OR
• Immortal 100+ higher, OR
• Ethereal 1+ higher Rarity

Step 13 of 13
Neutral
Final Activities & Loop
Final Optional Activities
Complete these optional activities before restarting the loop:

•
Farm Astrodust for 15 minutes to improve Donut stats15 min
•
Auto-Sacrifice farm for 10 minutes10 min
Do this once per +1 Ethereal Rarity or once per 100+ Immortal
•
Check Relics while using Pisces loadout (~10 min)
•
Check Refinement page for new nodes, upgrades, Polish Enhance, Runes
Final Checks
⭐
Relics: Check for buyable or sacrificeable relics
📊
Attack Prestige: Likely upgradeable now
🔧
Refinement: New nodes, Special Minerals, older node upgrades
✨
Polish Enhance: Check for available enhancements
🔮
Runes: Check for Rune upgrades
Ready to Loop!
Once you've completed all checks, click "Restart Guide" below to return to Step 1 and repeat the entire cycle!

Each loop makes you stronger as elements compound their benefits.

Key Tips to Remember
•
Always Unite at your maximum Attack Level
•
Elements before 1M points may not boost anything (normal)
•
Wind sometimes boosts nothing at all (normal for Wind)
•
Water boosts EVERYTHING - it's the most powerful element
•
After buying Elemental Nodes, re-farm that element
•
Follow the element order: Fire → Earth → Wind → Water

Guide:Secret Achievements
For normal achievements, see Achievements.
Secret Achievements are a special set of achievements located in the Secrets tab inside the Achievements menu.

There are currently 55 Secret Achievements in the game, and this guide will walk you through all of them.

Each Secret comes with a hint on how to obtain it, visible within the game while hovering over it. Some Secrets will also provide a Secret Bonus when unlocked, that acts as a hint to obtain a different Secret.

This guide provides step-by-step instructions on how to obtain each one.

The Secret Achievements are put in a content table so you can quickly find them.




Contents
1	1. Meet the Creators
2	2. Successful Promoter
3	3. Primary Progression
4	4. Lord of War
5	5. Straight to Infinity
6	6. Loyalty Rewarded

54	54. Jackpot!
55	55. Werewolf
1. Meet the Creators
To obtain this Achievement, simply go to the credits tab and click on either of the developer's logos on the top left. This will earn you the secret!

2. Successful Promoter
To obtain this Achievement, you must buy [10;1] from the Infinity Tree to unlock auto-promotions.

Once purchased, head to the Automations tab and in the "Automate Promote" section, click the "add" button 50 times (if you lose count, just spam it just like you spammed max buy before infinity).

Once you have unlocked the Achievement, you can delete them and setup proper auto-promotions.

3. Primary Progression
To obtain this Achievement, you must complete the 8th Eternity milestone (reaching 14 Eternities) to unlock Autobuy Generators.

Once done, head to Automations and set your automate buyables, ascension, and generators to only buy Red, Blue, and Green.

4. Lord of War
To obtain this Achievement, you must complete animals and unlock Dilation upgrades.

Once unlocked, set every bulk buy button (Yes, every single one) to "Max". These are located within the revolution attacks, infinity generators, animal points, laboratory and dilation tabs.

5. Straight to Infinity
For this achievement, you must have some progress in Eternity so it can be completed.

Once you are done with progressing, head to the Infinity Tree tab and buy only the first row of infinity upgrades [x;1] all the way up to [21;1]. Do NOT buy any other upgrades than those along the top row.

Here is the list of the upgrades you must only buy: [1;1],[2;1],[3;1],[4;1],[5;1],[6;1],[7;1],[8;1],[9;1],[10;1],[11;1],[12;1],[13;1],[14;1],[15;1],[16;1],[17;1],[18;1],[19;1],[20;1],[21;1].

6. Loyalty Rewarded
Claim the daily rewards 14 times. You don’t have to do the daily collections in a row.

7. Da Vinci Code
Go to the Shop, click “Add Code” and type “unity”, then press add.

8. Mad Scientist
Go to the help tab, a smaller version of the revolution is shown. Simply stay in the tab and let it run until it reaches 1 billion (1e9) score. This will take roughly 40 minutes.

Do NOT leave the tab, it will reset the Secret Achievement progress.

9. Out of Time
This Achievement unlocks after not using Time Flux or Offline Flux for 7 days straight. In this instance, 7 days refers to a calendar week, not ingame time.

You can still buy TF Upgrades using TF during that time.

10. The Seven Relics
To obtain this Achievement, you must gain 8 Eternity Points for Eternity Milestone 6 and unlock Eternity Challenges (If you are wondering why, it's because you wouldn’t have access to the needed tabs).

Once done, you have to go into multiple scrollable areas and scroll past the limit to find 7 icons. These are located in Automation, Credits, Generators, Animals, Shop, Eternity-Milestones-Challenges and Help-Achievements.

Try scrolling/dragging up, down, left and right whenever it doesn't appear.

11. You thought this was a cookie clicker game?
Press the center of the red circle in the Revolution page a total of 100 times.

12. Enigma
Go to the Time Flux tab, click the game speed buttons x1, x2 and x3 in the following order: 2, 2, 1, 3, 1, 2, 2, 1, 3, 3, 2, 2, 2, 1, 2, 2, 2, 1, 2, 1

13. Revolutionary Baby
Have 25 hours total playtime. Ingame time boosts do not count. This is 1 day and 1 hour.

14. Hypnotized
Have 250 hours total playtime. Ingame time boosts do not count. This is 10 days and 10 hours.

15. The Age of Wisdom
Have 1000 hours total playtime. Ingame time boosts do not count. This is 41 days and 16 hours.

16. Weekly Warrior
Log in for 7 days in a row. You can set an alarm for a daily reminder. Please be aware that missing 1 day resets the progress.

If you feel worried about missing a day, you can wait until you collect the daily reward.

17. Two-Week Streak
Log in for 14 days in a row. You can set an alarm for a daily reminder. Please be aware, that missing 1 day resets the progress.

If you feel worried about missing a day, you can wait until you collect the daily reward.

18. Unwavering Loyalty
Log in for 30 days in a row. You can set an alarm for a daily reminder. Please be aware, that missing 1 day resets the progress.

If you feel worried about missing a day, you can wait until you collect the daily reward.

19. Circle of Infinity
To get this Achievement, buy [15;1] from the Infinity Tree to unlock auto-infinity.

Once purchased, set the Automate Infinity Time(s) to 6.28.

20. Chemically Stupid
To get this Achievement, you must have 7 animals (Animals Milestone 3).

You can buy the cheapest animals to make this easier. Once purchased, head to the newly unlocked laboratory tab and set the custom amount of bulk buy to be one of the following: 1, 5, 10.

Once changed, select the custom button to buy the upgrade on any of the options.

21. Carnivore Instinct
You must have 3 Eternity Points to unlock the animals tab (Eternity Milestone 3).

Once unlocked, respec without having any animals bought (to respec, click the 2 arrows circling around each other).

22. Eat the Cookie
You must have Secret Achievement 11. Head to the Secret Achievements tab and spam click the cookie in Secret Achievement 11 until the achievement triggers.

23. Intermittently solid
Before going for this Achievement, you must have at least a few infinities.

- Alternate between a solid and non-solid circle

(Red solid, Orange not solid, Yellow solid, etc)

- Disable all automations and infinite, then buy 5 of each circle.

- Buy max of red, yellow, turquoise, blue and pink - it should give you the achievement.

- If it doesn't work, put some slowdowns on it.

- To get more slowdowns, keep buying circles.

24. Worldwide revolution
You must have certain progress in Unity to be able to attempt this Achievement.

- Disable all automations manually, This doesn’t work if you have disabled them using the "disable all" button.

- Start a new Unity.

- Enter Eternity Challenge 10-5.

- Enter Dilation.

- Enter Infinity Challenge 9

- Press the Infinite button.

Late-Game step by step guide:

- In case you have too much in game progression, you should start Insane Trial 2 and remove all your zodiacs (make sure you have disabled auto sell/auto sacrifice).

- Disable all automations manually, This doesn’t work if you have disabled them using the "disable all" button.

- Start a new Unity, and progress all the way to Eternity Challenge 10-5. If you are stuck on Eternity Challenge 10-4, buy Dilation Upgrade 1 in increments of 1 until you have enough to finish the Eternity Challenge 10-4 (Keep an eye on the score in the top right corner, you need e308 to finish Eternity Challenge 10-4).

- You can max buy all the other Dilation upgrades, max the Dilation Tree and buy bunch of Supernovas (if you have too much in game progression, avoid this until necessary).

- Start Eternity Challenge 10-5.

- Enter Dilation.

- Go to Revolution screen, do couple of Infinities for Infinity Points.

- Buy Infinity Upgrades until you reach 7;1, aka Infinity Challenges.

- Start Infinity Challenge 9.

- Go back to the Revolution Screen and check if you can infinite, if you can’t, buy more of Dilation Upgrade 1 but really slowly. After each purchase, check the revolution screen to see if you can infinite.

25. That Wasn’t an exploit..
Head to the Time Flux tab, and spam click offline flux tick-box (the small white box). Toggle it on and off.

If it feels like it's not working, keep spamming it. You will need to have below 50% Time Flux for this to work.

26. Busted Cheater
You must have unlocked the Dilation Tree by completing all the Eternity Challenges (Milestone 8 reward).

Copy the loadout below this message: (Ctrl+C after highlighting)

C5;T1,5,5,5;M5,5,5,5;B10,5,5,5

Go to the Dilation Tree tab, click the 3 dots and 3 lines button to the bottom right, click import and then click load.

27. Not yet Elon
To get this Achievement, you must have unlocked the Mercury planet in Unity.

Click on the Mercury planet in the Astrology tab and a rocket will appear. Click on the rocket.

28. Astrology expert
Before doing this Achievement, you must have all 12 planets.

Assign the zodiacs in this order:



Sun	Merc	Venus	Moon	Mars	Jupiter	Saturn	Uranus	Nept	Pluto	Chiron	Fortune
Aries	Tau	Gem	Canc	Leo	Virgo	Libra	Scorp	Sagit	Capri	Aqua	Pisc


29. Business first
Sell an immortal level zodiac.

30. Access locked
Lock all trials (Easy, Medium, and Hard).

If you have unlocked Minerals, wait until you unlock all Insane Trials and then re-attempt.

Remember to unlock them all when you're done!

31. Scatterbrained
To be able to do this Achievement, you must have completed Easy Trial 6.

Have a spare zodiac in your inventory, open the planet shop, put the zodiac in the "sell" box, then close the planet shop without putting the zodiac back into your inventory.

32. Flash the headlights
You need to encode BANANA in Morse code by opening and closing the stats popup on the revolutions page (button looks like a pie chart with a brown background). The image below shows the morse code for BANANA.

If you don’t know what Morse code is, it's encoding words as a series of dots and dashes, or beeps of various lengths. in this case, the image below shows it as dots and dashes, and the game will play it to you as beeps, a short beep for a dot and a long beep for a dash.


Banana code.png b a n a n a

To enter morse, you click the stats popup button (brown button that looks like a pie chart). **YOU MUST START WITH THE POP-UP CLOSED**. The length of time the popup is open for determines whether you enter a dash or a dot, so to do a dash you click to open the popup, wait at least a second, then click to close it. to enter a dot, simply double click to open and close it quickly.

You need to go relatively fast when entering the dots and dashes for a single letter, and when you finish a letter, leave the popup closed until the game beeps the combination you just entered back at you. after it does that, you can wait as long as you like before starting the next letter.

Once you enter the sixth letter, if you typed BANANA correctly it will unlock the achievement. If you didn't spell it correctly, it will make an incorrect noise. If at any point you enter more than 4 dots or dashes for a single letter, the letter will be invalid and ignored by the game, resulting in no beeps being played back at you after you leave the popup closed.

If you want a visual example, here is a video guide: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tw4KgySlOnw

33. Boosted Banana
You must have completed Secret Achievement 32 for you to be able to attempt this Secret Achievement.

Go to the shop, go to skins and equip the Banana skin. Go to the revolution screen and click the banana 100,000 times. Yes... it’s a lot.

34. White mining rabbit
Once you have Mineral 89 (Chronorium), drag it onto the Time Flux tab.

If you are a mobile user, you will have to do few extra steps:

- Head to Options.

- Go to Display.

- Click Hide tabs and hide all tabs but Unity.

- Head to Minerals tab and drag the Mineral 89 on the Time Flux logo.

- Go back into options and re-enable the hidden tabs.

35. Stone breaker
Before attempting this Achievement, make sure you can spawn Mineral 100.

Head to Automations > Unity > Autobuy Mineral Upgrades, and turn everything off (or just Chance to spawn mineral with Lv+1), Refine once to clear out the existing levels.

Manually spawn minerals from 1-100 and destroy them. You don’t have to do it in order, and you don’t have to have them all spawned at the same time before destroying either.

36. Infinity stones
It is recommended not to do this achievement until the next reset layer is present or you have all the achievements so you don’t have progression problems.

Destroy Special Minerals in this order: Dark Purple, Blue, Red, Orange, Green, Yellow

Fun fact: This Secret Achievement is reference to the order of obtaining the infinity stones by Thanos.

37. Titan’s fall
You need Secret Achievement 36 before attempting this.

Sacrifice relic 9 (Midas hand).

(Often asked question is if there is time limit to this, No, there isn’t, you can do it even after the Universe destroys itself and it will count.)

Fun Fact: This Secret Achievement is another Marvels reference to the infinity gauntlet being destroyed after collecting all the infinity stones.

38. Solar Eclipse
Go to Minerals > Refine > Runes Upgrades tab. Click the Sun icon and allow it to spin 4 times, then click it again to stop the rotation. Then click the moon icon and allow it to spin 7 times, then click it again to stop the rotation. The icons have to complete a full rotation and return to its starting position for the achievement to count. You have a 30° window to stop the rotation.

You can spin them both at the same time, it's not required to be 1 by 1.

39. Newton’s theory
Go to the refinement page, click the nodes in the order 9, 8, 1. An apple will appear in the tree, click on the apple to obtain the achievement.

40. Welcome to the Team
1: Turn off automations for Autobuy Animal Points and Supernova. (keep autobuy animals on)

2: Complete a Unity reset using button in trials.

3: You should just have -1 AP, :P (go to eternity tab)

4: Click on the "-1 AP".

- Alternative solution: If you can't hit -1 AP and you have 60 AP base put the 4 animals into auto buy animals  : Cat, Giraffe, Fox, Panther to autobuy and remove the rest.

- Improvising solution: if your base is not 60, take your total base and add 1, and set the auto buy animals to the total of it (including the 1 so its negative), example: if your Animal points base is 50, set the auto buy animals to take total of 51 ap.

41. The Big Bang Theory
Leave the game 50 times. (It's recommended to use the "Save and Close" button, and wait at least 15 seconds before re-opening the game.)

If you lose count just keep doing it. The Secret Achievement will eventually trigger.

42. Marathoner
Have the game open for 3 hours without closing it.

43. Iron Will
Have the game open for 6 hours without closing it.

44. Nerd Commander
Have the game open for 9 hours without closing it.

45. Apollo 11
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement, you must have decent progress in the Tarots tab.

Go to the Elements tab → Element Tree and activate the following Elemental nodes (numerically):

Fire: 1
Earth: 9
Wind: 6
Water: 9
Yeah, this Secret Achievement is reference to the Moon Landing.

46. Silence
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement, you must have the Tarot Upgrades in the Tarot Tab unlocked.

Go to the Tarot tab → Tarot Upgrades and spam the arrows until the Secret Achievement pops up.

47. Not very smart
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement you must have the Smart Man (relic 38) unlocked and the Autobuy for relics.

Go to the Automations tab > Unity Automations > Autobuy Relics and move Smart man (relic 38) and put him first on the list, then disable him.

48. Agent Smith
Go to the Revolution page, click the blue icon on the top right (the leaderboard) and edit your username to be Smith or Agent Smith.

49. Deal with a Devil
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement you must have The Devil Arcan card from the Tarot tab.

This Secret Achievement is RNG, best way to do is by stacking up a lot of Time Flux and activating The Devil, if he didn't take your soul, use Time Flux to skip the Cooldown Time.

Warning: If you have just began Tarots recently, it’s recommended to avoid focusing on this Secret Achievement due to the annoying debuffs.

50. Positive Debuff
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement you must have The Devil and Wheel of Fortune Arcana cards from the Tarot tab.

This Secret Achievement is RNG, you need to lose the gambling from Wheel of Fortune and have negative draws, and then get the debuff from the devil that reduces your Tarot resouces and draws by 6,66%.

It's called Positive Debuff because The Devil formula makes your negative draw closer to 0 instead of even deeper into debt.

Warning: It is recomended to start getting this secret achievement between the mid game Tarot and before buying RN 83. If you start too early, you can face the annoying debuffs from the wheel that takes days to recover and starting too late means that the wheel has a 1% chance to lose, meaning you have to wait longer to get a lose than before buying RN83.

51. Catch me!
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement you must have The Devil Arcana card from the Tarot tab.

This Secret Achievement is RNG, best way to do is by stacking up a lot of Time Flux and activating The Devil, if he didn't spawn the Chadium mineral, use Time Flux to skip the Cooldown Time, but if he did spawn the Chadium, catch it by clicking him.

Warning: If you have just began Tarots recently, it’s recommended to avoid focusing on this Secret Achievement due to the annoying debuffs.

52. My username in the credits
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement you must have Ach #001.

This Secret Achievement is straightforward, go to the Credits tab and click on the pink highlighted “Alex” on the testers. Type your username to get that Secret Achievement. If you don’t know your username, click on the leaderboard as the top right on the Revolution tab and you will see it. If your username is not memorable, change it to a memorable one.

53. Fortune Breaker
Enter The Fool Tarot Challenge to get a black gem, then go to the Wheel of Fortune challenge and destroy the black gem.

54. Jackpot!
Lose 500 times in the wheel of fortune tarot challenge. (This gives a 90.9% win chance, hence the hint for this secret)

55. Werewolf
To be able to attempt this Secret Achievement, you must have some progression in the astrology tab.

This Secret Achievement is straightforward, go to the Unity tab between 12:00 AM and 1:00 AM and click the moon planet.

(Note: Changing your computer time will also work, so that is recommended if you aren't awake during these hours.)

Achievements

Secret Achievements
Unlock all 51 hidden achievements and maximize your Time Flux bonuses

What are Secret Achievements?
Secret Achievements are hidden challenges in Revolution Idle with obscured unlock conditions. There are currently 51 Secret Achievements in the game, each providing valuable rewards when completed.

🏆
51
Total Secrets
⚡
+5.1%
Max TF Boost
💎
~6000
Total Souls
Key Information
✓
+0.1% Time Flux per Secret:
Each Secret Achievement unlocked adds +0.1% to your Time Flux gain, making offline time more effective.

💡
Hints Available In-Game:
Hover over locked Secrets in the Achievements menu to see cryptic hints. This guide provides full solutions.

🔗
Some Secrets Unlock Others:
Certain Secrets provide hints for others (e.g., #36 must be completed before #37). Check the notes!

⏱️
Time-Gated Achievements:
Several Secrets require real-world time: daily logins, playtime milestones, or long AFK sessions.

🎲
RNG-Based Secrets:
Late-game Tarot Secrets (#49-51) rely on RNG and can take multiple attempts with The Devil card.

⚠️
Spoiler Warning

This guide contains full solutions for all Secret Achievements. If you prefer discovering them yourself, only scroll to the specific Secrets you need help with!
All Secret Achievements (1-51)
Meet the Creators
#001
Meet the Creators
Reward
+100 Souls
💡 Hint
Explore the game's credits.

✓ Solution
Go to the Credits tab and click on either of the developer's logos on the top left.

Reward
+100 Souls

Boost your strategy by automation.

✓ Solution
Accumulate 50 auto-promoters. You can spam the add button to reach 50, then delete them after unlocking.

Stick to the basics.

✓ Solution

Reward
+100 Souls
💡 Hint
Push your firepower to the max.

✓ Solution
Set EVERY buy button to 'Max'. This includes Revolution, Generators, Animal Points, Laboratory, and Dilation upgrades. The last buy buttons are unlocked in Dilation.

#005

Reward
+15:00 TF
💡 Hint
Find the right path to the stars.

✓ Solution
Turn OFF Infinity Tree autobuyer if unlocked. On the Infinity Tree, purchase ONLY the top row upgrades [X;1] all the way to Stars at [21;1]. Automation upgrades don't count.

Reward
+150 Souls

A parallel revolution?

✓ Solution
In the Help tab, a miniature version of the revolution is shown. Stay in the tab and let it run until it reaches 1e9 (1 billion) score. Takes about 40 minutes.

+30:00 TF

Stay out of time.

Seek out the cursed objects hidden in the shadows.

✓ Solution
Scroll past the limit in these 7 scrollable areas to find hidden icons: Automation, Credits, Generators, Animals, Shop, Eternity Challenges, Help-Achievements. Icons disappear once found.

This one is solid, maybe that one isn't?

2. Enter Eternity Challenge 10-5
3. Enter Dilation
4. Enter Infinity Challenge 9
5. Press the Infinity button

Flash the Headlights
#032
Flash the Headlights
Reward
Banana Skin
💡 Hint
Hold... and release.

✓ Solution
Encode "BANANA" in Morse code using the Stats button on the Revolution page. START WITH POPUP CLOSED.
B: -... (dash dot dot dot)
A: .- (dot dash)
N: -. (dash dot)
A: .- (dot dash)
N: -. (dash dot)
A: .- (dot dash)

Short click = dot, Long hold = dash. Wait for beep confirmation between letters.

3. Start a new Unity via Trials
4. Click the -1 AP indicator in the Eternity tab

Positive Debuff
#050
Positive Debuff
Reward
+300 Souls
💡 Hint
When negative becomes positive.

✓ Solution
Get negative draws from Wheel of Fortune gambling, then get The Devil's debuff that reduces Tarot resources/draws by 6.66%. The Devil's formula makes your negative draws closer to 0 instead of deeper into debt.
Catch Me!
#051
Catch Me!
Reward
+250 Souls
💡 Hint
Quick reflexes required.

✓ Solution
Activate The Devil card until he spawns the Chadium mineral (flying head). Click it quickly before it disappears. Use Time Flux to skip cooldowns for multiple attempts.
Tips for Secret Hunters
🎯
Start with Easy Wins:
Secrets #1-7 are quick and accessible early. Get these for immediate Souls and TF boosts.

📅
Set Daily Login Reminders:
Secrets #6, #16-18 require consecutive logins. Set phone alarms to avoid losing streaks!

💾
Keep the Game Running:
Secrets #42-44 need 3, 6, and 9 hours of continuous playtime. Leave the game open overnight on a device.

⚠️
Avoid Tarot Secrets Too Early:
Secrets #49-51 involve The Devil card with harsh debuffs. Wait until you have strong Tarot progress and plenty of Time Flux.

🔄
Check Progress Regularly:
Some Secrets (like #8, #10, #32) have multi-step processes. Keep track of where you are!

💡
Estimated Completion Time

Completing all 51 Secret Achievements takes approximately 40-60 days of active play due to time-gated requirements (30-day login streak, 1000 hours playtime, etc.). Focus on the non-time-gated Secrets first to build up your bonuses while waiting!

Revolution Idle Guide
A comprehensive guide for Revolution Idle. Master your progression from Infinity to Unity with optimal strategies for Eternity, Dilation, and Element builds.
