Revolution Idle Unity Guide
Revolution Idle Unity Guide.jpg
Unity is available after Unity reset which is available at 1.08e2.466 EP in Eternity after you are done with Dilation (around 45 DTP).

When you perform Unity reset you will be able to pick one of 4 Zodiacs. On your first Unity you will be guaranteed the option of a Lv3 Rare Revolution Idle Aries.pngAries with 2 buffs: common exponent and mult gain. This is the best Zodiac to pick for your first Unity, after this your Zodiac options and their buffs will be randomized. Pick the one which gives you the most stats/higher rarity. Upon Unity Reset game progress resets and you will have to start from the scratch, but you will have Unity's Bonuses to help you out. Basic bonuses from the first reset are:

IP gain: x4
EP gain: x2
Eternities gain: x2
DP gain: x2
On top of that you get your equipped Zodiac's bonuses, that should give you a good boost.

Now you have to go through all game phases again to reach the next Unity and get additional Zodiac. It takes anywhere from 1-2 days to a week or more depending on the strategy, feel free to use previous Revolution Idle Guides to remind you of different strategies for each game stage.

Unity Mechanics
There are 3 Main Unity mechanics, the first "Astrology" is located in the first Unity tab. You get an option to pick one of 4 random Zodiacs on the Unity reset based on the Chance. You get all your Zodiacs in the Inventory and in order to get it's bonuses you have to equip it in one of the planet slots. By default, you will have Sun and will unlock Revolution Idle Mercury.pngMercury upon placing your first Zodiac in the Revolution Idle Sun.pngSun slot. Be careful, since assigning or removing a zodiac to a planet will trigger a Unity reset. If you forgot how to play the earlier stages feel free to rely on Ultimate Beginners Guide, Eternity Guide, Zoo and Animals Guide, and the Dilation Guide most things will be the same, just faster and easier.

Unity Walkthrough
Unity walkthrough is different from other progression layers, because Zodiacs are random and your early Unity progression might be different. Because of that specific milestones and requirements for other parts of the Unity, like Unity Trials have to be adjusted to each player depending on their progression, Zodiacs, Relics etc. Basics stay the same though and there is overall strategy and direction you can take to progress more effectively and don't get stuck.

You can use this REVO Youtube Playlist to follow all the unity progression, it's split in multiple episodes, so can't embed them in the article.

Here is a Unlimate Unity Video guide that will allow you to grasp the Unity mechanics:


Early Unities take a while and heavily dependent on the Zodiac RNG, it's ok that they make take days and weeks, later on things will get faster. It's essential to pick the right Zodiac upon Unity Reset, and early on you want to balance between 2 strategies:

Getting Red Zodiacs (preferably Revolution Idle Aries.pngAries with Mult Gain stat) to progress Easy Unity Trials faster
Getting Red/Light Blue Zodiacs for faster Unity runs.
Since early Unities take a long time it makes sense to do a reroll of the available Unity Zodiacs, it might give you a better option, but it costs Souls and you won't be able to revert it, so use it only if your option are really bad. Note that the value of rerolling will go down as you progress thru unity and the Unity's become faster.

Good Red Zodiac stats to aim for in the early game:

Mult Gain
Slowdown Power
Once you will get 3 Red Zodiacs you can attempt your first Easy Trials, start with Easy Trials #2 as that one is the easiest, most of the times it can be completed with a single Revolution Idle Aries.pngAries that you get at the start, but it's better to get more red Zodiacs to make it faster. Refer to Unity Trials Guide for more detailed info.

Completing your first Trial will unlock Moon and you will be able to slot more Zodiacs for additional multipliers and progress faster.

You will also unlock EXP factor that increases Unity Reset Zodiac Level, which gives it more stats. Those exp factors are multiplicative, so they boost each other a lot.

After you beat Easy Trials 2 and 1 you can attempt other trials, if you fail just keep farming Unities. Unity bonuses will allow you to go through Unity faster even if you get bad Zodiacs. After a unity run rearrange your zodiacs and give a try to a trial, rinse and repeat until you beat Easy Trials.

Once you beat all Easy trials you get access to several Factors that will allow you to go for higher level zodiacs. Keep in mind that exp factors are based on current values, so Max Score is Max Score this unity. Also, Prestige and Promotes will reset nothing at this point even at the new unity. Use this to your advantage and put proper Automation options and boost your progression and farming speed. You should have around 7-13 Unities by now that will allow you to push further.

You will also unlock #Planet Shop where you can sell zodiacs you don't want. It doesn't have a lot of effect on the progression early on, but might worth checking out, especially as you start getting too many useless Zodiacs.

Now your goal is to prepare for Medium Trials. Try to get Zodiacs with + Lab Level bonuses and ^^ SN Req to beat the medium trials, you can always try to beat the trials, if it's too slow and not working - come back and farm a bit more. At this point Unities will take way less time and their bonuses will start stacking up, helping you to progress faster.

Once you beat all the Medium trials it's time to get ready for Hard Trials. Get ready by farming DP Gain Zodiacs and refer to the Hard Trials Guide for more info on specific trials. Getting trials 1,2,3 will be relatively easy, you should be able to do them all without too much issues if you get proper Zodiacs. Trials 4,5,6 are way more complex and will require specific approach and preparations.

Trial 3 is important milestone that allows you to fully automate Unities using Macros. It might feel complicated at first, but with the help of Marco Guide you should be able to master most important macros for your progression.

Also completing 15 Trials unlocks #Attacks - the new progression layer which will be extremely important in your late game progression.

To deal with Trials 4+ you will need progression with Planet Shop, Attacks/Relics and Achievements 200, 201. They will all drastically improve your farming speed, multipliers and make you stronger.

Once you beat hard trials, you will need to focus on Attacks and getting Achievements 230-233. Your final goal will be pushing to Achievement 240 and unlocking Minerals.

Planet Shop
Planet shop is unlocked at finishing Easy Trials and being used for selling and merging Zodiacs, and spending Astrodust from sold zodiacs for various upgrades.

The proper upgrade priority is:

Zodiac Exp. Factor - will allow you to get higher lvl zodiacs, essential in the early game
Quality - make new zodiacs stronger, but bonuses aren't that significant early on
Luck - Early upgrades won't give you much, so it's useful mostly in late game
Game Speed - Nice place to spend extra Astrodust, but bonuses aren't that crazy early on and getting better zodiacs is more important
Other than that, you can Merge, Enhance, Redistribute and Sacrifice Zodiacs:

Merging combines 3 zodiacs of the same element to get a higher rarity one. Getting similar Zodiacs will result in the same sign on the merge.
Enhancing boosts zodiac's quality at a specific chance. I found it pretty useless, because the bonuses are slim and don't worth it.
Redistribution - rerolls zodiac. Might be useful to change zodiac to another one in the same element.
Sacrifice - the most useful mechanic that adds passive stat bonuses based on the 1st zodiac stat bonus. Has diminishing returns, but very powerful.
Attacks
Attacks is a whole new level of gameplay and progression that is unlocked upon finishing 15 Trials. You will fill attack circles based on your color speed. Damage is calculated differently in the attacks: it uses base damage that you can see at the top of the screen which is based on your Score and multipliers and it's being multiplied by specific color Damage Multiplier.

Currently there are 5 colors available in the attacks, each one will have separate multipliers, speed etc. You can upgrade and ascend them just like normal colors, but it costs Gold.

Gold is a special currency that is being awarded upon infinity based on the Attacks progression. You can spend it either on the Color Upgrades or on Relics.

You can refer to Attacks Guide for more information.

Relics
Relics are progression layer located in the Unity tab, it provides multiple relics that unlocks as you progress max Attack lvl and boost various gameplay aspects.

It's better to focus on the relics that are useful for the current goals, like pushing the attacks, getting better zodiacs or farming more gold.

When all the relics are unlocked it's advised to start upgrades on Relic #14 Palate Expander, since it decreases costs of all relics. It's also better to wait until you reach high attack level in the current Unity (close to max) before buying the relics, since it will be cheaper.

You will need to beat specific Attacks milestones to unlock new relics. Usually unlocked relics are pretty expensive and it will require a bit more of progress to actually buy them.

Keep in mind that relic upgrades have diminishing returns, so first levels of relics will provide more stat boosts than higher levels. It doesn't matter for most cases, since you will buy all available relics anyway, but it's nice to keep it in mind.

Minerals
Minerals tab offers LOADS of new mechanics to the game, it's own progression layer, unique prestige mechanics and variety of resources and multipliers.

The main idea is that you spawn minerals using Gold and those minerals generate Value Points (VP) that can be used to buy variety of upgrades. Early on those upgrades affec mostly minerals themselves, but Luck and Quality bonuses directly boost Zodiacs spawn quality. Gold Gain helps a lot with Relics and Common Exponent Mult... well, it's there.

Also based on your max Value Points you will get multipliers to Attacks M.Gain and Asc. Power making them A LOT stronger. This in turn allows you to push attacks that will generate more gold, meaning better relics, meaning better progression.

Minerals have Polish Prestige, that resets current minerals and VP, but keeps your purchased upgrades in the Minerals tab. You get Polish Prestige (PP ^^) that you can spend to Polish 5 types of weapons that provide multipliers and unlock bonuses and trials upon specific milestones.

Next prestige layer in Minerals in Refine. When you do Refine Prestige you lose all Minerals progress (other than Special Minerals and milestones for Polished Weapons) and get Refine Points for that. Refine Points (RfP) can be used to buy refine nodes on the refine tree (sort of skill tree with a very long progression). You can also spend them for Polishing Enchance, that makes your Weapons a bit more effective, Rune Upgrades and Special Minerals upgrades (makes your Special Minerals a bit better).

Refine Tree unlocks many game mechanics that will be necessary later on, such as:

Merge Level (node 5)
Magnets (node 18)
Polishing Enchance (node 28)
Runes and Runes Upgrades(node 35)
Special Minerals (node 41)
Special Minerals Progression (node 46)
Elements (node 55)
Tarot (node 70)
You can refer to Minerals Guide for more information.

Runes
Runes are sepearate enitity in Refine Tree. You get Runes avery specific amount of time and can spend them for Rune Upgrades or Spawning Special Minerals. At first they generate very slowly and there aren't many ways to increase rune generation speed. You can spend RfP to lower the generation time and get them faster, spend runes to get more runes per cycle or boost local game speed to speed process up.

Runes timers run during the offline time warps, so it's possible to get them by using that, but overall there is no easy way to speed up the process.

Special Minerals
Special minerals reside in their separate minerals tab. They are spawned using the Runes and provide variety of awesome bonuses for gameplay. Spawning Special Mineral raises the price which will eventually go down to Base Cost through Cost Fall. Main issue with Special Minerals is that Base Cost gets more and more expensive as your SM Spawned Factor increases. Basically more runes you have spawned - more expensive it will be to spawn new ones.

Runes can be merged, but unlike normal runes it doesn't provide any stat bonuses, only saves space in the Special Runes grid.

They can be autospawned later, and automation options for Runes autospawn can be set so their autospawn will work only when cost reached Base Cost. It seems to be the best option, since Runes generation is the common bottleneck in Rune farming.

Special Runes Progresson increases Base Special Mineral Spawn level when you spawn sufficient amount of Special Minerals of the same type. You can also improve Special Minerals efficiency using RfP here.

Elements
Elements tab contains 4 elements which are generated over time, affected by the Local Game Speed.

You can select only one of the elements to generate, it's impossible to generate all 4 (at least early on). Each Element will require special Zodiac Build to farm them effectively. You can find all the Builds on Elements Zodiac Build Page.

Element generation is based on Global Element Mult (that you can see at the bottom of the screen) multiplied at Power Factor 1 for Element (node 1 of Every Element Tree) and multiplier from specific Stat that Element is based on.

Early on the gains will be very low and won't provide any significant bonuses, but it's important to check your gains from time to time and push them to higher values.

In the Element Tree you can spend Elements to get various bonuses. Most of the bonuses scale with their Current Element Value, so there is a point to grind elements even if you can't afford a new tree node. Just keep in mind that node bonuses scaling might be a bit disappointing early on.

Small tip: When you are farming Minerals or anything else with your Game Speed build full of Pisces make sure to set Water element, even though the gains might feel tiny, it will be more efficient than any other element.

Revolution Idle Zodiacs and Planets Guide
Revolution Idle Zodiacs and Planets Guide.jpg
Zodiacs are essential part of Unity progression. They can be equipped to Planets to get specific stat multiplier boosts and acquired by performing the Unity reset.

Check out other Revolution Idle Guides for additional information and strategy tips.

You can pick one of 4 Zodiacs upon Unity reset or reroll for another set of Zodiacs. Try to utilize various Exp Factors to increase Zodiac lvl prior to performing Unity reset.

Explaining the Zodiacs
Zodiacs are pretty complex, so require a breakdown to understand how they work.

Each Zodiac has its Element and Season. Those are set per zodiac and never change. Seasons effect the Planet bonuses and Element kind of shows which bonuses you will get.

Each Zodiac can get up to 4 bonuses. Early zodiacs have 2 bonuses, legendary-mythic rarity gives 3, while godly+ gives all 4 bonuses. Bonuses you get depend on the Zodiac. Some Zodiacs will get one of the bonuses all the time.

In the following table there is a full list of zodiacs, with their potential bonuses. BOLD bonuses means that this is unique bonus for this specific Zodiac, it's not guaranteed to show up though.

Zodiacs, Elements, Seasons and their Bonuses
Zodiac Name	Element	Season	Potential Bonus 1	Potential Bonus 2	Potential Bonus 3	Potential Bonus 4

Revolution Idle Leo.pngLeo	Fire	Summer	Lap speed	Promotion Power	Ascension Power	Common exp.Power
Revolution Idle Sagittarius.pngSagittarius	Fire	Winter	Lap speed	Slowdown Power	Ascension Power	Common exp.Power
Revolution Idle Gemini.pngGemini	Wind	Summer	Eternity Gain	EP Gain	SN Req	Lab mult Power
Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra	Wind	Autumn	DP Gain	EP Gain	SN Req	Lab mult Power
Revolution Idle Aquarius.pngAquarius	Wind	Winter	DP Gain	Free Lab Lvs	SN Req	Lab mult Power
Revolution Idle Cancer.pngCancer	Water	Summer	DTP Cost	Center DTU Effect	Ach. Reward	Game Speed
Revolution Idle Scorpio.pngScorpio	Water	Autumn	Luck	Center DTU Effect	Ach. Reward	Game Speed

Revolution Idle Capricorn.pngCapricorn	Earth	Winter	Star Base	Stardust exponent	Gen.Exponent	Mult/bought Gen


Planets
Planets is the place where you put / equip Zodiacs. When Zodiac is equipped it will activate its bonuses. Additionally you will get Planet bonus based on the Planet and equipped zodiac's Season.

Elements Builds
These builds are extremely useful for Elements progression and will boost your Elements gain. Make sure to equip right element loadout build, then restart your Macro to get your score up for higher elements gain.

Early on it might feel tedious to make those build, because of slow zodiac spawn speed and need for picking specific zodiacs. There are some tips that can help you to make things faster:

You can lock zodiacs by double clicking to prevent them from being sold accidentally. You can still manually sell those zodiacs though.
When getting Earth Build turn off Auto-Sell, get zodiacs you need, lock them and turn on Auto-Sell again. That will allow you to easily get other zodiacs with active Auto-sell/Auto-Sacrifice.
You can wait until you get your zodiac inventory filled up with specific zodiac types (e.g Fire if you are building Fire set), then equip loadout for that set. It will pull the right zodiacs right into their slots. Then you can either lock them in their planet slots or just activate Auto-Sell for fire zodiacs while your set is equipped. It will remove the rest of the fire zodiacs from the inventory. Turn of Auto-Sell for Fire Zodiacs and you can unequip your Fire Set safely now. This removes the need for manual zodiac management, but requires a bit more of Zodiac inventory space.
You can use 3x game speed using time Flux to quickly fill up your zodiacs inventory.
Late-game cycle of zodiacs is: Water - Fire - Earth - Wind - Water.
Do not use too much Time Flux to get Elements. Small Amounts of 10-20 minutes per zodiac is a better option.
Most of the time you don't want to use Lazy/Easy builds for Elements farming, they aren't as efficient.
Here is a table of comparison of various Zodiac Elements Builds on Ethereal rarity:

Revolution Idle Zodiacs and Planets Guide
Revolution Idle Zodiacs and Planets Guide.jpg
Zodiacs are essential part of Unity progression. They can be equipped to Planets to get specific stat multiplier boosts and acquired by performing the Unity reset.

Check out other Revolution Idle Guides for additional information and strategy tips.

You can pick one of 4 Zodiacs upon Unity reset or reroll for another set of Zodiacs. Try to utilize various Exp Factors to increase Zodiac lvl prior to performing Unity reset.

Explaining the Zodiacs
Zodiacs are pretty complex, so require a breakdown to understand how they work.

Each Zodiac has its Element and Season. Those are set per zodiac and never change. Seasons effect the Planet bonuses and Element kind of shows which bonuses you will get.

Each Zodiac can get up to 4 bonuses. Early zodiacs have 2 bonuses, legendary-mythic rarity gives 3, while godly+ gives all 4 bonuses. Bonuses you get depend on the Zodiac. Some Zodiacs will get one of the bonuses all the time.

In the following table there is a full list of zodiacs, with their potential bonuses. BOLD bonuses means that this is unique bonus for this specific Zodiac, it's not guaranteed to show up though.

Zodiacs, Elements, Seasons and their Bonuses
Zodiac Name	Element	Season	Potential Bonus 1	Potential Bonus 2	Potential Bonus 3	Potential Bonus 4
Revolution Idle Aries.pngAries	Fire	Spring	Mult Gain	Promotion Power	Ascension Power	Common exp.Power
Revolution Idle Leo.pngLeo	Fire	Summer	Lap speed	Promotion Power	Ascension Power	Common exp.Power
Revolution Idle Sagittarius.pngSagittarius	Fire	Winter	Lap speed	Slowdown Power	Ascension Power	Common exp.Power
Revolution Idle Gemini.pngGemini	Wind	Summer	Eternity Gain	EP Gain	SN Req	Lab mult Power
Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra	Wind	Autumn	DP Gain	EP Gain	SN Req	Lab mult Power
Revolution Idle Aquarius.pngAquarius	Wind	Winter	DP Gain	Free Lab Lvs	SN Req	Lab mult Power
Revolution Idle Cancer.pngCancer	Water	Summer	DTP Cost	Center DTU Effect	Ach. Reward	Game Speed
Revolution Idle Scorpio.pngScorpio	Water	Autumn	Luck	Center DTU Effect	Ach. Reward	Game Speed
Revolution Idle Pisces.pngPisces	Water	Spring	Luck	Zodiac Quality	Ach. Reward	Game Speed
Revolution Idle Taurus.pngTaurus	Earth	Spring	IP Gain	Inf Gain	Gen.Exponent	Mult/bought Gen
Revolution Idle Virgo.pngVirgo	Earth	Autumn	Star Base	Inf Gain	Gen.Exponent	Mult/bought Gen
Revolution Idle Capricorn.pngCapricorn	Earth	Winter	Star Base	Stardust exponent	Gen.Exponent	Mult/bought Gen


Planets
Planets is the place where you put / equip Zodiacs. When Zodiac is equipped it will activate its bonuses. Additionally you will get Planet bonus based on the Planet and equipped zodiac's Season.

Planets and their Bonuses for each Season
Season	Revolution Idle Sun.pngSun	Revolution Idle Mercury.pngMercury	Revolution Idle Venus.pngVenus	Revolution Idle Moon.pngMoon	Revolution Idle Mars.pngMars	Revolution Idle Jupiter.pngJupiter
Winter	Eternity Rewards ^1.2	Star Cost ^0.9	DU Power x1.05	Zodiac Quality x1.3	Eternity Gain x2	Game Speed x1.1
Summer	Luck Mult x1.15	Infinity Gain x10	Lap Speed x3	Unity Rewards ^1.15	DP Mult x1.8	SN Rewards ^1.03
Autumn	Unity Rewards ^1.1	DP Mult x2	SN Rewards ^1.02	Eternity Rewards ^1.15	Star Cost ^0.85	DU Power x1.03
Spring	Zodiac Quality x1.5	Eternity Gain x2	Game Speed x1.2	Luck Mult x1.2	Infinity Gain x5	Lap Speed x2
Season	Revolution Idle Saturn.pngSaturn	Revolution Idle Uranus.pngUranus	Revolution Idle Neptune.pngNeptune	Revolution Idle Pluto.pngPluto	Revolution Idle Chiron.pngChiron	Revolution Idle Fortune Pars.pngFortune Pars
Winter	Luck Mult x1.1	Infinity Gain x3	Lap Speed x10	Unity Rewards ^1.15	DP Mult x2.5	Luck Mult x1.5
Summer	Eternity Rewards ^1.2	Star Cost ^0.93	DU Power x1.06	Zodiac Quality x1.25	Eternity Gain x2	Luck Mult x1.5
Autumn	Zodiac Quality x1.2	Eternity Gain x3	Game Speed x1.04	Luck Mult x1.25	Infinity Gain x100	Luck Mult x1.5
Spring	Unity Rewards ^1.1	DP Mult x5	SN Rewards ^1.04	Eternity Rewards ^1.15	Star Cost ^0.9	Luck Mult x1.5
Zodiac Builds
Following builds will be useful throughout most of the Unity gameplay and barely become obsolete. There are other variants of those builds as well, but they can be a good basis for the players for specific situations.

Zodiac Farming Build
Zodiac Farming build is used for farming Zodiacs. You get fantastic Game Speed bonuses and Zodiac Quality and Luck that will help you to push for better zodiacs. Also useful for time-related achievements and parts of the game where you don't use Score Build. This is the main build for Minerals Part of the game.

Elements Builds
These builds are extremely useful for Elements progression and will boost your Elements gain. Make sure to equip right element loadout build, then restart your Macro to get your score up for higher elements gain.

Early on it might feel tedious to make those build, because of slow zodiac spawn speed and need for picking specific zodiacs. There are some tips that can help you to make things faster:

You can lock zodiacs by double clicking to prevent them from being sold accidentally. You can still manually sell those zodiacs though.
When getting Earth Build turn off Auto-Sell, get zodiacs you need, lock them and turn on Auto-Sell again. That will allow you to easily get other zodiacs with active Auto-sell/Auto-Sacrifice.
You can wait until you get your zodiac inventory filled up with specific zodiac types (e.g Fire if you are building Fire set), then equip loadout for that set. It will pull the right zodiacs right into their slots. Then you can either lock them in their planet slots or just activate Auto-Sell for fire zodiacs while your set is equipped. It will remove the rest of the fire zodiacs from the inventory. Turn of Auto-Sell for Fire Zodiacs and you can unequip your Fire Set safely now. This removes the need for manual zodiac management, but requires a bit more of Zodiac inventory space.
You can use 3x game speed using time Flux to quickly fill up your zodiacs inventory.
Late-game cycle of zodiacs is: Water - Fire - Earth - Wind - Water.
Do not use too much Time Flux to get Elements. Small Amounts of 10-20 minutes per zodiac is a better option.
Most of the time you don't want to use Lazy/Easy builds for Elements farming, they aren't as efficient.
Here is a table of comparison of various Zodiac Elements Builds on Ethereal rarity:

Revolution Idle Unity Trials Guide
Revolution Idle Trials Guide.jpg
Trials are available after you have United 3 times and equipped all 3 of your zodiacs. This Guide covers requirements and builds for all the trials in Revolution Idle.

It's a part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find more guides for other game aspects.

Once you complete your 3rd Unity and equipped all 3 Zodiacs you will unlock the Achievement #165 (Super Achievements?), with this you’ll unlock Trials—You can think of these just like the EC's. These are crucial to progressing fast in unity, which is why we've been selecting all of those Fire Zodiacs earlier, as ONLY Fire zodiacs can help you in ET's (Easy Trials). ETs will give you a bunch of great Factors. Factors are how you gain exp which allow you to level up your Zodiacs, and improve their buffs.

TRIALS tips and notes Trials are extremely important and the next major step forward in your progress with Unity There are (as of vs 1.001) 3 Trial difficulties for you to complete (insane and bonus trials will come in later updates) • You must complete ALL 6 trials within a trial difficulty section before you are allowed to move onto the next one • Each Trial completion will give you a unique reward be it a great QoL or a buff to common exponent • THERE ARE NO PENALTIES FOR TRYING AND FAILING this means it's safe to just give any trial a try, you may be able to skip a unity or 2 if you just try a Trial and manage to Complete it" • Each ET completion will also let u keep 2 EC completions on unity For all trials but especially the ETs make sure that you have your auto slowdown enabled and set at 10.0

• To complete a Trial:

  1. Lock the Trial.
  2. Press the Reset Unity button (🔄).
  3. Reach the goal 
⚠️ Important Note: • If you did not choose a Fire Zodiac in all 3 of your first Unities, you won’t be able to beat ET1 on your first attempt.

Note: Revolution Idle Sagittarius.pngSagittarius (+slowdown) only works in ETs after 5 unities/ach 168 (automation for AP & animals) which keeps Animals tab unlocked from the start

How to Complete Easy Trials (ETs) • You can attempt all Easy Trials (ETs), but follow this order for success:

 1. Start with ET2 first.
 2. Now attempt ET1.
    (If you can’t beat ET1 right away, don’t worry—it’s normal! just go for another unity and try again until you beat the trial)
 3.Just go in order from here.
ALL ET TRIAL COMPLETION GIVES: Keep revolutions doesn't take score/ prestige/promote reset nothing

Below you can find detailed guides for each Unity Trial

Easy Trials
In order to complete Easy trial you will need to hit Infinity by getting ~e308 score. This means that Unity bonuses and most Zodiacs will be useless. Focus on the Red Zodiacs, especially Aries with Mult Gain Bonuses.

For Animal Purchases, buy [1,1], [1,2], [2,1], [2,2] with your free AP.

Completing all easy trials keeps revolutions. Promotes and Prestige resets nothing now even in the new unitys.

#1 Easy Trial
Handicap: You can buy only first 6 colors.

Reward: x1.4 to base zodiac quality.

Solution: Should be completed after #2 Easy Trial. It's recommended to have at least 3 red Zodiacs. Perfect conditions are 2+ Aries with Mult Gain bonuses, but can be done with other red zodiacs as well.

Completion Time: Depends on the Zodiacs. It can take anywhere from 1 to 20 minutes. If it takes longer it's better to farm better red zodiacs through unity.

#2 Easy Trial
Handicap: You can't promote 3 and 4.

Reward: Unlock Trials Exp Factor.

Solution: Should be the First trial to complete, it's the easiest one. Can be completed with basic lvl 3 Revolution Idle Aries.pngAries straight away, but you can do more unities and get more red zodiacs if it's taking too long.

Completion time: less than a minute with 3 red zodiacs.

#3 Easy Trial
Handicap: Common exponent is multiplied by 0.6

Reward: Common exponent is multiplied by 1.05

Solution: Attempt after Unity Trial 1, if fail go for stronger Zodiacs.

Completion time: ???

#4 Easy Trial
Handicap: You can't use Promotions 2 and 4 or Prestige

Reward: Unlock Mars

Solution: Attempt after Unity Trial 3, if fail go for stronger Zodiacs.

Completion time: ???

#5 Easy Trial
Handicap: Common exponent can't be >0.7

Reward: Unlocks Max Score factor

Solution: Attempt after Unity Trial 4, if fail go for stronger Zodiacs. This trial is a huge stat check due to the exponent cap, stack more Aries Multi as necessary to clear it.

Completion time: ???



#6 Easy Trial
Handicap: You can't prestige, promote or ascend any buyable

Reward: Unlock Planet Shop!

Solution: Attempt after Unity Trial 5, if fail go for stronger Zodiacs. Lap Speed bonuses might be needed to trigger AutoSlow for the ascensions

Completion time: ???

Medium Trials
In order to complete Medium Trials you will need to hit Eternity Reset by getting ~e308 Infinity Points. Finally Unity bonuses are useful and you can farm more unities in order to help with trials if you are stuck.

It's possible to do them with various builds through farming, but most people prefer to cheese through using Aquarius Zodiac with Lab Levels bonuses. Additional Zodiacs include SN Req^^ to get more Supernovas cheaper.

Recommended Planet bonuses:

Revolution Idle Sun.pngSun: Autumn (Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra, Unity Rewards ^1.1 for IP Gain),
Revolution Idle Mercury.pngMercury: Outside MT2 Only: Winter (Revolution Idle Aquarius.pngAquarius, Star Costs ^0.9),
Revolution Idle Venus.pngVenus: Autumn (Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra, Supernova Rewards ^1.02), gives a small bonus on Star Base and AP
Revolution Idle Mars.pngMars: Outside MT2 Only: Autumn/Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra (Star Costs ^0.85),
Revolution Idle Jupiter.pngJupiter: Summer (Supernova Rewards ^1.03)
Completing all medium trials keeps IP per second through Unitys and unlock Max IP factor.

#1 Medium Trial
Handicap: You can only buy the first two infinity generators.

Reward: x1.337 Luck boost.

Solution: Farm enough Zodiacs with SN Req^^ to gain last AP needed for Animal 7 Milestone to unlock the Laboratory. Animal Order: [1,1], [1,2], [2,1], [1,3], [1,4], [2,4], [1,5].

Completion Time: Depends on the Zodiacs. It can take anywhere from 1 to 20 minutes. Hardest part is getting the right Zodiacs.

#2 Medium Trial
Handicap: You can't buy stars.

Reward: Keep your IC and fastest infinity at 0.001 on Eternity and Unity.

Solution: Same as Medium Trial 1, just boost through with Animal 7 milestone and free Lab Levels.

Completion time: less than a minute with 3 red zodiacs.

#3 Medium Trial
Handicap: You can't prestige.

Reward: Supernova resets nothing, unlocks DTP automations.

Solution: Try same preset as for the previous trials or switch to some Fire zodiacs and free lab lvls.

Completion time: ???

#4 Medium Trial
Handicap: You can only infinite twice.

Reward: Unlock automation for Unity

Solution: The easiest way to complete this challenge is reach 20 animals milestone for IP generation. Make sure that you have a lot of ^^SN req zodiacs and Free Lab Lvls. Disable AutoGen, Star and AP & Enable AutoEternity to avoid losing the IP from accidental purchases.

Completion time: ???

#5 Medium Trial
Handicap: You can only infinite once.

Reward: Unlocks DTP count factor.

Solution: The easiest way to complete this challenge is reach 20 animals milestone for IP generation. Make sure that you have a lot of ^^SN req zodiacs and Free Lab Lvls. Disable AutoGen, Star and AP & Enable AutoEternity to avoid losing the IP from accidental purchases.

Completion time: ???

#6 Medium Trial
Handicap: You can't prestige, promote and you can only buy the first 5 colors.

Reward: Keep the fastest Eternity on Unity reset at 0.005s.

Solution: Same as before, free lab levels and sn requirements will allow you to clear this Trial very fast. Aim for 100 free lab levels and .67 ^^SN Req multiplier Completion time: ???

Hard Trials
In order to complete Hard Trials you will need to hit Unity Reset by getting ~e2466 Eternity Points. This means that you have to go through whole game cycle with additional handicaps. You will need new set of zodiacs, DP^ bonuses for HT 1-4 and Attacks Zodiac build for HT 5-6.

Recommended Season-Planet Bonuses:

Revolution Idle Sun.pngSun: Winter (Revolution Idle Aquarius.pngAquarius, Eternity Rewards ^1.2), It's stronger than Unity Rewards as the latter is not huge enough to give a larger benefit.
Revolution Idle Mercury.pngMercury: Winter (Revolution Idle Aquarius.pngAquarius, Star Cost ^0.9), More influential than DP x2, which is applied AFTER the DP Power bonuses.
Revolution Idle Venus.pngVenus: Winter (Revolution Idle Aquarius.pngAquarius, DU Pow x1.05), This multiplies the base strength of each Dilation Upgrade (DUs). This bonus is the most important, so always have it active!
Revolution Idle Moon.pngMoon: Autumn (Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra, Eternity Rewards ^1.15),
Revolution Idle Mars.pngMars: Autumn (Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra, Star Cost ^0.85),
Revolution Idle Jupiter.pngJupiter: Autumn (Revolution Idle Libra.pngLibra, DU Power x 1.03),
Revolution Idle Neptune.pngNeptune: Summer (Revolution Idle Gemini.pngGemini/Revolution Idle Cancer.pngCancer/Revolution Idle Leo.pngLeo, DU Power x1.06), Revolution Idle Cancer.pngCancer is the best option for Neptune, with DTP Cost^^, C-DTU Multi, Ach29 Bonus for bonuses, from highest to lowest priority.
Completing all hard trials unlocks Break Unity, removing the EP limit.

#1 Hard Trial
Handicap: You can't buy more than 20 DTP.

Reward: DP amount factor.

Solution: Get ~ ^2.5 DP gain. Supernova Req^^ is recommended for your secondary bonus. For DTP Trees, use the following:

Supernova: C:1 T:1-1-5-4 B:1-1-1-5,
DP: C:5 M:4-1-5-5,
AP/EP: C:1 T:1-1-1-5 B:5-5-0-0 (Yeah, it's 19, not sure where to put in the last one here)
#2 Hard Trial
Handicap: You can't buy Lab or RP upgrades

Reward: Lab Level factor unlock

Solution: Slot enough Lab Levels to get to dilation. ~300 lab points will help to get easy and fast completion.

#3 Hard Trial
Handicap: You can't Supernova.

Reward: Unlocks automation for selling zodiacs.

Solution: Supernova-related bonuses will not be useful unless they come with DP^ Bonus, so focus on maxing those.

Completion time: ???

#4 Hard Trial
Handicap: You can't buy more than 1500 AP or get past Supernova 10.

Reward: Unlocks AP factor.

Solution: Having Neptune is highly recommended for the last DU Power bonus. ^3.5 or 4 dp gain req, you can put aqua on Revolution Idle Venus.pngvenus, Revolution Idle Libra.pnglibra on Revolution Idle Jupiter.pngjupiter and Revolution Idle Cancer.pngcancer on Revolution Idle Neptune.pngneptune for DU power

#5 Hard Trial
Handicap: You can buy only 50 of each dilation upgrade and you can buy only 2 DTP.

Reward: Divides all relic costs by 1000000

Solution: full red lvs +80 divine doesn't need to be all Revolution Idle Aries.pngAries

#6 Hard Trial
Handicap: You can't dilate or enter EC

Reward: Unlocks EP factor (past e2,466)

Solution: Same build as Hard Trial 5, might need to farm higher level/rarity/quality zodiacs.

Credit: @blixer from official Revolution Idle (Oni Gaming) Discord

Insane Trials
Insane trials unlocks when you reach specific milestones on the weapon polish in the Minerals Tab. Each Insane Trial has it's own requirement and optimal way to complete it. As always if it takes too long, probably you aren't ready yet, keep grinding.

#1 Insane Trial
Requires lvl 25 Knuckles milestone.
Goal: Buy 65 DTP

Handicap: First DU is useless and dilation penalties are ^2 stronger.

Reward: You get 10 more EC from Trials

Solution: Use Score Zodiac Build, it should pop pretty much instantly. If macros/automations don't work, do things manually. You should be ok with the zodiac quality and levels you have, if you can't reach it - grind for better zodiacs.

Reward: Now you keep all of your EC on Unity Reset. Trials reward is changed to PP gain mult.

Solution: Use Score Zodiac Build. This time you will have to grind zodiacs in order to reach required score. Score Build:

#6 Insane Trial
Requires lvl 100 Knuckles milestone.
Goal: Reach level 100 Attacks in locked trial 6

Handicap: Divider in the attacks is very big now. Relic 13's effect is inversed. Also level scaling is much harder now, you can't gain gold and ascension power for attacks is logarithmed.

Reward: Gain 1 Unity per second (boosted by local minerals speed multiplier)

Solution: Use Score build. If you can't complete it fast - get better Zodiacs, sacrifice them or gain more VP for Attack Multipliers. Make sure not to do Refine Prestige accidentally, since it will reset your VP and you will have to grind them back. You need to have more than e1050 Value Points to beat the Trial.

Revolution Idle Attacks Guide
Revolution Idle Attacks Guide.jpg
Attacks is another gameplay layer in Revolution Idle that is separate from Revolutions. It has its own tab and unlocked upon completing any 15 Unity Trials. This guide will help players to understand attacks mechanics, learn optimal progression path and figure out which relics to focus on when spending Gold.

This Guide is a part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find more guides for other game aspects.

Attacks are somewhat similar to Revolution, but in Attacks each revolution will do damage and it requires specific amount of damage to go to the next attacks level. Each next level gets progressively harder and require more damage to finish.

Damage is calculated based on the current Score from Revolutions and then multiplied by 5 multipliers from Attacks, additional multiplier and powered by exponent, all of which will be unlocked later in the game.

You can upgrade and Ascent your Attacks just like Revolutions. They have following stats:

Speed - how long it takes to do 1 attack
Mult Gain - Multiplier added to the corresponding color at the top for each attack
Damage Mult - Multiplier to the damage done by this Attack upon its revolution
Asc. Power - Multiplier to Mult. Gain and Damage Mult on Ascension
Progressing Attacks rewards player with Gold on Unity Prestige. It's important to notice that switching Zodiacs doesn't trigger that effect and your Attacks count and progress stays. You can use it to your advantage by switching Zodiac builds to push further or faster.

Gold can be spent on Attacks Upgrades or Relics early on, and there will be other options to spend gold later in the game as well.

Relics
Revolution Idle has 35 relics that can be purchased with Gold, in order to unlock a new relic player needs to reach specific max Attack lvl. Relics can be upgraded to the next level with gold with diminishing returns and exponential price increase. Most of the Relic bonuses affect Attacks, Gold Gain and Zodiacs Quality and Luck, although other multipliers are there as well.

Early on you can buy any relic you want, in most cases it will depend on the available gold and all the relics will be leveled up to the max every Unity reset.

Later on there are some specific tips that will help to level relics faster:

Upgrade Relics only when you reach max Attacks (it will lower their Gold Price)
First Relic should be #014 Palate Expander since it Decreases cost of all relics
Depending on the goals focus on the relics that will help with the current push/goal. For example get #021 Queen of Spades, #017 Magical Magnet, #009 Midas Hand if you are farming Gold or #015 Comfortable Telescope, #002 Pinewood Bracelet and #004 Four Leaf Clover if you are going for Zodiac farm or grind.
Because of diminishing returns lower lvl relics usually give better bonuses per lvl or per gold spent. This is even more important for newly unlocked relics
Relics price and levels don't scale at the same pace, some relics will get higher level faster than others
Revolution Idle Relics 
Relics Sacrifice
Relics Sacrifice is available when relic reaches level 301. When you sacrifice a relic its level will be reset to 1 and it will get additional multiplier to its bonus using the following formula: (Sacrifice Level - 250) / 500. Sacrifice at level 301 gives 10% additional bonus, while every level after that provides additional 0.2%

After Sacrifice relic gets levels back through progressing attacks or spawning minerals:

1 relic level per 500 Attack levels
1 relic level per 10 Mineral levels Spawned
This means that progressing attacks fast will be an easy way to get relic levels back in early progression, but it still takes couple of hours to get back to level 300. Getting sacrificed relics levels back using the Mineral spawn is faster and more efficient, especially later in the game when you can spawn high level minerals and perform polish prestige.

It's recommended to sacrifice relics either before overnight run, so they can recover during that time or when you get access to polish, so they will be leveled back up fast enough. Make sure not to sacrifice relics and perform Attack Prestige at the same time, since it will reset your gold amount and make relic recovery way slower.

Attacks Prestige
Upon reaching 2025 Attacks you unlock Attacks Prestige. Just like normal prestige it will reset your attack upgrades and Gold. In return you will get better Attack Mult. Gain and Attack Exp. You will need to buy all the Attack upgrades and Ascend them again. Those bonuses will help you to push attacks even further but can stall you if you do prestige at the wrong time.

For your first Attack Prestige it's better to wait a little bit until multipliers will be high enough. Prior to prestige make sure to max out your Relics, do all Relic Sacrifice, get them all back to max level and make sure your Mineral Upgrades are in the place. That will allow you to build up your attacks fast enough.

Another thing that can help to recover fast after prestiging is Refine Tree Node 2, that allow to bulk attacks. It helps to go back to max level attacks significantly faster.

Later in the game Attacks Prestige recovery takes about 2 Unity Resets (~1 minute in terms of real time) but doesn't give that much of a bonus. So you can do it anytime you feel stuck or if accumulated additional multipliers feel significant for you.

Revolution Idle Special Minerals Guide
This guide covers special minerals mechanics, description and progression in Revolution Idle. Special minerals are long-term progression layer that provide passive bonuses to various game aspects based on the Special Minerals level.

It's a part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find more guides for other game aspects.

Special Minerals Sacrifice
Special Minerals Sacrifice is available upon reaching refine node 85. This unlocks a new tab and allows Special minerals to scale without taking grid space. First you need to delete 100 lvl 20 Special minerals of every type. Once one type of minerals being sacrificed it stop spawning and be removed from the spawning pool. Once all Special Minerals are sacrificed spawning special minerals provides you with Sacrifice Dust that can be used to upgrade the Special Minerals (Pretty much like merging). Sacrificed special minerals provide additional bonuses on top of the normal ones.

All Sacrificed Special Minerals start at lvl 27 which provide more stat bonuses than full grid of lvl 20 Special Minerals of one type.

Your main goal is to maximize the gain of Sacrifice Dust through various means:

Refine Node 86 provide passive SMP exp/s
Tarot Challenges provide gain multiplier per challenge completion
Refine Node 90 provides multiple upgrades for Sacrifice Dust gain
After a while you will get base 20 SMP, which will give you an achievement that makes total SMP EXP buff the amount of sacrifice dust you get, which will make level 28 pushes easier. Your goal will be to reach e228 RFP to get RN89, which you should be able to get at some point after getting level 28 light blue gem. Once RN89 is unlocked, you can start Tarot Challenges.[1]

The recommended order of Upgrades for Special Mineral Sacrifice[2]:

Special Minerals Sacrifice Upgrade order
Level 28	Yellow	Light Blue	Red	Blue	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 29	Yellow	Light Blue	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 30	Light Blue	Yellowe	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 31	Light Blue	Yellow	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 32	Yellow	Light Blue	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 33	Yellow	Light Blue	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 34	Yellow	Light Blue	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 35	Yellow	Blue	Light Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Green	Black	Orange	Pink
Level 36	Yellow	Blue	Red	Dark Purple	White	Light Blue	Green	Black	Orange	Pink

Revolution Idle Tarot Challenges Guide
Tarot Challenges are available once you obtain Refine Node 89. They are similar to other Challenges and Trials in the Revolution Idle, but focused on Tarot Cards and various handicaps related to them.

This guide will list the approximate order of Tarot Challenges completion and some milestones that should be completed before attempting them, but there can be a variety or more optimal order of completion. Don't forget to do Elements run, get new Zodiacs or check other upgrade options if you feel like you should be able to finish the challenge but something is not right.

It's a part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find more guides for other game aspects.

Just because a challenge is right after another doesn't necessarily mean it can be attempted right away (eg. magician challenge), it simply means thats your next goal to aim for. If a challenge can be attempted right after another, that will be pointed out in the guide.

Challenge 1: The Fool
The Fool can be completed immediately after unlocking Tarot Challenges.

Strategy:

Create a macro that repeatedly casts The Fool.
Run the macro for about 20 minutes.
You may leave Time Flux at 3x, although it isn't required.
Macro:

useArcan(fool)
Challenge 2: Ace of Wands
Ace of Wands can be completed immediately after The Fool.

Preparation:

Equip your Water Zodiac setup.
Strategy:

Use the Arcane Spam macro.
The challenge should complete almost instantly.
Challenge 3: Ace of Cups
Complete Ace of Cups after Ace of Wands.

Preparation:

Equip your Earth Zodiac setup.
Strategy:

Complete the challenge.
After completing this challenge, continue progressing until your Gems reach Level 28.
Challenge 4: Ace of Swords
Ace of Swords becomes trivial once your Red Gem reaches Level 28.

Preparation:

Red Gem Level 28.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete almost instantly.
Challenge 5: Ace of Pentacles
Ace of Pentacles can be attempted immediately after Ace of Swords.

Preparation:

Equip your Fire Zodiac setup.
Strategy:

The challenge should only take a few minutes.
If progress slows down, use a The Hierophant spam macro.
Challenge 6: Two of Swords
Two of Swords can be completed with the Fire Zodiac setup and a simple Strength macro.

Preparation:

Equip your Fire Zodiac setup.
Strategy:

Run a macro that repeatedly casts Strength.
Use four 30-minute Time Warps.
Macro:

useArcan(strength)
Challenge 7: Two of Wands
Two of Wands is your next progression goal after Two of Swords.

Strategy:

Time Warp each element until all four reach 1e9.
After completing this challenge, continue progressing until your Yellow and Light Blue Gems reach Level 29.
Challenge 8: Two of Cups
Two of Cups should be attempted once your Astrology Luck is high enough to reliably spawn an Epic Zodiac.

Preparation:

Yellow Gem Level 29.
Light Blue Gem Level 29.
About 2.79 Luck in the Astrology Luck menu.
Purchase Pentacles Upgrade V.
Strategy:

Wait for an Epic Zodiac to spawn.
Complete the challenge.
Challenge 9: Three of Cups
Three of Cups can be completed shortly after Two of Cups.

Preparation:

Enable Auto Unity.
Enable Auto Sell Zodiac.
Strategy:

Complete the challenge once you have enough Luck.
After completing this challenge:

Upgrade all Gems to Level 29 if you haven't already.
Set your Lab-5 (Generation Exponent) RP distribution to 100% while generating Earth.
During some stages you may only gain around x1e10 to x1e50 per element. This is expected.
Challenge 10: Two of Pentacles
Two of Pentacles can be completed quickly once you're ready to progress your Minerals.

Preparation:

All Gems should be around Level 31.
Refine Node 91 is highly recommended before continuing.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Use a macro that repeatedly casts The Lovers until the challenge is complete.
After completing this challenge:

Continue progressing until all Gems reach Level 31 if you haven't already.
Continue progressing until Refine Node 91 is bought
Challenge 11: Three of Wands
Three of Wands is primarily a waiting challenge.
Most of the time is spent building each element individually.

Strategy:

Progress each element separately using its corresponding Zodiac setup.
Spend about 30 minutes on each element, or more if needed.
Challenge 12: Four of Cups
Although Four of Cups looks intimidating, it's much easier with the proper automation enabled.

Preparation:

Enable Auto Unity.
Enable Auto Sell Zodiac.
Equip The Star Tarot Card.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Continue obtaining higher-rarity Zodiacs until the challenge completes.
Challenge 13: Five of Cups
Five of Cups can be completed almost instantly with the proper preparation.

Preparation:

Perform a full round of Sacrificing and Selling Zodiacs.
Switch to the standard Wind Zodiac setup.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete almost immediately.
After completing this challenge:

Reach Refine node 90 (level 17 is recommended)
Grind for Yellow lvl 33
Get Refine Node 90 up to 19 
Get all the Gems to lvl 33, you can proceed. (if you don't have enough tf to get all lvl 33s, you can try with just yellow, blue and light blue)
Challenge 14: The Magician
The Magician requires a significant amount of progression before it becomes practical.
This is one of the largest progression walls in the early Tarot Challenge sequence.

Preparation:

Upgrade Refine Node 90 to approximately Level 17.
Reach Yellow Gem Level 33.
Optionally upgrade Refine Node 90 to about Level 19 before leveling the remaining Gems.
Reach Level 33 on all Gems.
Strategy:

Each use of The Magician weakens the next cast, so the activation order is important.
Switch to the appropriate Zodiac setup before each cast.
Use the following sequence:
Water
Earth
Earth
Wind
Wind
Water
Fire
Fire
For both Earth casts, set your Lab-5 (Generation Exponent) RP distribution to 100% for the best results.
Challenge 15: The High Priestess
The High Priestess is a straightforward macro challenge once you've completed The Magician.

Strategy:

Set up a macro that repeatedly uses The High Priestess.
Time Warp for about 1 hour.
Macro:

useArcan(highPriestess)
Challenge 16: The Empress
The Empress cannot be reliably automated due to its timing requirement.

Strategy:

Use The Empress manually.
Activate it at 67.
Challenge 17: Three of Pentacles
Three of Pentacles can be completed very quickly.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Use The Emperor.
Wait a few minutes for the challenge to complete.
Challenge 18: Four of Pentacles
Four of Pentacles is nearly instant.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
The challenge should complete almost immediately.
Challenge 19: Five of Pentacles
Five of Pentacles is another short waiting challenge.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Time Warp for about 50 minutes.
Challenge 20: Six of Pentacles
Six of Pentacles requires using two Arcana in sequence.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Wait 15 seconds.
Use The Hierophant.
Use The Emperor.
After completing this challenge:

You should have all Gems at Level 34 before attempting the next challenge.
Challenge 21: Four of Wands
Four of Wands becomes available once your Blue Gem is sufficiently upgraded.

Strategy:

Complete the challenge using a high-level (lvl 34) Blue Gem.
Challenge 22: Five of Wands
Five of Wands is completed in the same way as Four of Wands.

Strategy:

Complete the challenge using the same approach as the previous challenge.
After completing this challenge:

You will need all Gems at Level 35, and possibly one or two Level 36 Gems.
Make sure you have Refine Node 93 before attempting the next challenge.
Challenge 23: Six of Wands
Six of Wands is primarily a waiting challenge.

Strategy:

Use Time Warp or AFK until the challenge completes.
Challenge 24: The Emperor
The Emperor is another macro-based challenge.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Run a macro containing only The Emperor.
Use a 10-minute Time Warp (or higher game speed).
Wait for the challenge to complete or use additional Time Flux if needed.
If this fails, Time Warp may be interfering with automation, especially on lower-end hardware. Try adjusting your game speed 3x Game Speed instead of 60x.

Macro:

useArcan(emperor)
Challenge 25: The Hierophant
The Hierophant can be completed immediately after The Emperor.

Preparation:

Disable Auto Delete Minerals.
Strategy:

Run a macro that repeatedly uses The Hierophant.
Use four 10-minute Time Warps.
Finish with one 3-minute Time Warp.
Macro:

useArcan(hierophant)
Challenge 26: The Lovers
The Lovers can be completed immediately after The Hierophant.

Strategy:

Run a macro that repeatedly uses The Lovers.
Wait about 10 minutes without using Time Warp.
Macro:

useArcan(lovers)
Remember to set your Lab-5 (Generation Exponent) RP distribution to 100% while generating Earth.
At some stages, you might only gain around x1e10 to x1e50 to each element. This is normal.
After completing this challenge: Huge timewall ahead. You need to grind for Relic 53 that requires Attack level 20600.

 You need to unlock Relic 53, which requires Attack Level 20600. Keep The Strength in your Arcana macro while working toward it.
You should also get all of your Gems to Level 36. Level 37 Gems take too long at this point, so most of your progression will come from farming Moon Runes and upgrading RN7 Efficiency.
Your RfP should be at least 1e518, although 1e526 or higher is recommended. Aim for at least ^1.75 SMP Efficiency and ^1.35 Black Gem Efficiency, and upgrade Polish Enhancing as much as possible.
Moon Runes are the most important part of this grind. Upgrade RN7 Efficiency to approximately 11.8x. In some cases, 11.6x may be enough after completing Three of Swords.
Challenge 27: The Chariot
The Chariot is completed automatically once you reach the required progression milestone.

Strategy:

Reach Level 40 on all Gems.
Completing Relic 53 should allow you to complete this challenge instantly.
Challenge 28: Justice
Justice can be attempted immediately after completing The Chariot.

Preparation:

Enable Auto Spawn Special Minerals.
Set Max Base Cost to a small non-zero value (for example, 100).
Leave Base Cost blank.
Set up a macro that repeatedly uses Justice.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Let it run using 3x Time Flux for about 1 hour.
Do not use Time Warp.
Macro:

useArcan(justice)
Challenge 29: Three of Swords
Three of Swords is a short waiting challenge.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Wait about 15 minutes.
Use Cups Upgrade VI to unlock Level 41 Gems. Purchase the Yellow Gem first, then the remaining Gem colors should unlock almost instantly.
Challenge 30: Four of Swords
Four of Swords is completed quickly.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Keep Strength in your macro while completing it.
Challenge 31: Five of Swords
Five of Swords can be attempted immediately after Four of Swords.

Strategy:

Use the same setup as the previous challenge.
The challenge should complete quickly.
Push for Relic 54 before continuing. To get it you need around these stats:
1e649 rfp
1e20 Tarot Card Levels
2e370 tarot resources
71-73k elements
14.4x Refine Node7 efficiency
Focus on upgrading Moon Runes and RN7 Efficiency whenever possible. Continue improving your Polish Enhancing and other permanent multipliers as they become available.
Push to Level 42 on all Gems
Once you've reached the required progression, continue with the next Tarot Challenges.
Challenge 32: Seven of Wands
Seven of Wands is another waiting challenge where each element must be built separately using its corresponding Zodiac setup.

Strategy:

Generate each element using its respective Zodiac setup and Lab settings.
Spend approximately:
52 minutes on Water
65 minutes on Earth
75 minutes on Wind
65 minutes on Fire
Challenge 33: Six of Swords
Six of Swords can be completed with the Arcana spam macro while keeping Strength in the rotation.

Strategy:

Use the Arcana spam macro with Strength included.
Use short 10-minute Time Warps.
The challenge should complete in about 1 hour of Time Flux.
Grind all Special Minerals to Level 43. The Refine Node 7 Efficiency Moon Rune upgrade makes this grind noticeably shorter than the previous one.
Challenge 34: Six of Cups
Level 43 Gems should provide more than enough power to complete this challenge.

Strategy:

Start the challenge.
If needed, grind a little more Astrodust before trying again.
Grind all Special Minerals to Level 44. Purchasing additional Refine Node 7 Efficiency upgrades with Moon Runes makes this grind even shorter.
It's also recommended to refresh your Zodiacs if you haven't done so in a while. Your Zodiacs should be at Amazing+1000 rarity by this point. Merge three of them into a Prime Zodiac, then refresh all of your Zodiac loadouts with Prime rarity Zodiacs.
Challenge 35: Seven of Cups
Seven of Cups is much easier than it appears.

Strategy:

Enable Auto Unity.
Let the game run for a few minutes.
Even if it looks like you won't complete the challenge, keep waiting—it will eventually finish.
You now need to push to Level 45 Gems, which requires reaching 1e800 RfP to purchase Refine Node 95.
To reach this, grind Common Minerals until Refine Points after Prestige reaches at least 5e791 RfP, then use a high-level Death Arcana Card.
Cycling through Elements and purchasing the 1e400 Tarot Resource upgrades should be enough to reach the requirement with a little Time Flux. This will also push all Elements to around 1e100000.
Challenge 36: Seven of Swords
Seven of Swords is another waiting challenge.

Strategy:

Keep The Strength in your Arcana macro.
Wait until the challenge completes.
Your next goal is to increase your Attack for Relic 55.
After that, purchase the 1e418 Wands upgrade and the 1e424 Cups upgrade.
Then, use your Arcana spam macro to grind 1e875 RfP for Refine Node 96 and continue farming Sacrifice Dust for Level 46 Gems.
This progression will quickly push you to approximately 1e900 RfP and 1e450 Tarot Resources, allowing you to reach Level 47 Gems and unlock Relic 56.
Challenge 37: Eight of Cups
Preparation:

Reach approximately 3.69–3.73 Luck inside the challenge.
This corresponds to roughly e8515 Luck outside the challenge.
Upgrade your Luck and Quality Donuts.
Increase the Refine Node 7 Efficiency Moon Rune upgrade to approximately 23.2x–23.4x.
Strategy:

Use Auto Unite to check how close you are.
Complete the challenge once you reach the required Luck.
Your next goal is 1e950 RfP, which requires approximately 8.5e940 Refine Points after Prestige before using the Death Arcana Card.
This is a long progression wall. Continue cycling Elements, pushing Common Minerals as far as possible, upgrading Refine Node 7 Efficiency to about 23.6x, increasing Tarot Resources to around 1e470, and reaching approximately:
Fire: e116k
Earth: e113k
Wind: e113k
Water: e111k
Challenge 38: Seven of Pentacles
Seven of Pentacles is mostly a waiting challenge.

Strategy:

Wait for your first Common Mineral spawn.
Afterward, you'll be spawning approximately Level 83–84 Common Minerals.
Continue until you reach approximately Level 105 Common Minerals.
The challenge typically takes between 1 and 3 hours, depending on luck.

Challenge 39: Eight of Wands
Eight of Wands is one of the longest Tarot Challenges so far.

Strategy:

Generate each Element until completion.
Expected production rates are approximately:
Water: 1.5e10/s
Fire: 8.5e9/s
Earth: 3.8e9/s
Wind: 3.5e9/s
The challenge typically takes around 24–25 hours in total.
After completing it, push your Gems to Level 48. This shouldn't require much Time Flux.
Then, increase Refine Points after Prestige to approximately 8e990. Using the Death Arcana Card should bring you to 1e1000 RfP.
With 1e1000 RfP, purchase Refine Node 99 to unlock Artifacts and continue with the Artifacts Guide.
Unlocking Artifacts will also allow you to obtain Level 49 Gems almost immediately.
Challenge 40: Eight of Swords
Preparation:

Have approximately 10 of each Golden Resource in the Artifacts tab.
If necessary, perform an Attack Prestige beforehand.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete almost instantly.
Push your Gems to Level 52. This shouldn't take long if you have at least 20 of each Golden Resource.
While farming Golden Resources, it's recommended to fully complete the Refine Tree.
Challenge 41: Eight of Pentacles
Eight of Pentacles can be completed immediately after Eight of Swords.

Preparation:

Have approximately 20 of each Golden Resource if needed.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete almost instantly.
Make sure the Crystal Mirror Artifact's fourth piece, Focal Point, is upgraded to around Level 7–8. This shouldn't be difficult once Golden Resource automation is unlocked.
Challenge 42: Nine of Swords
Preparation:

Upgrade Focal Point to at least Level 7.
Strategy:

Use the Score build, as it's the only build capable of providing enough Attack Power for this challenge.
Challenge 43: Nine of Cups
Preparation:

Upgrade Teeth to approximately Level 5.
Strategy:

If you can comfortably complete Nine of Swords, this challenge should also be straightforward.
Challenge 44: Nine of Wands
Nine of Wands can be attempted immediately after Nine of Cups.

Strategy:

Use the standard Water build.
The Water Element alone should complete the challenge almost instantly.
Challenge 45: The Hermit
Strategy:

Use approximately 80 minutes of Time Flux to skip the initial 8-hour cooldown.
Afterward, either spend a little more Time Flux or simply wait for the challenge to finish.
Challenge 46: Wheel of Fortune
Wheel of Fortune is entirely luck-based.

Strategy:

Keep attempting the challenge.
You'll eventually complete it.
Challenge 47: Nine of Pentacles
Nine of Pentacles can be completed immediately after Wheel of Fortune.

Preparation:

Have enough progression to spawn approximately Level 270 Common Minerals.
Strategy:

Spend around 20 minutes of Time Flux.
Complete the challenge once you reach the required Mineral Level.
You should have Special Mineral Level 60 and the required amount of Golden Resources for LOGPOW before attempting the next challenge.
Challenge 48: Ten of Pentacles
Preparation:

Have enough Wind Element to spawn approximately Level 548 Common Minerals.
Strategy:

Spend a few minutes using Time Flux.
The challenge should complete quickly.
Challenge 49: Ten of Swords
Preparation:

Obtain the Memory Crystal Artifact Piece.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete quickly.
Challenge 50: Ten of Cups
Preparation:

Upgrade Authenticity Glaze to Level 2.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Once the requirement is met, the challenge is essentially completed instantly.
Challenge 51: Ten of Wands
Preparation:

Upgrade LOGPOW to at least Level 2.
Strategy:

Spend approximately 3 minutes of Time Flux on each Element.
Complete the challenge.
You should now be able to upgrade your Special Minerals to Level 61.
Don't forget to purchase as many Tarot Upgrades as possible before attempting the final challenge.
Challenge 52: The Strength
Preparation:

Upgrade your Red Special Mineral to Level 61.
Strategy:

Spend approximately 30–40 minutes of Time Flux to build the multiplier.
Wait through several Strength cycles until you reach Attack Level 2000.
Challenge 53: Page of Cups
Preparation:

Have approximately 30 million of each Golden Resource.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete instantly.
Challenge 54: Page of Wands
Preparation:

Reach approximately e2026 as the combined multiplication of all Elements.
This is roughly equivalent to e510 in each Element.
Complete at least Plague Stage 2-1.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete quickly.
Challenge 55: Page of Swords
Preparation:

If you have around 30 million Golden Resources, complete at least Plague Stage 2-9.
If you have around 40 million Golden Resources, completing Plague Stage 2-7 is sufficient.
Strategy:

Complete the challenge once the above requirements are met.
After completing this challenge:

Upgrade your Golden Resources to approximately:
84 million for Swords.
98 million for Wands.
Set up a VE farm.
Use Auto Craft for ERs and configure it so each craft takes about 100 ms. Then repeatedly sell ERs until reaching approximately 5 million VE.
Once you reach 5 million VE, stop buying ER upgrades and wait until you reach 1e10 ViP. This unlocks Achievement 549, allowing ERs to be dissolved automatically.
After unlocking the achievement, set up your farm again. This will rapidly increase your ViP, allowing you to reach Level 67 Special Minerals along with approximately:
111 million Golden Resources for Swords.
130 million Golden Resources for Wands.
Challenge 56: Page of Pentacles
Preparation:

Have approximately 89–105 million Golden Resources.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It should complete quickly.
Continue progressing through the Plague page. Reaching approximately Stage 4-5 is recommended.
Challenge 57: The Hanged Man
Strategy:

Run a macro containing only The Hanged Man.
The challenge should complete instantly.
Macro:

useArcan(hangedMan)
Challenge 58: Death
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Activate the Death Card.
Wait a short time before performing a Refine Prestige.
If necessary, repeat the process several times.
Waiting at least 10 seconds before using Refine Prestige improves the chance of success.
Challenge 59: Temperance
Preparation:

Place a Temperate Zodiac into the Part of Fortune slot.
Strategy:

Run a macro that repeatedly uses Temperance.
Place each new Zodiac into its correct planetary slot.
The challenge completes immediately once every Zodiac is correctly assigned.
Afterwards, manually sell all Temperate Zodiacs.
Planet assignments:

Sun — Leo
Mercury — Gemini or Virgo
Venus — Taurus or Libra
Moon — Cancer
Mars — Aries or Scorpio
Jupiter — Sagittarius or Pisces
Saturn — Capricorn or Aquarius
Uranus — Aquarius
Neptune — Pisces
Pluto — Scorpio
Chiron — Taurus or Libra
Part of Fortune — Any
Macro:

useArcan(temperance)
Challenge 60: The Devil
Strategy:

Activate The Devil.
Click all 10 Chads.
Progress through the Plague page until you obtain a Latent ER with approximately ^0.357 Stealth or higher.
Challenge 61: Knight of Pentacles
Preparation:

A ^0.36 Stealth ER is recommended.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
It may take some time to complete.
Challenge 62: Knight of Wands
Preparation:

Reach approximately ^0.37–0.38 Infectivity.
Virulent ERs are recommended.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
Complete it once your Infectivity is high enough.
Challenge 63: Knight of Swords
Strategy:

Attempt the challenge immediately after Knight of Wands.
If necessary, continue increasing Spread Power.
Challenge 64: Knight of Cups
Strategy:

Farm Plague Stages that can be completed consistently.
Increase your Total Infected Population until the challenge becomes manageable.
Challenge 65: The Tower
Preparation:

Increase your Relic 67 Chance as much as possible.
Complete the Bonus Trial before attempting the challenge.
Strategy:

Start the challenge.
If you fail, continue improving your ERs before trying again.
This challenge is heavily RNG-based. If you fail, continue improving your ERs so you can quickly recover to 20,000 Infected in the Universe before attempting it again.
Challenge 66: Queen of Swords
Queen of Swords is almost entirely determined by your Singularity Multiplier.

Preparation:

Reach approximately x35.1–35.5 Singularity Multiplier.
Strategy:

Start the challenge once you reach the recommended Multiplier.
It should complete almost instantly.
Challenge 67: Queen of Pentacles
Queen of Pentacles depends on both your Singularity Multiplier and your equipped Evolutionary Relic.

Preparation:

Reach approximately x70–75 Singularity Multiplier.
Equip an Evolutionary Relic around Level 60–62.
Make sure you've kept up with your Plague progression.
Strategy:

Hierophant is not disabled during this challenge, making Refine Node 105 extremely valuable.
Use a Time Flux speed that's a power of two (x2, x4, x8, x16, or x32) to trigger bulk Mineral spawning more efficiently.
Challenge 68: Queen of Wands
Queen of Wands scales with both your Singularity Multiplier and your Singularity Count.

Preparation:

Recommended Multiplier by Singularity Count:

S20 — approximately x90
S22 — approximately x80
S24 — approximately x60
S26 — approximately x50
Strategy:

Attempt the challenge whenever your Singularity Multiplier matches your current Singularity Count.
If you're progressing naturally, S22 at approximately x80 Multiplier is a good point to complete it.
Pushing additional Singularity Counts first allows the challenge to be completed with a lower Multiplier.
Challenge 69: Queen of Cups
Queen of Cups has a fixed requirement.

Preparation:

Increase your Total Mult Factor to approximately 1.98743.
This is the Total value shown below the Mult Factor table.
Reaching this value provides exactly enough EXP to spawn a Level 6 Zodiac.
Recommended stats:
Singularity 22–25
x80–100 Singularity Multiplier
STN1: A5LX
Refine Node 125 Level 5
Approximately e600 Atoms
Strategy:

Continue increasing your Total Mult Factor until it reaches the required value.
The final push usually takes a couple of hours.
It's generally efficient to continue progressing until around Singularity 25 and complete this challenge while claiming Singularity 26.
Challenge 70: The Star
The Star is almost entirely RNG-gated.

Preparation:

Unlock Houses from the Singularity 32 Milestone.
Equip your standard Water Zodiac build.
Use The Star Arcana Card.
Strategy:

Singularize at approximately e1000 Atoms after unlocking Houses.
The challenge is completed by obtaining a Singular Zodiac with Uncommon rarity or higher.
Each attempt immediately ends the challenge, so you must re-enter before trying again.
Your chance of obtaining an Uncommon or better Singular Zodiac is approximately 9.44%.
There are currently no pity mechanics or fail safes, so completion is entirely RNG-based.
A dedicated macro can fully automate repeated attempts.
It is recommended to begin attempting this challenge immediately after unlocking Houses at Singularity 32 and continue retrying until it is completed.
Macro:

WaitUntil (Atoms >= 2.00e308)
EnterTC(Arcan, 18, False)
RepeatUntil (Atoms >= 1.00e1000)
    UseArcan(17)
    FlushSM([Red,Blue,Green,Pink,Yellow,Orange,Darkpurple,Lightblue,White,Black], 3)
Singularize(0, 0, False)
Challenge 71: King of Cups
King of Cups should generally be your first Kings challenge.

Preparation:

Complete all Queen Challenges.
Complete The Star.
Unlock Full House.
Make reasonable progress through the Singularity Tree.
Strategy:

Use the Zodiac build TTT-GTG-GTG-TTT to maximize EXP gain.
Sun

Unite every time you obtain a higher-level Zodiac to minimize the Unite penalty.
Reach approximately e590–e600 Atoms.
Recommended Singularity Multiplier: x250–x500.
Expected completion time: approximately 3–4 hours.
Challenge 72: King of Swords
King of Swords mainly scales with your Singularity Multiplier.

Strategy:

Higher Mult significantly reduces completion time.
Very high Time Flux speeds may actually reduce efficiency due to frame-locked mechanics.
Recommended Singularity Multiplier: approximately x500.
Estimated completion times:
x300 — ~10 hours
x500 — ~9 hours
x1000 — ~6 hours
x3600 — ~4.5 hours
Challenge 73: King of Wands
King of Wands primarily depends on your Singularity Multiplier and Fire Factor 3.

Preparation:

A strong Singular Aries is highly recommended.
Strategy:

Use the following Fire Zodiac build:
Sag Leo Leo – Libra Leo Leo – Leo Leo Leo – Aries Leo Leo

A 1% Lab 2 / 99% Lab 6 setup is recommended.
Recommended Singularity Multiplier: x1000–x1500.
Expected completion time: approximately 9 hours.
Players with a weak Singular Aries may need to wait for a higher Multiplier.
Challenge 74: King of Pentacles
King of Pentacles has a specific breakpoint for fast completion.

Strategy:

Spawn Mineral Level 64 inside the challenge.
Reaching M64 allows the challenge to complete instantly when e308 Atoms is reached.
Attempting the challenge below this breakpoint greatly increases completion time.
Since Mineral Level scales with √(Mult + 1), the required Singularity Multiplier depends on your Time Flux speed.
Recommended breakpoints:
No Time Flux — x3969
x8 Time Flux — x3600
x32 Time Flux — x3364
Challenge 75: The Moon
The Moon is a long challenge, although it's still considerably shorter than Eight of Wands.

Preparation:

Complete the King challenges first.
Equip your normal Zodiac build before starting.
Strategy:

Use only The Moon Arcana Card.
Do not change your Zodiac during the challenge. Changing the Zodiac assigned to The Moon resets its Quality back to 1.
Do not switch Elements while the challenge is running.
A macro containing only UseArcan(18) is recommended.
To avoid missing activations, fill all 30 macro slots with UseArcan(18).
At 60 FPS with x60 Time Flux, the card should activate consistently without missing cycles.
If your FPS is unstable, lower your Time Flux speed to match your average FPS.
Expected completion time is approximately 7–9 hours when attempted immediately after completing the King challenges.
Completing this challenge before pushing to higher Singularity counts is recommended, as the Luck bonus improves your chances of obtaining higher-rarity Singular Zodiacs.
Macro:

UseArcan(18)
