Revolution Idle Artifacts Guide
Revolution Idle Artifacts are next step of progression after completing Tarot Challenges.

It's a part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find more guides for other game aspects.

Artifacts Progression Guide
Acronyms used in the guide:

GRes = golden resources. res = tarot resources. X.Y: X = part amount, Y = level amount; for example: 1.5 would mean part 1, level 5.

Note:

it is possible your game will start having quite severe frame drops later on. once this starts to happen, disable auto slowdown.

flux usage: artifacts is a pretty afk/flux heavy area, and with some long afks, like the first 4 flushes or the hermit challenge. therefore, after unlocking flush automation in the automation tab (do not use the macro block), you will be able to use OF instead of TF to accumulate GRes, and it is even recommended to do so.
About Judgement: it is recommended to take judgement out of your loop macro as it hurts resource generation.

1. as soon as you unlock artifacts, check if you can flush for 1.01 on each and if yes, flush instantly on all resource types
2. you now have to get 5 GRes (golden resources), which can be done in two different ways: either 4 flushes of about 1-1.3 GRes per flush, or 3 flushes of 1.33-1.35 GRes per flush. at 4 hours per flush, the first option will take about 16 hours, and the second one 12. the second option is recommended in the case that you can reach those numbers of GRes per flush, otherwise, the first will be better. tf recommended to actually do the flushes in between the wait, otherwise afk; dont use OF
3. to set up a base, artifacts pushing consists of afking for the cooldown to go to 0, flushing, occasionally refining with death to push polish enhancing, runes timers, and SMP efficiency, as well as rn100 early on. always check if you can get rn100, its the lifeblood of artifacts. heres a visual explanation of this step:

Revolution Idle Artifacts Guide 1.png

4. push to sac level 50 minerals
5. after these 4 flushes, you should be at 2-4 rn100 levels, and will be getting to 4 soon
6. keep doing the artifacts loop, buying part levels when possible and pushing rfp. get to 10 GRes and buy the upgrades that come along with it for the next challenges
7. 8 of swords: will be completed instantly
8. 8 of pentacles: mostly consists of pushing mineral levels in the classic way.
9. further artifacts loop, pushing sacrifice levels and rfp, and getting GRes and regular res (will be referred to as just res from now on).
10. with 1.5, 2.3, and 3.1, you should be able to finish the refine tree fully.
11. hermit challenge: to be attempted at about e1k-e1.5k res, its simply a long wait with hermit in a loop mode macro. if you have the souls, using instant arcana cooldown resets is worth it for the first hermit use, after which you can just wait and it will complete itself quite quickly.
12. wheel of fortune challenge: set up a macro with only WOF in it on loop mode (nothing else!), start the challenge, run the macro, and then wait until about 65%+ chances to get 10 wins in a row (you might get it earlier or later, its luck at the end of the day)
13. push artifacts to 3.6 at least
14. artifacts push to e2000 res, get tarot upgrade 9 on all suits, which should let you buy a lot of other upgrades and push you to e2300+ res. then push to 2.8 artifacts
15. 9 of cups: might take a while/have to be done later/need more pushing, needs to be tested, otherwise this can be attempted right after getting 3.6
16. keep looping artifacts, after a while youll be able to push sacrifices to level 59, tho it likely wont be instantly after 9 of cups
17. 9 of pentacles: should only take a while of afking or otherwise mineral pushing
18. 9 of swords: as long as it starts out at 730+ for you it should get you there (might take a good amount of time)
19. 9 of wands: much easier than it might read at first as it only requires your elements to, when all multiplied together, be equal to e765, which means you only need about e191 on each element to complete it (water will have much more than that).
20. now comes a pretty long period of pushing artifacts, sacrifice levels, tarot upgrades, rfp upgrades (polish enhance, runes, SMP efficiency), and etc to acquire the seventh part of each artifact, which will start with the skull, then engine, then vessel and lastly mirror. once you have the seventh part of an artifact, you unlock LOGPOW, which is needed for all 10th challenges and strength challenge
21. 10 of pentacles: can be done as soon as LOGPOW of pentacles is unlocked, might require some amount of manual merging and some afk, but it shouldnt take too long as long as youre spawning lvls 549-550.
22. 10 of swords: as soon as swords LOGPOW is unlocked, it can be completed
23. 10 of wands: as soon as wands LOGPOW is leveled to lvl 2, it can be completed
24. 10 of cups: as soon as cups LOGPOW is unlocked, switch to 12 gemini build on zods and it should be able to be completed
25. another long period of pushing artifacts, your goal is to get up to sac lvl 62
26. when everything is at sac lvl 62, push for artifacts 6.6 and you should be able to unlock relic 60 (possibly not able to be bought yet)
27. strength challenge: can be completed with a macro that loops strength quite quickly

and now you have finished the artifacts update!

Credits: Made by @idkdiegoiguess from Oni Discord

many thanks to @Mastergame_angel ✪ 520 Sac63 💎 @jokk ier @Money Printer and many more for all the help on the creation of this, analysing stat sheets and theorising over next steps, this guide couldnt have been made without you (and everyone who has suggested changes, found errors, or helped brainstorm with us that i didnt mention!).

Artifacts Effects
List of artifact effects per artifact.

Mirror 🪞

1: Sacrificial Dust gain
2: Game speed Donut
3: Sacrificial Dust Gain (R56 > RN90)
4: Attack levels (R13)
5: Zodiacs Quality (R2)
6: Gold gain (R21)
7: Swords LOGPOW
8: Coming soon...

Skull 💀

1: Sacrificial Dust gain
2: Luck Donut
3: Zodiac exp factor ( R8 )
4: Relic price discount ( R14 )
5: Zodiac Quality ( R2 )
6: Gold gain ( RN7 )
7: Wands LOGPOW
8: Attack Power ( R52)
9: Hermit Upgrade
10: Water Quality factor Upgrade
11: Coming soon...

Prosperity Engine ⚙️

1: Sacrificial Dust Gain
2: Zodiac quality donut
3: Gold gain ( Polish Spear)
4: Hierophant Upgrade
5: Zodiac quality ( R2 )
6: Gold gain ( Yellow SPM )
7: Pentacles LOGPOW
8: Earh Node 2 ( N1 upgrade )
9 : Coming soon...

Emotional vessel 🫙

1: Sacrificial Dust Gain
2: Zodiac Exp Donut
3: Elements factor 3 upgrade
4: Zodiac Quality ( R2 )
5: Zodiac Quality ( R2 )
6: Gold Gain ( Polish Spear )
7: Cups LOGPOW
8: Polish enhancement Upgrade
9: PP boost ( R32 )
10:Coming soon...

Revolution Idle Macros Guide
Revolution Idle Macros Guide.jpg
Revolution Idle Macros help players to automate monotonous tasks and can significantly improve prestige loops in the late game.

Most of the macros become really useful upon reaching enough Unity progression when automating some things like Eternity Challenges will actually save up time (and sanity).

Also most of the macros needs to be tuned for each player because of stats and multiplier difference for it to work properly.

Eternity 4-Eternity 34
Eternity 4-8 Macro
You will be able to build your very first useful macro when reaching Eternity 4 and unlocking achievement #074, which will be used for going through all Infinity Challenges (ICs), from 1 to 9, without manually clicking on each one of them.

Click to show Preparations, Macro and Template for Import >>>

Eternity 8-16 Macro
By reaching Eternity 8, you will unlock achievement #076, which will add the Break/Fix Infinity macro block.

Click to show Preparations, Macro and Template for Import >>>

Eternity 16-34 Macro
By reaching Eternity 16, you will unlock Eternity Milestones 7, 8 and 9, which will unlock Autobuy for Infinity Upgrades, Generators and Stars.

Click to show Preparations, Macro and Template for Import >>>

Credits: johnydn_ @ Oni Gaming Discord

Early Unity Promotions and Prestige
Right around the time you Break Infinity, Prestige Automation and Promotion Automation become inefficient. The solution? Not-So-Manual Manual Promotions!

We want to quickly manually prestige a few times and then manually cycle through promotions so we can keep levelling them. This is straightforward enough to be put into a macro, and can make the entire gameplay of Breaking Infinity pre-eternity all the way to Breaking Eternity very straightforward.

Click to show Preparations, Macro and Template for Import >>>

Credits: angelamoureux @Oni Gaming Discord

This macro can be used at 1000+ Attacks to farm Unities and Zodiacs for Astrodust.

Set your Auto Eternate to 1e2467 and enable Auto Unity. Then you can sell your extra zodiacs and farm Unities.

Eternate
Wait Until EP = 0

After you complete #4 Insane Trial you will have all the Challenges completed by default on Unity reset or at any time. You can change your macro to this to be most effective. It will run automatically, allow you to get Zodiacs faster and won't glitch out. Some people report that it cannot be imported, so you can make it manually:

Wait Until DTP = 0
Restart Macro

Marcos for Singularity push and progression. Most of them can be adjusted to your liking and edited as you go.

Most macros are made by Discord community, big thumbs up to @endoplasmicreticulum1 for Macros and Singularity guides.

Plague stage macro. Cycles elements, uses required Arcans and Flushes Special Minerals.

    UseArcan(17)
    UseArcan(20)
    FlushSM([Red,Blue,Green,Pink,Yellow,Orange,Darkpurple,Lightblue,White,Black], 3)
GenerateElement(Fire)

    UseArcan(20)
    FlushSM([Red,Blue,Green,Pink,Yellow,Orange,Darkpurple,Lightblue,White,Black], 3)

    UseArcan(20)

Outdated Zodiac Farming / Unity loop
This macro is pretty old, and prior to marco rework, but if everything else fails you can try to use it to build temporary macros until you will push a bit more to get other macros to work.

There are 2 variations of full Unity loop macro that will help to get better zodiacs. One consists of 2 Macros and has to be manually activated, but viable earlier in the game. Can be used when you reach Medium trials, or when your Unity takes around couple of minutes. It can be a good basis for other macros and will help to understand how macros work in the game. It can be automated with purchasable go to macro block or buying additional macro steps slots.

Other one is late-game macro that can be used when all the Hard Trials are completed that allows to free up some macro spots and run it looped for full automation.

Early Game Unity Loop
Early Unity 2-macro set that will allow you to speed up your farming drastically and automate your path from Unity Reset to Dilation Tree, and to the Unity reset.

Click to show Macro and its Description >>>

Late Game Unity Loop
This macro can be used upon completing all Hard Trials and allows players to run it in the loop and unite using automation, making farming Zodiacs fully automated. Make sure to tune the times of wait to your build and progression. It can be run with various zodiac presets but works best with Dark Blue heavily Pisces focused builds.

Revolution Idle Souls Spending and Earning Guide
Revolution Idle Souls Guide.jpg
Revolution Idle Souls are a premium currency in the game that can be used to purchase various multiplier and cosmetic rewards. They can be earned ingame or bought using the real money, but in no way required to progress and finish the game. Revolution Idle can be completed fully F2P without any major progression walls. If you need help with the progression, Revolution Idle Ultimate Beginners Guide will help you out.

This guide is part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find the rest of the guides on the game.

Earning Souls
There are several ways how you can get Souls in Revolution Idle as f2p and you can also by them using the real-world money.

1. The easiest way to get extra Souls is to input Promotion Codes in the Shop Menu of the game. Currently there are active codes for 1500 Souls, but that might change over time, so check Revolution Idle Codes for the updated info.

2. Achievements will reward you with Souls once completed. Currently only early to mid game achievements provide Souls rewards. You can claim a total of ~15k Souls from achievements (I've calculated 14,675, but might be wrong).


Revolution Idle Daily Rewards
3. Daily Rewards provide 500 Souls on day 7 and 1000 Souls on Day 14, and some extra souls on every second day.

Daily Souls Rewards
Day	Souls	Total Souls
2	200	200
4	250	450
6	250	700
7	500	1200
9	250	1450
11	300	1750
13	500	2250
14	1000	3250
4. Buying Souls in the Shop. There are 3 available packs:

x2,000 Souls for $0.99
x11,000 Souls for $4.99
x50,000 Souls for $19.99
You can watch Souls Video Guide for more visual representation and tips on the Souls Earning and Spending mechanics.


Spending Souls
There are various multipliers that you can buy with souls, but all of them are giving you very minor buffs to the progression unless you invest large amount of souls into them. Each upgrade gets progressively more expensive as you buy it

You can spend souls on 3 multiplier categories, Time Flux or Skins.

Revolution Upgrades
Revolution upgrades add multipliers to your circles. They can be useful in the early game and later on when you reach Dilation.

Income
Increases you Score income per revolution. It basically multiplies your score gain, acting as a basic multiplier. With 7% gain per upgrade it's the best upgrade for early investment in this category. Foe the long-term progression it's pretty useless until you hit Dilation where things slow down again.

Multipliers
A bonus multiplier (x) is added to others, this is an additive multiplier to the score. With 3% bonus per upgrade it's not as efficient as Income, unless the Income upgrade Souls price is double the Multiplier price.

Laps/s
Speed up revolution laps per second. In terms of gain it's the same as Multiplier, but it also improves Quality of Life, since laps go faster and you don't need to wait so long. Overall I'd take Laps/s over Multipliers for the same costs.

Ascension Power
Increase your Ascension Power, making Circles ascension more effective. Adds 3% per step, so for the optimal progression upgrade evenly with other Revolution Multipliers.

Prestige Upgrades
Prestige Upgrades add bonuses to Prestige multipliers and exponent. Those upgrades will be useful most of the time prior to rapid Infinities and will kick in in Dilation phase again.

P.Exponent
Increase your Prestige Exponent by 0.1%, while second upgrade gives only 0.05% increase to a total 0.15%. With the diminishing returns here, just evenly invest Souls into each of those.

P.Multiplier
Increase your Prestige Multiplier by 1%, while second upgrade gives only 0.5% increase to a total 1.5%. With the diminishing returns here, just evenly invest Souls into each of those.

Infinity Upgrades
Infinity Upgrades add bonuses to Infinity aspects of the game. They have diminishing returns as well, so further upgrades get more expensive and add less of a bonus too.

Infinite Gain
Increases number of infinities gained on infinity. It's not Infinity points that can be spent, it's count of infinities, that it being used as a base in some multipliers. It's actually a pretty good upgrades, since it's really hard to farm Infinities Count early in the infinities. First upgrade gives 3%, second is 4.5%, so each next is getting 50% increase of the base.

IP Gain
Increases your IP gain. Now those are the points you can spend for infinity upgrades or generators. Level 1 is 3%, lvl 2 is 4.5%, overall useful throughout most of the game, so it's a pretty good upgrade, but can get expensive pretty fast.

Gen Mult
Gives a bonus Multiplier to Generators. Generators contribute a lot to overall Score gain, and 3% will help a bit too.

Stardust Gain
Increases your Stardust Gain. I don't find this upgrade to be useful, since Stardust stage of the game goes by pretty fast and overall bonuses are not that crazy in the later game. Extra 5% won't change the situation that much, those souls better spent elsewhere.

Eternity Upgrades
Eternity Upgrades add bonuses to Eternity game phase. They have diminishing returns as well, so further upgrades get more expensive and add less of a bonus too.

EP Gain
Increases Eternity Point gains, that can be spent in the Laboratory and to buy Animal Points. Overall, it's not so great, since once past that stage, those bonuses won't matter much, at least for now. First upgrade is 3%, second is 4.5%.

DP Gain
Increases Dilation Point gains, which is very useful for the Dilation part of the game. Since Dilation takes most of the endgame it would be best investment if you want to play long-term and reach higher Dilation scores. First upgrade is 1.5%.

Astrology Upgrades
Astrology Upgrades add bonuses to Zodiacs game phase. They have diminishing returns as well, so further upgrades get more expensive and add less of a bonus too.

Zodiac Luck
Increase your chances of obtaining higher rarity Zodiacs. Pretty good, but needs higher % to be useful, but the price is very steep. First upgrade is 1%, second is 2%.

Zodiac Quality
Improve the quality of your Zodiacs. Same as the last one, but effects aren't that crazy. First upgrade is 2%.

Zodiac Sell Price
Sell your Zodiacs for a higher price and earn more Astrodust. Once again, could be better, but might be a good deal. First upgrade is 5%.

Minerals Upgrades
Minerals Upgrades help you to get through Minerals phase of the game. Since that part of the game takes loads of time, most of those upgrades might be good to speed it up. Even a little bit helps.

VP Gain
Increase the amount of Value Point gain. Bonus is significant, so this upgrade is actually worth its price. First upgrade is 25%, second 50%.

PP Gain
Increase the amount of Prestige Points you earn. Now this bonus isn't that powerful, but not so expensive, so might be worth a point. First upgrade is 5%, Second is 10%.

Fall Speed
Accelerate the fallinf speed of minerals by faster decreasing the cost. It might feel powerful early on, but bonus is tiny and later on it will be obsolete. First upgrade is 1%, Second 2%.

Magnet Chance
Raise the chance to attract with the magnet effect. Later on it won't be as relevant and it's pretty expensive, but early on might feel like a good investment. First upgrade is 2%, second 4%.

Merge Experience
Earn more experience when merging minerals. Average upgrade, it doesn't cost that much, but effect isn't great either. First upgrade is 10%, second 20%.

Minerals Local Game Speed
Accelerate the mineral local game speed. It's really good, since game local game speed is very useful, but price is steep as well. First upgrade is 2%, second 4%.

Sun Rune Speed
Increase the Sun Rune generation Speed. Pretty neat bonus, especially since getting runes is hard and slow and you need a lot of them, but price is high as well. First upgrade is 2%, second 4%.

Moon Rune Speed
Increase the Moon Rune generation Speed. Same as Sun rune. Pretty useful, but expensive. First upgrade is 2%, second 4%.

Elements Upgrades
Elements Upgrades are boosting Elements area.

Elements Multiplier
A global Multiplier to all Elements. Not that expensive, and gets a decent bonus, might be worth it. First upgrade is 2%.

Revolution Skins
Revolution Skins provide no ingame bonuses, but make your visual experience more varied.

Revolution Idle Skins Price
Skin	Souls Cost

Orbital	1,000

Time Flux
You can also buy Time Flux using the Souls that can speed up the game time or allow you to do time warp, skipping hours of gameplay very fast.

2 hours of Time Flux - 3,000 Souls
8 hours of Time Flux - 10,000 Souls
24 hours of Time Flux - 25,000 Souls

Leave the game - 4,000 Souls
Redirection - 4,500 Souls

Daily Reward - 3,500 Souls
Switch Skin - 3,000 Souls
Alert Message - 5,500 Souls

Dilation Tree Loadout Slot - 3,000 Souls
Zodiac Slot - 500 Souls
Zodiac Loadout Slot - 4,000 Souls
Soul Spending Priorities
Since Souls provide minor bonuses to the progression there is no real need to minmax the Souls investment and the game can be completed without any of them spent for the upgrades. But if you want to give it a little bit of push you have several options:

Go for what you currently need. Upgrade those based on the tips given in the guide for each specific upgrade. Enjoy the smooth journey and once you reach endgame invest your daily Souls in the DP Gain.
Try to be effective and think about long-term progression. Skip Revolution Upgrades, get P.Exponent, P.Multiplier, Infinite Gain, and focus on DP Gain for faster Dilation progress.
Save souls for Quality of Life stuff like more Zodiac Slots, Loadout slots etc. That won't make your gains faster, but will make the grind more pleasant.
Although it's still debatable if going efficient way will be faster, since your early game will be a bit slower and bonuses are not so powerful. Especially with diminishing returns and increased price for each step. Later on you can reevaluate your options and invest somewhere else, that helps you in the current game situation to speed up the progression.

Moreover each next upgrade cost more, so if you would like to buy some multipliers spread your extra souls among multiple upgrades.

Revolution Idle Achievements Guide
Revolution Idle Achievements Guide.jpg
In this guide we will cover most difficult Revolution Idle Achievements, that might be difficult to obtain naturally. Most of the achievements are easy to earn and come naturally as you progress the game, but there are some that are important for the progression because they provide important bonuses, so let's focus on them. If you are looking for Secret Achievements you can check out Secret Achievements Guide.

This guide is part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find the rest of the guides on the game.

29. Backwards
Make White mult > Pink mult > Purple mult, etc...

Reward: (upon completing Achievement 140) Dilation Tree TOP-4, MID-4 and BOT-4 upgrades are 30% stronger

Solution: What this means is those multipliers, colored numbers at the top of the game screen need to be smallest on the left (red) and largest on the right (white). Each number must be larger than the one to its left, and smaller than the one to its right.

he set up is actually quite simple: recommendations to make it not take too long, and the distribution of color levels to hit the ascending mult order.

Quality of Life: Get Promotion #2 (laps speed mult) up, I like at least x3 Get some prestige mult, I like at least 3e8 (300,000,000); you can definitely do more or even get some mult from Promotion #1, but I wanted to take it nice and slow

Then, make sure any and all automations are OFF, except you CAN (and SHOULD) leave the autobuyer for white ON.

Prestige to reset your color levels, and we're off!

Pay attention to your buy number so you don't blow past the targets in the table below. Red starts at 5 and we don't put any levels into that. Orange is available off the bat, and each subsequent color requires at least 5 levels in its preceding color to unlock. Start leveling to hit these targets!

Caption text
Color	Level
Red	5
Orange	12
Yellow	19
Green	41
Turquoise	52
Cyan	63
Blue	74
Purple	85
Pink	100
White	Max it!
I like to set my buy number to 5 for quick and easy unlocks and to get everything close, and then switch it back to 1 to click in those final levels. Do NOT ascend Pink, but you WILL need to ascend White and then keep buying more levels (again, the autobuyer on White really helps here).

Voila, now we just have to wait! I turned on Time Flux for 2x time and that also sped things up, but you don't have to. You can also put a few extra levels in cyan/blue/purple to help those get sorted faster, but it's not necessary.

Before too long, your achievement should unlock! Congratulations, and enjoy your newfound fading sense of accomplishment.

Credit: nu at Steam Community Guides

200. Inefficiently Efficient
Generate less than 0.05 lab pts/s, but more than 0.01 lab pts/s

Reward: It's useless, but you can't get acheivement 201 without this one. (Better Formula for achievement rewards that adds huge bonuses to Mult gain, Generators gain, DP Gain,Boost Zodiacs quality and Attacks damage.

Video Guide:


Solution:

Auto SN, Auto Lab stuff off
Have atleast one Lab Level Zodiac equipped
Unity Reset
Eternate about twice to have enough EP (est. e80+ EP)
Open Lab
Buy 1 base level
Keep buying single or 5 power levels at a time until achievement pops up.
230. Dance of Fire and Flames
Try with a full-fire build (all 12 planet slots are with only fire zodiacs) and reach e5.00e11 score.

Reward: All relics are 1% more effective, divide all relics costs by 5 and total quality for fire zodiacs is powered by ^1.1 now.

Video Guide:


Solution: 6k quality, full Aries Zodiacs. To upgrade zodiac rarities between each attempt, go full pisces. Attempt any achievement as soon as you can since it'll upgrade relics effectiveness, thus upgrading quality of your zodiacs and also boost zodiacs quality directly. If you can't succeed at a recommended quality, just upgrade it a bit. There numbers can be a bit off if you don't sacrifice enough or whatnot

231. Growing Mountains
Try with a full-earth build (all 12 planet slots are with only earth zodiacs) and reach 1.10e111 infinities, e350,000 IP and e6,300,000 Gen.Power Mult Gain

Reward: All relics are 1% more effective, divide all relics costs by 5 and total quality for earth zodiacs is powered by ^1.1 now.

Solution: 60-80k quality, full Taurus Zodiacs. To upgrade zodiac rarities between each attempt, go full pisces. Attempt any achievement as soon as you can since it'll upgrade relics effectiveness, thus upgrading quality of your zodiacs and also boost zodiacs quality directly. If you can't succeed at a recommended quality, just upgrade it a bit. There numbers can be a bit off if you don't sacrifice enough or whatnot

232. I Feel it in the Wind my Dear
Try with a full-wind build (all 12 planet slots are with only wind zodiacs) and reach 1.00e36 Eternities and 1.00e45,00 DP

Reward: All relics are 1% more effective, divide all relics costs by 5 and total quality for wind zodiacs is powered by ^1.1 now.

Solution: 250k quality, 4 Libras 8 Geminis. To upgrade zodiac rarities between each attempt, go full pisces. Attempt any achievement as soon as you can since it'll upgrade relics effectiveness, thus upgrading quality of your zodiacs and also boost zodiacs quality directly. If you can't succeed at a recommended quality, just upgrade it a bit. There numbers can be a bit off if you don't sacrifice enough or whatnot

233. Swimming With Sharks
Try with a full-water build (all 12 planet slots are with only water zodiacs) and get an immortal +5 zodiac with 1,000,000+ quality.

Reward: All relics are 1% more effective, divide all relics costs by 5 and total quality for water zodiacs is powered by ^1.1 now.

Solution: 400-450k quality, 11 Pisces, 1 Scorpio on Pluto, 1650+ Luck needed, Water zodiacs won't have over 1m quality because you don't have this achievement, so pick any other element, since they already have ^1.1 bonus to their quality. To upgrade zodiac rarities between each attempt, go full pisces. Attempt any achievement as soon as you can since it'll upgrade relics effectiveness, thus upgrading quality of your zodiacs and also boost zodiacs quality directly. If you can't succeed at a recommended quality, just upgrade it a bit. There numbers can be a bit off if you don't sacrifice enough or whatnot

320. Base Evolution
Reach Base Level 2+ for any of Special Minerals in SMP

Reward: x1.02 to Runes Generation

Solution: Since this Achievement is right before Elements, it feels like it should be unlocked before that, but that isn't the case. This achievement was last one prior to Elements incroduction and takes A WHILE to finish. So don't sweat it, it will come naturally, but WAY later than you expect. I've managed to get it around achievement #342 and 4k attack Levels.

342. Here comes new rarities
Merge 3 zodiacs with Immortal +1000 rarity

Reward: Now you can naturally get higher rarity zodiacs

Solution: It will come naturally, but you need full Revolution Idle Pisces.pngPisces Build and high rarity. New zodiacs are op and you can easily replace everything with Ethereal rarity zodiacs.

Revolution Idle Secret Achievements Guide
Revolution Idle Secret Achievements Guide.jpg
There are 33 Secret Achievements in the Revolution Idle, but their requirements are hidden and it might take a while to guess how to unlock them. This Guide will help you to unlock all the secret achievements in the game and get Time Flux gain multipliers. If you are looking for Normal Achievements you can check out Hard Achievements Guide.

This guide is part of Revolution Idle Guides series, where you can find the rest of the guides on the game.

Each unlocked Secret Achievement adds +0.1% bonus to Time Flux gain, making your offline time more effective.

Video Guides
Secret Achievements 1-31:


Secret Achievements 32-51:

1. Meet the Creators
Hint 1: Meet the Creators
Hint 2: Click on something to do with the creators
Solution: Click on the creator icons in the credits

2. Successful Promoter
Hint 1: Successful Promoter
Hint 2: Promotion automation is key
Solution: Add 50 auto promotion settings

3. Primary Progression
Hint 1: Primary Progression
Hint 2: What are the primary colors of the digital age
Hint 3: Some automations involve colors
Solution: Enable only red, green, and blue in the Color, Ascend, and generator autobuyers

4. Lord of War
Hint 1: It refers to buying things
Hint 2: Have you tried buying things in bulk?
Hint 3: Buy everything in bulk. It's faster
Solution: Set all bulk buyers to max (Color, Generator, Dilation upgrades, etc.)

5. Straight to Infinity
Hint 1: Infinity tree autobuy is your enemy
Hint 2: The stars are high above in the sky
Solution: Buy only X;1 (Top row) upgrades all the way to stars at 21;1

6. Loyalty Rewarded
Hint 1: This one takes max 14 days
Hint 2: Something you do every day, but only once a day
Solution: Claim Day14 of daily rewards

7. Davinci Code
Hint 1: Secret bonus from secret 20
Hint 2: United we redeem
Solution: redeem code unity in the shop

8. Mad Scientist
Hint 1: There is one more place where one can gather revolution score
Hint 2: You probably read it when starting the game
Hint 3: Patience is required
Solution: Reach 1bil score in Help>General. Takes about 45 minutes.

9. Out of Time
Hint 1: Stop messing with time
Hint 2: Why not late time flux collect for a bit
Hint 3: Be patient it takes a few days
Solution: Don't use time flux or offline flux for 1 week

10. The Seven Relics
Hint 1: the seven relics
Hint 2: Ever notice something odd with the corner of your eye just to not find it after?
Hint 3: I swear I saw something when I scrolled
Solution: Scroll pass the edge on credits (bottom), help>achievements (top), eternity challenges (top), animals (horizontal scroll - right), automations (bottom), Generators (bottom) and shop (bottom). Ps. Mouse can be used to drag around instead of scrolling

11. You thought this was cookie- a clicker game?
Hint 1: Choco chip cookie?
Hint 2: Ever played cookie clicker?
Hint 3: Imagine this is cookie clicker
Solution: Click on the center of the Revolution screen 100 times

12. Enigma
Hint 1: Secrets 2, 3, 5, 8 will help
Hint 2: Start with the word revolution
Hint 3: Some encoding and math is needed
Hint 4: Speed buttons
Solution: In the time flux page use the game speed buttons to type out 22131221332221222121

221-312-213-322-212-221-21

13. Revolutionary Baby
Hint 1: Just play the game
Hint 2: Closing the game doesn't help
Hint 3: 1 day and catch those 60 minutes
Solution: 25 hours of unscaled playtime (consult stats page)

14. Hypnotized
Hint 1: You will get this eventually
Hint 2: Most games would be over by this time
Hint 3: Yes it's playtime again
Solution: 250 hours of unscaled playtime (consult stats page)

15. The Age of Wisdom
Hint 1: You will earn twice a similar one before earning this one
Hint 2: One reaches unity before this.
Solution: 1000 hours of unscaled playtime (consult stats page)

16. Weekly Warrior
Hint 1: It's like 6, but shorter and you have to be more consistent
Hint 2: Spanish or vanish -Duo
Hint 3: C-C-C-Combo
Solution: Log in for 7 days straight

17. Two-Week Streak
Hint 1: Same as 6 but more consistent
Hint 2: My boss wants me to work weekends
Hint 3: It takes a fortnight
Solution 3: Log in for 14 days straight

18. Unwavering Loyalty
Hint 1: You have to be really consistent with this one
Hint 2: A moon cycle has to pass without skipping a beat
Solution: Log in for 30 days straight

19. Circle of Infinity
Hint 1: Circle of Infinity
Hint 2: The unlucky numbered secret hints to this
Hint 3: 2π makes a circle
Hint 4: Max 3 decimals
Solution: Enter 6.28 (aka 2π) for auto infinity time

20. Chemically Stupid
Hint 1: It's lab related
Hint 2: Custom values are sometimes not that useful
Hint 3: Why not have 2 of the same
Solution: Set the custom allocation of RP value for a lab upgrade to one of the existing ones (1, 5, 10) and use it
21. Carnivore Instinct
Hint 1: Carnivore Instinct
Hint 2: I ate them all, but I'm still hungry
Hints 3: Eating air
Solution: Reset animals with no animals bought

Source - Secret achievement hints and solutions thread in official Oni Gaming Discord by @pa3ckp7

22. Eat the Cookie
Reward: +150 Souls

Solution: Spam Click the cookie in Secret Achievement #011

23. Intermittently solid
Reward: +250 Souls

Solution: Alternate between a solid and non-solid circle

So e.g. red solid, orange not solid, yellow solid etc.

Remove automations and infinite, then buy 5 of each circle. Then buy max of red, yellow, turquoise, blue and pink and it should pop

or

Turn off Automate Buyables (Colors) and Automate Ascensions. Buy 5 of each circle to unlock all circles. Max out Slowdown. Buy max (up to ascension) of red, yellow, turquoise, blue and pink. You might also need to remove Zodiacs.

24. Worldwide Revolution
Reward: +198 Souls

Solution: Disable all automations.
Enter Eternity Challenge 10-5.
Enter Dilation.
Enter Infinity Challenge 9
Press the infinite button.

I did this with Supernova 155 and an empty DTP tree with three unities quite easily but you may need to do it earlier/later depending on personal progress.

Late game solution:

Make sure all automations are off, enable Autobuy for Animal Points, Animals, Laboratory Upgrades
Equip some Pisces zodiacs for faster progression
Start a new unity, do couple of Eternities to unlock Etenity Challenges.
Do Eternity Challenges until you will get stuck (Around EC 10-4).
Go to Dilation tab (if still locked do couple of supernovas until you will get enough AP to finish all Animals
While in EC 10-4 enter Dilation, Get some score, Exit Dilation.
Buy Dilation Upgrade #1 in increments of 1 until you will have enough to finish the EC 10-4 (Keep an eye on the score in the top right corner, you need e308 to finish EC 10-4.
Enter EC 10-5.
Enter Dilation.
Buy Dilation Upgrade #1 in increments of 1 or 5 until you will get Current Max Score to 1e350-1e400 or so. Make sure to not overbuy it and don't go over 1e900, since 1e1000 will finish EC 10-5 and you will need to start over.
Go to Revolution screen, do couple of Infinities for 1 IP.
Buy Infinity Upgrades through the bottom path until you reach 7;1 Challenges.
Select Challenges tab and enter Challenge 9.
Go back to Revolution Screen and Infinite to get achievement.
25. That wasn't an exploit...
Reward: +1000ms TF

Solution: Spam click the checkbox by offline flux in the Time Flux section

26. Busted Cheater
Reward: -100 Souls

Solution: Import a Dilation Tree Path Loadout with higher levels than five.

Copy this and import to a Dilation Tree path: C5;T5,5,5,5;M5,5,5,5;B5,5,5,10

27. Not yet Elon
Reward: +15:00 TF

Solution: In the Unity section, click on the planet Mercury, and then on the rocket as it flies past.

28. Astrology Expert
Reward: +200 Souls

Solution: Assign the planets in zodiacal order (Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius, Pisces).

29. Business First
Reward: +30:00 TF

Solution: Sell an immortal zodiac.

30. Access locked
Reward: +15:00 TF

Solution: Lock all trials

31. Scatterbrained
Reward: +150 Souls

Solution: Leave a zodiac in one of the planet shop or redistribution slots, and close the tab.

32. Flash the headlights
Decode the Morse code for BANANA Reward: Skin Banana

hint 1: morse code
hint 2: stats button on revolution page
hint 3: go crazy!

Revolution Idle Banana Morse Code
Solution: You need to encode BANANA in morse code by opening and closing the stats popup on the revolutions page (button looks like a pie chart with a brown background). The image below shows the morse code for BANANA (thanks @drotz in new update waiting room for making the image).

If you don't know what morse code is, its encoding words as a series of dots and dashes, or beeps of various lengths. in this case, the image below shows it as dots and dashes, and the game will play it to you as beeps, a short beep for a dot and a long beep for a dash.

To enter morse, you click the stats popup button (brown button that looks like a pie chart). YOU MUST START WITH THE POPUP CLOSED. the length of time the popup is open for determines whether you enter a dash or a dot, so to do a dash you click to open the popup, wait at least a second, then click to close it. to enter a dot, simply double click to open and close it quickly.

You need to go relatively fast when entering the dots and dashes for a single letter, and when you finish a letter, leave the popup closed until the game beeps the combination you just entered back at you. after it does that, you can wait as long as you like before starting the next letter.

Once you enter the sixth letter, if you typed BANANA correctly it will unlock SA32. If you didn't spell it correctly, it will make an incorrect noise. If at any point you enter more than 4 dots or dashes for a single letter, the letter will be invalid and ignored by the game, resulting in no beeps being played back at you after you leave the popup closed.

33. Boosted Banana
Reach a score of 100,000 in the Banana mini-game without auto-clicker Reward:

hint 1: not possible without SA32
hint 2: what if you had... more bananas?
Solution: click the banana 100k times
Use autoclicker. Even at the maximum autoclicker speed it will still take 50 minutes of continiuous clicking.

Credits to Steam Community Secret Achievements Guide by Drazz and Official Revolution Idle Discord Community

34. White Mining Rabbit
hint1: Oh dear! Oh dear! I shall be too late!
Solution: Drop Chronorium (M89) on top of the Time Flux icon
You can turn off autosummon and manually type mineral lvl 89 to spawn Chronorium.

35. Stone Breaker
hint 1: Mining is your destiny.
Solution: Destroy manually one of each type of common mineral
Yes, you gotta manually delete all 100 minerals. Turn off automation options: Autodelete Minerals, Autoset mineral spawn level, automerge mienrals for easier grind. Go through mineral levels 1-100. You can turn off Autodelete minerals if you need to clear the mineral grid of trash.

36. Infinity Stones
hint 1: Follow the path of the six stones... from darkness to light
Solution: Delete with the trash bin 6 different special mineral in this order : Dark Purple, Blue, Red, Orange, Green, Yellow

37.
hint 1: Finish the job to see the light
Solution: Sacrifice relic 9 "Midas Hand" after you completed SA36

If you can't get it after you get auto sacrifice you can use an auto clicker with 1000 cps, clicking on where "sacrifice" pops up

38. Solar Eclipse
hint 1: Two celestial bodies, two paths
Solution: On the Runes screen, rotate the Sun 4 times clockwise, and the Moon 7 times counter-clockwise. You have 45 degrees for your margin of error for this.

39. Newton's Theory
hint 1: Apples fall from the trees
Solution: click n9, n8 then n1 in the Refine tree. A red apple appears, click it

40. Welcome to the Team
hint 1: Help developers squash the bug
Solution: Trigger the Negative AP bug, then click on your AP amount

turn off automations for autobuy AP, and supernova. (keep autobuy animals on)
unity reset using button in trials
then you should just have -1 AP, :P (go to eternity tab)
click on the "-1 AP"
Alternative : If you can't hit -1 AP and you have 60 AP base put the 4 animal : Cat, Giraffe, Fox, Panther to autobuy and remove the rest

41. The Big Bang Theory
hint 1:Touch some grass
Solution: Exit the game 50 times

42. Marathoner
hint 1:Take a break? Never heard of it
Solution: Have the game open for 3 hours without closing

43. Iron Will
hint 1: Sleep is for the weak
Solution: Have the game open for 6 hours without closing

44. Nerd Commander
hint 1: After this one, you deserve a break
Solution: Have the game open for 9 hours without closing

45. Apollo 11
hint 1: Apollo 11
Solution: Enter the code 1-9-6-9 in Elements Upgrades

46. Silence
hint 1: Silence
Solution: Annoy the creepy Tarot Upgrades guy until he stops talking

47. Not Very Smart
hint 1: Not very smart
Solution: Place the Relic Smart Man automation first, while keeping it disabled.
Go to automations, Relic Autobuy, move Smart Man to the first slot and double-click it to disable it.

48. Agent Smith
hint 1: Agent Smith
Solution: Enter Agent Smith or Smith as the username in the leaderboard

49. Deal With the Devil
hint 1: Deal with a Devil
Solution: Let the Devil take your soul
1% chance to trigger when using Devil Tarot card

50. Positive Debuff
hint 1: Positive Debuff
Solution: Manage to increase your draws with the help of the Devil
First use Wheel of Fortune Tarot to get negative draws. Then use Devil Tarot to trigger Attachment debuff. It will lower the negative draws and you will get the achievement.

51. Catch Me!
hint 1: Catch me
Solution: Catch the Chadium that spawn with the devil by clicking on it

Credits: Lucas._ze @ Oni Gaming Discord
